<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Stephen Wolfram pens a heartfelt tribute to his wife Elise Cawley, celebrating their 36 years together and expressing gratitude for her support. The note offers a personal glimpse into how partnership shaped his life and work.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>斯蒂芬·沃尔夫拉姆在纪念帖中缅怀妻子 Elise Cawley，感恩与她共同度过的36个美好岁月。文章揭示了个人伴侣关系在其人生与工作中的重要影响。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49161518' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49161518' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(3) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.seangoedecke.com/llms-reward-expertise/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>LLMs reward expertise</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>大模型奖励专业知识</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49161518' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1364</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49161518' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 563</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49161518'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Recent work highlights that LLMs tend to reward content produced by or aligned with domain expertise. This bias emphasizes high-quality data and expert sources in training and evaluation, shaping model behavior and prompts. The takeaway: invest in expert-curated data and robust evaluation to avoid gatekeeping and misalignment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>最新研究表明，大型语言模型倾向于奖励来自专业领域的高质量内容，这种偏好来自数据质量和专家来源在训练及评估中的重要性，进而影响模型行为与提示设计。核心意义在于应重视专家数据的整合与严格评估，避免因过度依赖权威而带来评判偏差或门槛化。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.seangoedecke.com/og-image.jpg' data-full='https://www.seangoedecke.com/og-image.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49195231' data-tags='Science,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49195231' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(4) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.mayerowitz.io/blog/mario-meets-pareto' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mario Meets Pareto</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>马里奥遇见帕累托原则</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49195231' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1162</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49195231' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 165</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49195231'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A playful piece that uses Mario to illustrate Pareto efficiency in optimization, offering intuition for how a small subset of actions can drive most outcomes in complex environments. The concept translates to AI planning and resource allocation, guiding developers toward high-impact, scalable decisions. It’s a reminder that clever prioritization often beats brute-force attempts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过以马里奥为例来阐释帕累托效率在优化中的作用，帮助读者理解在复杂环境中少数行动如何推动大部分结果。这一思路可迁移到 AI 规划与资源分配，指引开发者关注高影响力、可扩展的决策。提醒人们，精明的优先排序往往胜过盲目扩展。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.mayerowitz.io/SEO/mario-meets-pareto.png' data-full='https://www.mayerowitz.io/SEO/mario-meets-pareto.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49150470' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1086</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49150470' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 591</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49150470'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Qwen3.8-Max arrives as a dedicated coding-focused model, emphasizing reliable code generation, smarter debugging, and smoother collaboration in team settings. It aims to raise the bar for AI-assisted software development and could influence how developers integrate AI copilots into their workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Qwen3.8-Max 是一款面向编码的模型，强调可靠的代码生成、更智能的调试，以及在协作场景中的更顺畅协同。它试图为 AI 辅助的软件开发设定新标准，可能影响开发者将 AI 助手整合到工作流中的方式。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(6) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.noemamag.com/why-is-everyone-in-tech-so-sad/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What happens if an entire class of workers loses faith in their careers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>当整类工人对职业失去信心时会怎样</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49209539' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1023</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49209539' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1241</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49209539'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>If an entire class of workers loses faith in their careers, broad churn and talent shifts could disrupt project roadmaps and innovation cycles. The piece argues burnout, misaligned incentives, and perceived stagnation in tech drive people to seek meaning elsewhere, urging leaders to rethink career paths and well-being.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>如果整类工人对职业前景失去信心，可能引发大量离职与技能转型，影响项目进度与创新节奏。文章指出倦怠、激励错位和对职业成长的感知停滞促使人们寻求更有意义的工作，呼吁领导层重新设计职业发展路径与员工福祉。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(8) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theregister.com/systems/2026/08/06/amd-acquires-ai-chip-startup-taalas-to-boost-inference-performance-by-etching-models-into-silicon/5284344' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AMD acquires Taalas to boost inference performance by etching models in silicon</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AMD收购Taalas以通过在硅上刻画模型提升推理性能</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49201970' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 912</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49201970' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 687</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49201970'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AMD expands its AI hardware play by acquiring Taalas, a startup focused on embedding AI models directly into silicon. The deal aims to boost inference throughput and energy efficiency, signaling a trend toward hardware-software co-design for large-scale AI.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AMD通过收购Taalas，意在将AI模型直接嵌入芯片中以提升推理吞吐量与能效，显示出硬件与软件深度协同设计的趋势。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(11) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.senko.net/code-was-never-the-hard-part-is-an-insult-to-all-programmers' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>“Code was never the hard part” is an insult to all programmers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>&quot;Code was never the hard part&quot; 对所有程序员都是侮辱</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49222189' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 827</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49222189' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 506</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49222189'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>&quot;Code was never the hard part&quot; is often cited as a simplification of software work, but a blog argues it insults programmers by overlooking design, coordination, and maintenance. The piece reframes what makes projects hard and where real value lies for developers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>&quot;Code was never the hard part&quot; 常被用来简化软件工作，但一篇博文认为这句话是在贬损程序员，因为它忽略了设计、协作与维护等真正的挑战。文章重新聚焦于影响项目难度与价值的关键因素。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.writeas.net/img/w-sq-light.png' data-full='https://cdn.writeas.net/img/w-sq-light.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49204352' data-tags='Legal,Health,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49204352' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(12) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/technology/2026/aug/06/new-mexico-court-meta' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>New Mexico court orders Meta to pay $567m over harms to children’s mental health</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>新墨西哥法院判决 Meta 因对未成年人心理健康造成伤害，需要赔偿 5.67 亿美元</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49204352' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 782</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49204352' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 420</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49204352'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A New Mexico court has ordered Meta to pay $567 million after finding that its platforms harmed children’s mental health. The ruling adds to growing regulatory pressure on social media companies and could push Meta to adjust safety measures and user protections, even as enforcement costs rise.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新墨西哥法院判决 Meta 因对未成年人的心理健康造成伤害，需要赔偿 5.67 亿美元。此案加剧了对社交媒体平台在青少年福祉方面的监管压力，并可能促使 Meta 在安全措施和用户保护方面做出更多调整，监管成本也在上升。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/6e9c72f1d4d1541ee16137214046b3255b6a1981/410_0_6827_5464/master/6827.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=bde32639cfe3328257ea8f512bc97b88' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/6e9c72f1d4d1541ee16137214046b3255b6a1981/410_0_6827_5464/master/6827.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=bde32639cfe3328257ea8f512bc97b88' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49167113' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49167113' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(14) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nelson.cloud/ai-generated-images-discourage-me-from-reading-your-blog/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI-Generated Images Discourage Me from Reading Your Blog</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 生成的图片让我不再愿意读你的博客</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49167113' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 769</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49167113' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 454</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49167113'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An author argues that AI-generated images reduce engagement with blog posts, making content feel artificial and distracting. The visuals can undermine the reader experience, causing skimming or distraction when images lack nuance. The piece recommends using authentic visuals—original photos or carefully curated art—to preserve reader trust and focus.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者认为，AI 生成的图片会削弱博客的阅读体验，让内容显得不真实且分散注意力。图片若缺乏层次感可能让读者快速跳读或走神。文中建议使用真实照片或精挑细选的视觉元素来维持读者信任与专注度。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://nelson.cloud/ai-generated-images-discourage-me-from-reading-your-blog/og.png' data-full='https://nelson.cloud/ai-generated-images-discourage-me-from-reading-your-blog/og.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49156111' data-tags='Open Source,Programming,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49156111' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(15) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.exe.dev/devtools-must-be-open-source' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Devtools must be open source</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>开发者工具必须开源</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49156111' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 716</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49156111' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 231</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49156111'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Open-sourcing devtools improves transparency and security through community review and faster bug-fixing. The piece argues that closed tools invite vendor lock-in and opaque risk, and outlines practical paths to open alternatives.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>开源开发者工具能提升透明度与安全性，因为社区评审和快速修复更易实现。文中指出封闭工具会带来供应商锁定和隐性风险，并提出让工具走向开源的可行路径。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.exe.dev/static/og-card.png' data-full='https://blog.exe.dev/static/og-card.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(16) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://research.jfrog.com/post/sqlite-critical-cves-or-llm-slops/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>SQLite Critical CVEs or LLM Slop?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>SQLite 关键 CVEs 或 LLM 漏洞？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49154332' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 715</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49154332' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 366</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49154332'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Raises questions about SQLite&#x27;s critical CVEs, and potential LLM-related misuses, prompting readers to consider threat modeling when embedding databases with AI workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨 SQLite 的关键 CVE 是否构成真实风险，以及与大型语言模型相关的潜在滑移/误用风险，促请在将数据库与 AI 工作流结合时进行威胁建模。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49167448' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(17) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://birchtree.me/blog/xbox-goes-down-you-cant-play-games-you-own-on-disc/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Xbox goes down. You can&#x27;t play games you own on disc</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Xbox 系统宕机致无法离线玩光盘游戏</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49167448' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 679</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49167448' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 725</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49167448'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An outage during which Xbox users cannot play disc-owned games highlights how modern consoles lean on online services even for physical media. The incident underscores resilience risks, DRM entanglements, and the trade-offs of cloud-enabled ownership.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Xbox 的服务中断让玩家即便拥有光盘游戏也无法离线游玩，凸显现代主机生态对在线服务的依赖。事件暴露了系统韧性、数字版权管理与“拥有即用”的取舍之间的矛盾。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(18) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.crimepaysbutbotanydoesnt.com/reading-list' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Crime Pays but Botany Doesn&#x27;t</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>犯罪有回报，植物学却不</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49192566' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 672</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49192566' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 210</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49192566'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An eclectic reading list contrasts crime-focused topics with botany, using the tension between risk and growth to illustrate how different domains reward careful inquiry and robust systems thinking. It offers cross-disciplinary insights into detection, growth, and learning strategies valuable to technologists.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一份以犯罪题材与植物学之间的对比为主的阅读清单，揭示不同领域对好奇心、方法论和系统思维的奖励机制。通过跨学科的对照，帮助科技工作者理解数据模式、风险评估与学习策略。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49182996' data-tags='Open Source,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49182996' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(20) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.cloudflare.com/cloudflare-os/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cloudflare OS: an open platform for agents, apps, and work</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Cloudflare OS：面向代理、应用与工作的新开放平台</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49182996' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 658</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49182996' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 324</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49182996'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare unveils Cloudflare OS, an open platform designed to host agents, apps, and work workflows at the edge. It aims to broaden extensibility across Cloudflare&#x27;s edge network by letting developers deploy自治代理和应用并与现有服务集成，提升边缘计算的可扩展性与安全性。</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare 推出 Cloudflare OS，这是一个面向在边缘运行的代理、应用和工作流的开放平台。通过允许开发者部署自治代理和应用并与现有服务集成，提升边缘计算的可扩展性与安全性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.cloudflare.com/_emdash/api/media/file/01KZ7PN06AYFEQJN4FBVAE8WHH.png' data-full='https://blog.cloudflare.com/_emdash/api/media/file/01KZ7PN06AYFEQJN4FBVAE8WHH.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49157930' data-tags='Science,Programming,AI,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49157930' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(21) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openai.com/index/ten-advances-in-mathematics/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ten advances in mathematics and theoretical computer science</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>数学与理论计算机科学的十项进展</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49157930' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 619</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49157930' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 920</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49157930'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A survey of ten notable advances at the mathematics-theory CS frontier, spanning new results in algorithmic information theory, cryptography, and mathematical methods. The core takeaway is that foundational research continues to drive practical gains in security, verification, and AI theory.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文盘点数学与理论计算机科学前沿的十项进展，涵盖算法信息理论、密码学和新数学方法等领域。核心在于基础研究持续推动安全、验证与人工智能理论等实际应用的突破。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Oracle bans AI-generated code from OpenJDK. It could force contributors to audit code provenance more carefully.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(31) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ankursethi.com/blog/prevent-cognitive-debt-by-manually-retyping-llm-generated-code/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Prevent cognitive debt by manually retyping LLM-generated code</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>通过手动重新输入LLM生成的代码以避免认知负债</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49153374' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 509</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49153374' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 421</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49153374'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Typing out LLM-generated code by hand helps developers verify logic and catch subtle errors, reducing cognitive debt from relying on machine output. The piece argues for deliberate code validation as a guardrail for safer AI-assisted software.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过手动重新输入LLM生成的代码来审查其逻辑，可以帮助开发者发现潜在的错误和偏差，降低因为依赖AI输出而产生的认知负担。文章呼吁在AI辅助编码场景中加入更严格的代码核对与测试。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ankursethi.com/static/favicon/favicon.svg' data-full='https://ankursethi.com/static/favicon/favicon.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49166491' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(33) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://short-stories.co/@raybradbury/there-will-come-soft-rains-6k8vr4xxlnmj' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ray Bradbury&#x27;s &quot;There Will Come Soft Rains&quot; is set today (2026-08-04)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>雷·布拉德伯里的《有一天，软雨会来》如今仿佛落在今天</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49166491' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 497</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49166491' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49166491'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Bradbury&#x27;s story about autonomous houses feels prophetic in today&#x27;s tech-saturated world. The piece notes how the tale&#x27;s themes of privacy, control, and reliance on technology align with current debates about smart homes and surveillance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>雷·布拉德伯里的《有一天，软雨会来》尽管写于1950年，但在今天的高度自动化时代显得尤为贴近。文章将故事对隐私、控制和对技术依赖的警示，与当前关于智能家居和监控的讨论相呼应。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://d2se8qvny5nxid.cloudfront.net/eyJidWNrZXQiOiJzaG9ydC1zdG9yaWVzLXdlYiIsImtleSI6ImltYWdlcy9zb2NpYWwvc29jaWFsLXNoYXJlLTMucG5nIiwiZWRpdHMiOnsicG5nIjp7InF1YWxpdHkiOjgyLCJwcm9ncmVzc2l2ZSI6ZmFsc2V9LCJyZXNpemUiOnsid2lkdGgiOjEyMDAsImhlaWdodCI6NjMwLCJmaXQiOiJjb3ZlciJ9LCJzaGFycGVuIjp0cnVlfX0=?mtime=1720649965' data-full='https://d2se8qvny5nxid.cloudfront.net/eyJidWNrZXQiOiJzaG9ydC1zdG9yaWVzLXdlYiIsImtleSI6ImltYWdlcy9zb2NpYWwvc29jaWFsLXNoYXJlLTMucG5nIiwiZWRpdHMiOnsicG5nIjp7InF1YWxpdHkiOjgyLCJwcm9ncmVzc2l2ZSI6ZmFsc2V9LCJyZXNpemUiOnsid2lkdGgiOjEyMDAsImhlaWdodCI6NjMwLCJmaXQiOiJjb3ZlciJ9LCJzaGFycGVuIjp0cnVlfX0=?mtime=1720649965' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49226636' data-tags='Hardware,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49226636' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(34) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://seg6.space/posts/phone-server/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>My server is a phone now</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我的服务器现在是一部手机</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49226636' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 493</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49226636' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 236</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49226636'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>People are running a server on a smartphone, using the device&#x27;s always-on nature and affordable hardware to host small services. This underlines edge computing feasibility with commodity devices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>将手机作为服务器的实践，展示用消费级设备承载小型服务的可行性。讨论的权衡包括电池、存储、操作系统支持和网络暴露时的安全性，并给出保持轻量、响应快速的实用建议。这也凸显了边缘计算在普通设备上的潜力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://seg6.space/img/phone-server/system-dashboard.png' data-full='https://seg6.space/img/phone-server/system-dashboard.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49207236' data-tags='Business,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49207236' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(35) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ign.com/articles/ramageddon-continues-another-year-as-2027-memory-capacity-is-reportedly-sold-out' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>2027 memory capacity is reportedly sold out</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>传闻称2027 年的内存容量已售罄</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49207236' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 489</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49207236' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 467</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49207236'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Rumors claim 2027 memory capacity is sold out, highlighting ongoing supply constraints driven by AI/demand surges and data-center needs. The chatter points to potential price volatility and longer lead times for RAM and related tech.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>传闻称到2027年的内存容量已售罄，反映持续的供给紧张、AI/数据中心对内存的高需求。此事或将带来价格波动与更长的交货期。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://assets-prd.ignimgs.com/2022/06/24/corsair-dominator-platinum-rgb-1656107201735.jpg?width=1280&amp;format=jpg&amp;auto=webp&amp;quality=80' data-full='https://assets-prd.ignimgs.com/2022/06/24/corsair-dominator-platinum-rgb-1656107201735.jpg?width=1280&amp;format=jpg&amp;auto=webp&amp;quality=80' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49223082' data-tags='Privacy,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49223082' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(36) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.fastmail.com/blog/fastmail-offers-eu-data-region/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Fastmail offers EU data region</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Fastmail 推出欧盟数据区域</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49223082' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 487</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49223082' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 279</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49223082'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Fastmail now offers an EU data region, letting customers store mail in the EU and meet data-residency preferences. The move enhances privacy-conscious deployments and GDPR compliance for EU customers, possibly affecting cross-region workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Fastmail 新增欧盟数据区域，用户可以将邮箱数据存放在欧盟境内，提升数据主权与合规性。这一举措对在欧盟运营的用户尤为有利，也可能影响跨区域工作流和备份策略。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.fastmail.com/assets/images/data-center-hero-3jPa_k89iw-1200.png' data-full='https://www.fastmail.com/assets/images/data-center-hero-3jPa_k89iw-1200.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49234675' data-tags='AI,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49234675' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(37) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://laurentiugabriel.github.io/blog/articles/how-i-use-llms-to-learn/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How I use LLMs to learn complex topics</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我如何用大语言模型学习复杂主题</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49234675' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 482</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49234675' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 273</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49234675'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>I use a staged approach to learning with LLMs: map an outline with the model, generate concise explanations, then test understanding with targeted prompts. This turns the model into a structured tutor that surfaces gaps and helps build solid mental models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>将学习分成阶段使用模型：用它勾勒大纲、生成简明解释、再通过有针对性的提问检验理解。把模型当作高效的学习辅导工具，帮助建立稳固的认知模型并暴露薄弱点。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://laurentiugabriel.github.io/blog/images/ChipTycoon.png' data-full='https://laurentiugabriel.github.io/blog/images/ChipTycoon.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49181099' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49181099' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(38) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wired.com/story/a-civilian-plane-crashed-in-new-mexico-was-the-militarys-tech-to-blame/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Civilian plane crash in New Mexico tied to military GPS blocking</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>新墨西哥民用飞机坠毁与军事GPS屏蔽相关</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49181099' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 480</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49181099' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 255</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49181099'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A civilian plane crash in New Mexico has been linked to military GPS disruptions or deliberate jamming. The incident highlights how reliance on GPS for aviation navigation can be vulnerable to adversarial tech, raising safety and policy questions about airspace interference. It underscores the need for better GPS resilience or backup navigation in civilian aviation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新墨西哥的一起民用飞机坠毁事件被关联到军事GPS干扰或阻断。此事凸显民用航空对GPS的高度依赖在对手干扰下的脆弱性，并引发关于空域干扰的安全与政策讨论，强调需要提升GPS韧性或采用备用导航方案。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.wired.com/photos/6a6a6d259beb8cd00d662700/191:100/w_1280,c_limit/072926_gps-jamming-roswell-flight.jpg' data-full='https://media.wired.com/photos/6a6a6d259beb8cd00d662700/191:100/w_1280,c_limit/072926_gps-jamming-roswell-flight.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49171268' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49171268' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(39) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mistral.ai/news/shieldstral/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mistral&#x27;s Shieldstral: 3B open-weights model for multimodal moderation</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Mistral Shieldstral：3B 开源权重的多模态审查模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49171268' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 479</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49171268' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 133</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49171268'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mistral releases Shieldstral, a 3B open-weight model designed for multimodal content moderation. It offers a compact, privately deployable option for filtering text and image content with governance controls. It aims to democratize access to responsible AI moderation at smaller scales.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mistral 推出 Shieldstral，一款3B参数的开源权重多模态内容审查模型，能够在本地部署并具备治理控制。它为小规模部署提供了高效的内容过滤能力，推动负责任的AI审核更广泛可及。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://mistral.ai/cms-media/api/media/file/Thumbnail-Model%3DShieldstral.jpg' data-full='https://mistral.ai/cms-media/api/media/file/Thumbnail-Model%3DShieldstral.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49168622' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(40) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://phoboslab.org/log/2026/08/xibalba64-making-of' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How to Make a Nintendo 64 Game in 2026</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>如何在 2026 年做出一个 Nintendo 64 游戏</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49168622' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 472</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49168622' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 240</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49168622'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A modern revisit to building a Nintendo 64 game in 2026, detailing tools, emulation strategies, and reverse-engineering approaches. It showcases how today’s tooling enables preservation and re-creation of classic hardware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍在 2026 年重新做 Nintendo 64 游戏的过程，涉及现代工具链、模拟/逆向工程以及对复古硬件的研究。展示当代工具如何让经典主机更易开发与再现，推动游戏保存与学习。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://phoboslab.org/content/assets/xibalba64/xibalba64_box_cart.png' data-full='https://phoboslab.org/content/assets/xibalba64/xibalba64_box_cart.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49188022' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(41) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://runarcn.no/android-to-linux/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I&#x27;m switching my phone from Android to Linux</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我把手机从 Android 换成 Linux</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49188022' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 457</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49188022' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 502</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49188022'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A personal blog chronicles switching from Android to a Linux-based mobile OS, detailing motivations like privacy and control and the practical hurdles of app compatibility and hardware support. It offers hands-on notes and tools, providing a candid view of what true openness looks like in a mobile device.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者记录从 Android 切换到基于 Linux 的移动系统，聚焦隐私、开源和对设备的掌控感，同时直面应用生态与硬件支持等现实挑战。文中给出实际操作笔记、使用工具，以及对移动端开放性在现实中的含义的坦诚观察。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://runarcn.no/static/og-image.png' data-full='https://runarcn.no/static/og-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49211386' data-tags='Security,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49211386' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(42) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://patronview.com/news/99-percent-of-my-website-traffic-is-bots/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A year of fighting scrapers on my 1.5 million-page website</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>一年对抗爬虫：我1.5百万页网站的战斗</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49211386' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 451</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49211386' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 413</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49211386'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>After a year fighting scrapers on a 1.5-million-page site, bot traffic dominated visits, prompting a mix of CAPTCHA, rate limiting, IP blocking, and other anti-scraping measures. The piece shares practical lessons on telemetry and defense costs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一年与爬虫斗争，一座拥有150万页的网站的访客大多为机器人流量，促使采取 CAPTCHA、限流、屏蔽 IP 等抗爬策略。文中分享了可操作的监控要点与防御成本的权衡。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49166202' data-tags='Open Source,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49166202' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(43) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/FFmpeg/FFmpeg/blob/n9.0/RELEASE_NOTES' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>FFmpeg 9.0</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>FFmpeg 9.0 发布</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49166202' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 448</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49166202' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 96</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49166202'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>FFmpeg 9.0 brings broad updates across encoding/decoding, including new codecs, format improvements, and API changes that may affect existing scripts. The release emphasizes performance gains and maintenance, with notes on deprecated features and migration steps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>FFmpeg 9.0 带来编码/解码栈的全面更新，包括新增编解码器、格式改进及 API 变更，可能影响现有脚本与工具。此版本强调性能提升与维护性，并附带弃用功能与迁移说明。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(45) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.runnersworld.com/news/a73355106/hamster-wheel-strava-running/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A physicist rigged his pet hamster’s wheel to upload to Strava</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A physicist rigged a hamster wheel to upload running data to Strava, creating a quirky example of data capture and the ease of turning real-world activity into digital metrics. It highlights how everyday objects can become data sources and prompts reflection on data provenance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一位物理学家把仓鼠轮改装，实现跑步数据上传到 Strava，成为一个有趣的关于数据采集的示例，凸显日常物品也能成为数据源并促使人们思考数据溯源。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49186762' data-tags='AI,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49186762' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(46) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://neon.com/blog/how-castform-neon-beats-frontier-models-on-price-and-efficiency' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Beating GPT-5.6 Sol on retrieval with 100x cheaper open models</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>以成本低得多的开源模型在检索任务上击败GPT-5.6 Sol</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49186762' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 427</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49186762' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 115</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49186762'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A team demonstrates competitive retrieval performance using open models that are 100x cheaper than proprietary ones, enabling cost-effective retrieval-augmented pipelines. The result suggests open models can close the gap on expensive closed models for some tasks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>团队用便宜100倍的开源模型在检索任务上取得与GPT-5.6 Sol相近的效果，推动成本更低的RAG解决方案。此成就显示开源模型在某些任务上能缩小与大型闭源模型的差距。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.neonapi.io/public/images/pages/blog/how-castform-neon-beats-frontier-models-on-price-and-efficiency/cover.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.neonapi.io/public/images/pages/blog/how-castform-neon-beats-frontier-models-on-price-and-efficiency/cover.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49220126' data-tags='AI,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49220126' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(47) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://deepmind.google/blog/weathernext-ai-model-achieves-breakthrough-in-forecasting-cyclones/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DeepMind&#x27;s WeatherNext model achieves breakthrough forecasting cyclones</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DeepMind 的 WeatherNext 在气旋预报上取得突破</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49220126' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 426</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49220126' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 125</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49220126'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DeepMind&#x27;s WeatherNext reportedly achieves breakthrough cyclone forecasting, improving accuracy and potentially lead times for storms. The model leverages advanced AI to simulate atmospheric dynamics at scale, with real-world potential for disaster response.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DeepMind 的 WeatherNext 在气旋预报方面取得突破，提升了准确性并可能缩短信号到袭来的时间窗。该模型利用大规模的气象动力学建模，若落地将对灾害应对产生实际影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/Mj8GyJnsjROScr1hYl7PL_QCAaLGukliPCTMUlpKiQtZuVkmh2ouydYh80ibejg9vWgKkg2dYPx2jCJOOohKid5P-dLzwvIyB4-fZjWXYIx0ImZd=w1200-h630-n-nu-rw' data-full='https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/Mj8GyJnsjROScr1hYl7PL_QCAaLGukliPCTMUlpKiQtZuVkmh2ouydYh80ibejg9vWgKkg2dYPx2jCJOOohKid5P-dLzwvIyB4-fZjWXYIx0ImZd=w1200-h630-n-nu-rw' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49187061' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(48) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.fogus.me/llm/born-against.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Born Against, or why hobby programming communities are against LLM usage</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>反对者的诞生：为何爱好者编程社区反对使用LLMs</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49187061' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 420</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49187061' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 500</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49187061'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post argues that some hobbyist programming circles push back on LLM usage, citing concerns about learning, ownership, and culture. It contrasts &#x27;born against&#x27; sentiment with pragmatic tool adoption, and discusses implications for tooling, tutorials, and community norms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章讨论为何某些爱好编程圈子对LLM工具持抵制态度，强调学习动机、所有权与社区文化等因素。它将“born against”的情绪与对工具的实际应用作对比，探讨这对开发工具与社区规范的影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(51) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.notebookcheck.net/Windows-11-s-built-in-Weather-app-wastes-more-than-1-GB-of-RAM.1364205.0.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Windows 11&#x27;s built-in Weather app wastes more than 1 GB of RAM</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49232138' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 414</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49232138' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 342</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49232138'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A review finds the built-in Weather app consumes over a gigabyte of RAM, highlighting how preinstalled apps can bloat systems and affect performance even on modern hardware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>评测发现 Windows 11 内置天气应用的内存占用超过 1GB，凸显系统自带应用的资源占用及对性能的潜在影响。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(52) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://users.wpi.edu/~zrbutzke/Docs/BradburyStories(1).pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>There Will Come Soft Rains (1950) [pdf]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>《软雨将至》 (1950) [PDF]</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49162653' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 411</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49162653' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 420</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49162653'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Bradbury&#x27;s iconic dystopian tale imagines an autonomous house continuing to function after humanity vanishes, a meditation on how technologies can outlive their creators. The pdf offers a concise, enduring example of science fiction about automation and loss.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>布拉伯里经典的反乌托邦短篇设想在人类消失后，自动化住宅仍在运作，思考技术若离开人类创作者会如何。该PDF提供了关于自动化与消逝的耐人深思的科幻范例。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49198069' data-tags='AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49198069' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(53) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.sydorets.com/en/posts/almost-no-skill-required-to-cook-a-steak/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Software development with AI is starting to feel like cooking steak</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用 AI 开发软件：几乎不需技能就像煎牛排一样简单</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49198069' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 411</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49198069' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 416</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49198069'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI-assisted software development lowers the skills barrier, enabling rapid prototyping and iteration. However, it also raises questions about code quality, maintainability, and developer judgment, as automated outputs require discipline and guardrails. The shift pushes teams to codify review, testing, and responsibility in an AI-enabled workflow.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI 辅助的软件开发大幅降低门槛，使快速原型与迭代成为可能。与此同时，代码质量、可维护性与开发者判断力成为新的挑战，因为自动化产出需要纪律与 guardrails。团队需要在 AI 驱动的工作流中建立审查、测试与责任分配。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.sydorets.com/assets/img/profile.jpg' data-full='https://blog.sydorets.com/assets/img/profile.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49201930' data-tags='Gaming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49201930' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(54) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://slayersclub.bethesda.net/en-US/news/quake-30th-anniversary-update' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Quake – 30th Anniversary Update</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Quake – 30 周年更新</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49201930' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 406</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49201930' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 199</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49201930'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Quake turns 30 with an anniversary update featuring new content and remaster touches. The celebration underscores the game&#x27;s enduring influence on engines, modding, and retro computing culture.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《雷神之锤》庆祝30周年，推出纪念更新，包含新内容与复刻改动，凸显这款游戏对引擎、模组化以及复古计算文化的持续影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://slayersclub.bethesda.net/_static-slayersclub/images/slayers-club.webp' data-full='https://slayersclub.bethesda.net/_static-slayersclub/images/slayers-club.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49156750' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(55) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pandoc.org/twenty-years-of-pandoc.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Twenty Years of Pandoc</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Pandoc 二十年</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49156750' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 405</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49156750' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 52</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49156750'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Pandoc marks twenty years as a universal document converter used by researchers and developers, reflecting on its evolution, community contributions, and ongoing relevance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Pandoc 已走过二十年，作为通用的文档转换工具在学术和开发工作流中被广泛采用。文章回顾其发展历程、社区贡献及对未来格式的兼容性工作。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.paypalobjects.com/en_US/i/scr/pixel.gif' data-full='https://www.paypalobjects.com/en_US/i/scr/pixel.gif' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
