<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49220609' data-tags='Security,Open Source,AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49220609' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(56) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://simonwillison.net/2026/Aug/7/openai-timeline/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Timeline of the OpenAI accidental attack against Hugging Face</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI 对 Hugging Face 的意外攻击时间线公开</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49220609' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 402</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49220609' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 392</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49220609'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A timeline release details an OpenAI incident that inadvertently affected Hugging Face, highlighting missteps in model sharing and cross-company trust in AI tooling. The account sheds light on how collaborative AI ecosystems can go awry.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>时间线披露 OpenAI 对 Hugging Face 的意外影响事件，暴露在模型共享与跨公司信任方面的处置失误。这一细节揭示了 AI 生态中的协作风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://img.youtube.com/vi/87DyyMV0kCY/maxresdefault.jpg' data-full='https://img.youtube.com/vi/87DyyMV0kCY/maxresdefault.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49217993' data-tags='Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49217993' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(57) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://discourse.nixos.org/t/the-nixpkgs-core-team-has-disbanded/79413' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Nixpkgs core team has disbanded</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Nixpkgs 核心团队解散</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49217993' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 395</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49217993' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 208</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49217993'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Nixpkgs core team has disbanded, marking a rare governance shift for the NixOS packaging ecosystem. The move signals a transition toward a more community-driven maintenance model, with potential changes in how contributions are reviewed and released. For users, this could mean slower patches in the short term but more distributed leadership in the long run.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Nixpkgs 核心团队解散，预示 NixOS 软件包生态的治理将转向更依赖社区的模式。短期内可能影响修复速度，但长期将出现更分散的维护权力与新治理路径。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://discourse.nixos.org/uploads/default/original/3X/a/5/a5e0d7873e3d34aecf874c577c91a1c06490f436.svg' data-full='https://discourse.nixos.org/uploads/default/original/3X/a/5/a5e0d7873e3d34aecf874c577c91a1c06490f436.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49171656' data-tags='Security,Privacy,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49171656' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(58) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.valleynewslive.com/2026/08/04/every-flock-camera-winona-minnesota-cut-down-stolen-coordinated-theft/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>All of Winona Police Department&#x27;s Flock cameras cut down and stolen</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Winona警察局的Flock摄像头全被砍下并盗走</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49171656' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 389</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49171656' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 244</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49171656'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>In a coordinated operation, every Flock networked camera installed by Winona PD was cut down and stolen. The incident exposes how municipal surveillance fleets remain vulnerable to physical tampering and highlights the cost and downtime of replacing hardware across a city. It also raises questions about the resilience of vendor-managed cameras and incident response for public-safety tech.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一次有组织的行动中，威诺纳警察局部署的所有Flock网络监控摄像头被砍下并盗走，城市一时无监控。事件暴露了市政监控设备在物理层面的脆弱性，以及大规模替换硬件所带来的成本和停机时间。这也引发对供应商托管摄像头韧性和公安全技改应急能力的关注。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://gray-kvly-prod.gtv-cdn.com/resizer/v2/7JWVBSRTRNDV3A7PDFGBWDXGIE.jpg?auth=e52a5ff85513d4f155a25eeeeb4bae4b3b88da0cf8dc34c271db69b3ae320ddc&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;smart=true' data-full='https://gray-kvly-prod.gtv-cdn.com/resizer/v2/7JWVBSRTRNDV3A7PDFGBWDXGIE.jpg?auth=e52a5ff85513d4f155a25eeeeb4bae4b3b88da0cf8dc34c271db69b3ae320ddc&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;smart=true' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49170479' data-tags='Security,Privacy,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49170479' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(59) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techcrunch.com/2026/08/04/apple-says-more-ex-employees-may-have-taken-confidential-data-to-openai/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple says more ex-employees may have taken confidential data to OpenAI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>苹果称更多前员工可能将机密数据带往 OpenAI</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49170479' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 384</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49170479' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 282</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49170479'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple indicates additional ex-employees may have copied confidential data to OpenAI, raising concerns about data governance and how AI vendors handle enterprise information. The episode underscores the need for strong exit controls and data-sharing policies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果表示更多前员工可能将机密数据带至 OpenAI，引发对数据治理及 AI 供应商如何处理企业信息的担忧。此事强调加强离职控制和数据共享策略的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/tim-cook-sam-altman-GettyImages-2233062347.jpg?w=1024' data-full='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/tim-cook-sam-altman-GettyImages-2233062347.jpg?w=1024' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49166386' data-tags='Hardware,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49166386' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(60) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/ryanzhou/deepseek-v4-flash-mi300x' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DeepSeek V4 Flash on a Single AMD MI300X</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DeepSeek V4 Flash 在单颗 AMD MI300X 上运行</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49166386' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 374</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49166386' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 100</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49166386'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DeepSeek V4 Flash runs on a single AMD MI300X, demonstrating a compact setup for high-performance AI workloads. This shows the potential of combining AI accelerators with unified memory to achieve strong throughput per watt, which could shift design choices for data-center AI inference.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DeepSeek V4 Flash 在单颗 AMD MI300X 上运行，展示了用于高性能 AI 工作负载的紧凑方案。该实现凸显将 AI 加速单元与统一显存结合以实现更高的吞吐功耗比，可能影响数据中心推理的架构选择。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/5b02f6ba94fe89a6a4ff02fc78bfd0cc8a80ecb85a172d7ebb29720da3941a2c/ryanzhou/deepseek-v4-flash-mi300x' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/5b02f6ba94fe89a6a4ff02fc78bfd0cc8a80ecb85a172d7ebb29720da3941a2c/ryanzhou/deepseek-v4-flash-mi300x' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49166860' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49166860' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(61) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cnbc.com/2026/08/04/us-has-used-virtually-all-of-its-long-range-precision-missiles-report.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>U.S. used &#x27;virtually all&#x27; of its long-range precision missiles during Iran war</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国在与伊朗战争中几乎用尽所有远程精确导弹</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49166860' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 373</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49166860' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 675</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49166860'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new disclosure reveals the U.S. used virtually all of its long-range precision missiles in operations against Iran. The implication is a stressed munitions stockpile and potential impacts on future readiness. It also highlights the fragility of reliance on precision stand-off weapons and may influence defense budgeting, modernization, and supply chain planning.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>最新披露显示，美国在对伊朗的行动中几乎用尽了所有远程精确导弹，暴露出弹药库存的压力及未来作战准备的潜在影响。这凸显了对高精度远程打击武器的高度依赖在持续冲突中的脆弱性，可能影响防务预算、现代化和供应链规划。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://image.cnbcfm.com/api/v1/image/108065002-1732026106584-gettyimages-824113280-96862338.jpeg?v=1732026139&amp;w=1920&amp;h=1080' data-full='https://image.cnbcfm.com/api/v1/image/108065002-1732026106584-gettyimages-824113280-96862338.jpeg?v=1732026139&amp;w=1920&amp;h=1080' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49152842' data-tags='Open Source,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49152842' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(62) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/janestreet/bonsai' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Bonsai: Janestreet&#x27;s UI Library</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Bonsai：Jane Street 的 UI 库</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49152842' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 372</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49152842' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 150</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49152842'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Bonsai is Jane Street&#x27;s UI library designed for building robust, composable UIs in OCaml with a focus on type-safety and incremental rendering. It demonstrates how a functional, language-integrated UI library can compete with broader JavaScript ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Bonsai 是 Jane Street 为 OCaml 构建的 UI 库，强调类型安全与可组合性，支持增量渲染。它展示了一个函数式、语言内置的 UI 库如何在广泛的 JavaScript 生态系统中保持竞争力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/58cda58f259c2e3e637715b0b509b634c2ba57955ec94eb3a61bbdd8ac4c7031/janestreet/bonsai' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/58cda58f259c2e3e637715b0b509b634c2ba57955ec94eb3a61bbdd8ac4c7031/janestreet/bonsai' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49184757' data-tags='Business,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49184757' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(63) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.axios.com/2026/08/05/google-deepmind-demis-hassabis-ai' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Demis Hassabis is moving from CEO to Chairman at Google DeepMind</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Demis Hassabis 将从 DeepMind 的 CEO 转任董事长</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49184757' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 371</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49184757' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49184757'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Demis Hassabis will move from CEO to Chairman at Google DeepMind, marking a major governance shift. He will remain involved as a board member while the executive team runs day-to-day operations, signaling continuity in strategy with new leadership balance. The change could influence DeepMind’s autonomy within Alphabet and its alignment with Google’s broader AI agenda.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Demis Hassabis 将从 DeepMind 首席执行官改任董事长，继续担任董事会成员并引导战略方向，日常运营将由高管团队负责，象征治理层的重大调整与策略连续性。此举可能影响 DeepMind 在 Alphabet 内的自治以及与 Google 广泛 AI 议程的对齐。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49218179' data-tags='Space,Science,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49218179' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(64) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.space.com/space-exploration/voyager/nasa-figured-out-how-to-keep-its-48-year-old-voyager-2-probe-running-for-yet-another-year' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>NASA figured out how to keep its Voyager 2 probe running for another year</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>NASA 找到让 Voyager 2 探测器再运行一年的方法</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49218179' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 371</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49218179' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 82</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49218179'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>NASA has tweaked power and software systems to extend Voyager 2’s life by about another year. The move demonstrates how long-range missions survive on conservative engineering and small, cost-efficient upgrades as hardware ages.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>NASA 通过调整供电与软件系统，使 Voyager 2 再续航约一年。这体现了在硬件老化的情况下，长期探测任务如何依靠保守设计与小幅高性价比改进来延长寿命。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/THvP2fXPd8rkVuYiU2LB97-2000-80.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/THvP2fXPd8rkVuYiU2LB97-2000-80.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49155359' data-tags='Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49155359' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(65) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.intellinews.com/wind-and-solar-overtake-fossil-fuels-in-germany-for-the-first-time-ever-458379/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Wind and solar overtake fossil fuels in Germany for the first time</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>德国风力与太阳能首次超过化石燃料发电</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49155359' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 361</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49155359' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 296</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49155359'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Germany’s electricity mix reached a milestone as wind and solar together produced more power than fossil fuels for the first time. The shift highlights rapid renewable deployment but also underlines grid reliability challenges, storage needs, and the importance of flexible generation to maintain balance. Policy support and market reforms remain critical as non-fossil share grows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>德国在电力结构上实现里程碑，风能和太阳能合计发电量首次超过化石燃料。此举显示可再生能源的快速增长，但也暴露出电网稳定、储能和调峰能力的挑战，需进一步的政策支持与市场改革来推动转型。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://d39raawggeifpx.cloudfront.net/styles/16_9_desktop/s3/articleimages/Screenshot_2026-08-01_at_15.48.16_Cropped.png' data-full='https://d39raawggeifpx.cloudfront.net/styles/16_9_desktop/s3/articleimages/Screenshot_2026-08-01_at_15.48.16_Cropped.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49219508' data-tags='Security,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49219508' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(66) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/xoreaxeaxeax/rosenbridge' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Hardware backdoors in some x86 CPUs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>某些 x86 CPU 可能存在硬件后门</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49219508' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 360</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49219508' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 101</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49219508'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Rosenbridge project points to potential hardware backdoors in certain x86 CPUs, illustrating how microarchitectural features could be misused. The finding underscores supply-chain security risks and the importance of firmware and chipset transparency.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Rosenbridge 项目指出某些 x86 CPU 可能存在硬件后门，说明微架构层面的特征可能被滥用。此发现强调了供应链安全风险，以及固件与芯片组透明度的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/43826bf9cc71979cf6a25681411cb4df54b4b13e32faed4ed166302441c1f1bb/xoreaxeaxeax/rosenbridge' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/43826bf9cc71979cf6a25681411cb4df54b4b13e32faed4ed166302441c1f1bb/xoreaxeaxeax/rosenbridge' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49209546' data-tags='Business,Finance'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49209546' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(67) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nbcnews.com/business/economy/july-2026-jobs-report-rcna591138' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>U.S. economy lost 23,000 jobs in July, a sudden reversal</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国7月就业减少2.3万，形势突然逆转</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49209546' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 351</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49209546' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 350</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49209546'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>U.S. payrolls fell by 23,000 in July, capping a surprise reversal after a stretch of gains. The report signals a cooling labor market and complicates expectations for Fed policy, highlighting the fragility of the jobs rebound and potential sector-specific softness.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美国7月就业人数下降2.3万，结束了一轮连增后的意外逆转。此数据表明劳动力市场正在降温，并给美联储的政策预期带来挑战，凸显就业反弹的脆弱性及行业性疲软的可能。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media-cldnry.s-nbcnews.com/image/upload/t_nbcnews-fp-1200-630,f_auto,q_auto:best/rockcms/2026-08/260806-job-fair-ch-0949-3e7dc6.jpg' data-full='https://media-cldnry.s-nbcnews.com/image/upload/t_nbcnews-fp-1200-630,f_auto,q_auto:best/rockcms/2026-08/260806-job-fair-ch-0949-3e7dc6.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49216946' data-tags='AI,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49216946' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(68) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://genesisopenmodels.anl.gov/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>U.S. Department of Energy Launches the Genesis Open Models Initiative</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国能源部启动 Genesis Open Models Initiative</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49216946' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 348</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49216946' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 154</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49216946'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Genesis Open Models Initiative aims to develop and openly share foundational AI models for energy research, fostering collaboration across national labs, academia, and industry. The program emphasizes responsible AI practices and provides benchmarks to evaluate model performance in energy-centric tasks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美国能源部启动 Genesis Open Models Initiative，致力于开发并公开共享用于能源研究的基础 AI 模型，促进国家实验室、学界与产业界的协作，并强调负责任的 AI 实践与评估基准。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://genesisopenmodels.anl.gov/wp-content/uploads/sites/134/2026/07/Logo-lockup-transparent.png' data-full='https://genesisopenmodels.anl.gov/wp-content/uploads/sites/134/2026/07/Logo-lockup-transparent.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49214863' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(69) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://daringfireball.net/2026/08/app_store_rejection_of_the_week_dark_hours' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>App Store Rejection of the Week: Dark Hours</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>本周应用商店拒绝案例：Dark Hours</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49214863' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 344</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49214863' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 225</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49214863'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Dark Hours was rejected by the App Store this week, underscoring ongoing tensions between developers and Apple’s review rules. The snippet highlights how policy interpretation and gatekeeping shape indie app viability, monetization, and discoverability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Dark Hours 本周遭苹果应用商店拒绝，凸显开发者与苹果审核规则之间持续的紧张关系。此事揭示了政策解释和准入门槛如何影响独立应用的生存、创收和曝光。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(70) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.andrewt.net/dithered-qr-codes/wtf/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Dithered QR Codes</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>抖动化二维码</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49226742' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 340</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49226742' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 37</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49226742'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Dithered QR Codes describe rendering QR codes with dithering patterns to work better on limited-color displays or printers while preserving scanability. Explains trade-offs for scan reliability and potential visual aesthetics or data encoding extensions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>抖动化二维码通过抖动模式在有限颜色深度下保持可读性，兼顾美观与易扫性。文中讨论了对扫描稳定性、容错和潜在的扩展应用的权衡。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(71) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://simonwillison.net/2026/Jul/31/stateless-mcp/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Stateless MCP has recaptured my interest</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>对无状态 MCP 的再度关注</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49131438' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 338</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49131438' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 191</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49131438'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A fresh look at a stateless MCP design, weighing its scalability and fault-tolerance benefits against traditional stateful approaches.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者重新审视无状态的 MCP 设计，权衡其在分布式系统中的可扩展性和容错性相对状态化方案的优势与折衷。文章探讨通过小幅架构改动即可提升性能和部署简化的可能性。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Show HN: Isopolis—an isometric pixel map of San Francisco. The project renders SF in a retro isometric pixel art style, creating a compact, zoomable map ideal for demos, games, or creative data visualization.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Show HN：Isopolis 将旧金山以等距像素风格呈现，提供一个复古风格、可缩放的像素地图，适合演示、游戏或创意数据可视化。它展示了在简化街区布局的同时保留地标特征的可视化方法。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(73) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://clickhouse.com/blog/andy-pavlo-joins-clickhouse' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Andy Pavlo joins ClickHouse to establish ClickHouse Labs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Andy Pavlo 加入 ClickHouse，设立 ClickHouse Labs</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49156011' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 336</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49156011' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 75</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49156011'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Andy Pavlo, a respected database researcher, is joining ClickHouse to launch ClickHouse Labs. The lab will pursue applied research and experimentation around performance, analytics workloads, and new features for the open-source columnar store.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>知名数据库研究者 Andy Pavlo 加入 ClickHouse，设立 ClickHouse Labs。该实验室将开展面向实际场景的研究与实验，聚焦性能、分析工作负载及新特性的探索。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49175192' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(74) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.troyhunt.com/thanks-fedex-this-is-why-we-keep-getting-phished/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Thanks FedEx, This Is Why We Keep Getting Phished (2024)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>感谢 FedEx，这就是我们持续遭遇钓鱼攻击的原因（2024）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49175192' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 335</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49175192' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 81</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49175192'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece uses FedEx-style phishing lures to illustrate why credential harvesting remains effective. It emphasizes user education, verification habits, and layered security controls like MFA to blunt phishing and supply-chain social engineering.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章通过 FedEx 为例的钓鱼手段说明为何钓鱼攻击屡禁不止，强调提升用户教育、核验流程和多因素认证等防御策略的重要性。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49138446' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(75) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://saopaulosecreto.com/en/tiquatira-linear-park-en/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>São Paulo resident transforms degraded area into urban forest</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>圣保罗居民将退化地区改造成城市森林</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49138446' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 335</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49138446' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 120</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49138446'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A resident in São Paulo turns a degraded site into an urban forest, showcasing community-led greening as a tool for urban resilience. The project hints at scalable, local approaches to biodiversity and climate adaptation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>圣保罗一名居民将一处退化区域改造成城市森林，展示社区主导的城市绿化如何提升韧性与生物多样性。该项目或为城市绿色改造提供可借鉴的模式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://offloadmedia.feverup.com/saopaulosecreto.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/04/08142257/1440px-Tiquatira14.jpg' data-full='https://offloadmedia.feverup.com/saopaulosecreto.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/04/08142257/1440px-Tiquatira14.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49195468' data-tags='AI,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49195468' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(76) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://scalex.dev/blog/ai-agent-permissions-stats/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Humans missed 1 in 3 threats approving AI agent commands across 40k game runs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>人类在40k次游戏中错过了1/3的 AI代理命令批准</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49195468' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 332</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49195468' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 243</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49195468'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A study finds humans approve AI agent commands incorrectly about one-third of the time across large-scale simulations, highlighting pervasive risk in human-in-the-loop AI governance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究发现在人机协同的AI代理控制中，人工在大量仿真中错误批准命令的比例约为三分之一，揭示人机协作治理中的普遍风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://scalex.dev/img/permission_game_stats_card.png' data-full='https://scalex.dev/img/permission_game_stats_card.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49155629' data-tags='AI,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49155629' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(77) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.comfy.org/p/minimax-h3-day-0-support-in-comfyui' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>MiniMax H3 Day-0 Support in ComfyUI: Open Weights, Native Audio, and 2K Video</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ComfyUI 的 MiniMax H3 Day-0 支持：开放权重、原生音频与 2K 视频</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49155629' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 329</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49155629' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 93</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49155629'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ComfyUI adds Day-0 support for MiniMax H3, including open weights, native audio, and 2K video capabilities. This lowers barriers for experimentation and makes high-efficiency models more accessible to hobbyists and developers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ComfyUI 为 MiniMax H3 提供 Day-0 支持，开放权重、原生音频及 2K 视频功能，降低了试验门槛。这样爱好者和开发者更容易在本地体验高效模型的应用场景。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!w-S9!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F67ff80a9-2436-4749-a553-5f6c8c77e23c_1920x1080.png' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!w-S9!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F67ff80a9-2436-4749-a553-5f6c8c77e23c_1920x1080.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49184190' data-tags='Privacy,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49184190' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(78) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.404media.co/cops-used-flock-to-track-a-man-across-state-lines-to-create-pretext-to-search-his-car-for-weed/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cops Used Flock to Track a Man Across State Lines for a Pretextual Weed Search</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>警察用 Flock 跨州追踪男子，为大麻搜查制造借口</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49184190' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 327</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49184190' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 192</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49184190'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A watchdog report shows police used Flock’s license-plate data to track a man across state lines to manufacture a pretext for a marijuana search. The case highlights civil-liberties concerns around automated surveillance and cross-border data sharing. It’s likely to fuel calls for stronger oversight and tighter restrictions on data brokers in policing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>监督机构的报告显示，警方利用 Flock 的车牌数据跨州追踪一名男子，以制造借口进行大麻搜查。此事凸显自动化监控和跨境数据共享对公民自由的风险，可能推动对数据经纪商的更严格监管与监督。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.ghost.io/c/0f/76/0f76b548-bc58-4f25-abc3-3f5ebca07da4/content/images/size/w1200/2026/08/FLOCK-04.jpg' data-full='https://storage.ghost.io/c/0f/76/0f76b548-bc58-4f25-abc3-3f5ebca07da4/content/images/size/w1200/2026/08/FLOCK-04.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49187575' data-tags='AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49187575' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(79) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://research.meta.ai/blog/introducing-muse-code-and-muse-spark-1-2' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Muse Code and Muse Spark 1.2</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Muse Code 与 Muse Spark 1.2</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49187575' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 326</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49187575' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 257</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49187575'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Muse Code and Muse Spark 1.2 update brings improvements to code generation and AI-assisted tasks, with new features and performance tweaks. It positions Muse as a toolkit for developers to compose, test, and deploy AI-powered workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Muse Code 与 Muse Spark 1.2 发布，带来代码生成与 AI 辅助任务的改进，并引入新特性与性能提升，为开发者提供更高效的 AI 工作流工具。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49172836' data-tags='Business,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49172836' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(80) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://waymo.com/blog/shorts/dallas-open-to-all/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Waymo in Dallas</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Waymo 在达拉斯：面向公众开放的自动驾驶</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49172836' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 323</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49172836' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 706</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49172836'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Waymo expands its autonomous ride-hailing program in Dallas, inviting the public to use its self-driving cars. The move signals progress toward broader commercial rollout while regulators weigh safety and city readiness.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Waymo 在达拉斯扩大自动驾驶打车计划，向公众开放使用其无驾驶员车辆。此举标志着更广泛商业化落地的推进，同时需关注安全性与城市适配的监管环境。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/UpGFncEYbr15chkw9d8DPh26pB8KSQq98ds8DQBSI96OgziXaaUYsebvbdgAS1a-e29jRUm2AqAmJwB-1M_B9BEtrWwfBNBNIg' data-full='https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/UpGFncEYbr15chkw9d8DPh26pB8KSQq98ds8DQBSI96OgziXaaUYsebvbdgAS1a-e29jRUm2AqAmJwB-1M_B9BEtrWwfBNBNIg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49187977' data-tags='AI,Legal,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49187977' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(81) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wired.com/story/meta-ran-ads-that-contained-ai-generated-child-sexual-abuse-imagery/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Meta Ran Ads That Contained AI-Generated Child Sexual Abuse Imagery</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Meta 投放含有 AI 生成的儿童性虐待意象广告</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49187977' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 321</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49187977' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 265</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49187977'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Meta reportedly ran ads that contained AI-generated imagery depicting child sexual abuse, exposing a dangerous gap in content moderation for AI media. The incident underscores the risks of scaling automated content and the regulatory exposure that comes with ad placements. It argues for stricter policies and more robust vetting of AI-generated material in advertising.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据报道，Meta 投放的广告中包含 AI 生成的儿童性虐待意象，暴露了 AI 媒体内容审核的严重漏洞。此事凸显了大规模自动化内容的风险以及广告投放所带来的监管压力，强调需要更严格的政策与对 AI 生成材料的更强审核。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.wired.com/photos/6a6d058753afe1590265744d/191:100/w_1280,c_limit/073126-meta-nudify-ads-children.jpg' data-full='https://media.wired.com/photos/6a6d058753afe1590265744d/191:100/w_1280,c_limit/073126-meta-nudify-ads-children.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49208535' data-tags='Programming,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49208535' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(82) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://malisper.me/how-we-made-postgres-hundreds-of-times-faster-the-query-engine/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Making Postgres 300x faster for analytics: batching, operator fusion, and SIMD</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>通过批处理、算子融合和 SIMD 将 Postgres 的分析性能提升约300倍</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49208535' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 321</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49208535' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 159</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49208535'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Describe how a data-intensive PostgreSQL optimization leverages batching, operator fusion, and SIMD to accelerate analytic queries by hundreds of times, including practical implications for OLAP workloads. It shows the potential of rethinking query execution with retrofits rather than full engine rewrite.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过对批处理、算子融合与 SIMD 的应用，将 PostgreSQL 的分析查询性能提高到原来的数百倍。这些技术实现对 OLAP 场景具有显著意义，也展示了在不重写引擎的情况下提升性能的可行路径。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://malisper.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/image.png' data-full='https://malisper.me/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49191624' data-tags='Politics,Business,DevOps,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49191624' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(83) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.costar.com/article/970809918/nashville-council-approves-eminent-domain-action-to-halt-data-center-project' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nashville uses eminent domain to block data center near zoo</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>纳什维尔动用征用权阻止动物园附近的数据中心</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49191624' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 319</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49191624' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 470</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49191624'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Nashville approves eminent domain action to halt a data center project near the zoo, signaling how cities leverage legal tools to shape tech development near sensitive spaces. The move underscores tensions between infrastructure growth, land use, and public spaces, and could set a precedent for how municipalities manage future facilities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>纳什维尔市议会批准就动物园附近的数据中心项目使用征用权，以阻止建设。这一举动凸显了城市在基础设施扩张、土地利用与公共空间之间的博弈，并可能为未来在敏感区域的科技项目定下先例。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49164896' data-tags='Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49164896' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(84) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lilianweng.github.io/posts/2026-07-04-harness/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Harness engineering for self-improvement</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用工程学提升自我</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49164896' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 318</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49164896' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 75</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49164896'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Advocates applying engineering methods to personal growth, with clear goals, measurement, and rapid iteration. It recommends modular experiments and lightweight tools to optimize habits, learning, and productivity, while warning against over-optimizing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>主张将工程思维用于个人成长，设定明确目标、量化进度并进行快速迭代。建议用模块化的实验和轻量级工具来优化习惯、学习与生产力，同时警惕过度优化带来的副作用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://lilianweng.github.io/posts/2026-07-04-harness/openai-agent-loop.png' data-full='https://lilianweng.github.io/posts/2026-07-04-harness/openai-agent-loop.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49226536' data-tags='Programming,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49226536' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(85) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://shopify.engineering/scaling-inventory-reservations' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We replaced Redis with MySQL for inventory reservations and it scaled</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用 MySQL 替代 Redis 实现库存预留并实现扩展</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49226536' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 318</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49226536' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 220</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49226536'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Shopify engineering case study shows replacing Redis with MySQL for an inventory reservation system, achieving scalable performance and simpler maintenance, with tradeoffs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一家 Shopify 的工程案例展示，将库存预留系统从 Redis 替换为 MySQL，并实现了可扩展性与维护简化，同时也伴随一些权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.shopify.com/b/shopify-brochure2-assets/d5a289aad251536cebddff8378b429f0.png' data-full='https://cdn.shopify.com/b/shopify-brochure2-assets/d5a289aad251536cebddff8378b429f0.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Munich funds libexpat for up to six months to support dedicated OSS maintenance. This reflects growing municipal backing for open-source projects that undergird critical infrastructure, offering developers breathing room and stability. Such programs can help avert maintainer burnout and keep security patches timely.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>慕尼黑市宣布为 libexpat 提供最长六个月的资助，旨在支持其维护工作。此举体现出城市对支撑关键基础设施的开源项目日益增加的资助力度，为开发者提供喘息空间和稳定性。此类计划有助于缓解维护者倦怠并确保安全补丁的及时性。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI improves GPT-5.6 Sol integration in ChatGPT and expands GPT-5.6 Luna access to free users, broadening access to advanced capabilities while raising questions about cost and usage limits.</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49132992' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49132992' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(89) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://200ms.thenodebook.com' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>200 Milliseconds</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>200毫秒</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49132992' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 311</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49132992' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 95</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49132992'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Interaction latency around 200 milliseconds is enough to feel instantaneous; designers should aim to keep critical interactions under this threshold. The piece surveys measurement strategies and practical techniques—prefetching, streaming, edge computing—to close the latency gap without compromising reliability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>大约200毫秒的交互延迟会给人“瞬时响应”的感觉，设计应围绕这一阈值优化关键交互。文中探讨了测量方法与实际做法，如预取、流式渲染以及边缘计算，以在不影响可靠性的前提下缩短延迟。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://200ms.thenodebook.com/og.png' data-full='https://200ms.thenodebook.com/og.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49158333' data-tags='AI,Show HN,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49158333' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(90) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/leonickson1/Swiftlet' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Run an 80B Qwen in 4.3 GB of RAM on a Mac, and a 35B on an iPhone</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：在 Mac 4.3 GB RAM 条件下跑 80B 的 Qwen，在 iPhone 上跑 35B</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49158333' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 306</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49158333' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 137</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49158333'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A hands-on project demonstrates running large language models on consumer hardware, with an 80B Qwen on a Mac using 4.3 GB RAM and a 35B variant on an iPhone. The approach relies on memory-efficient quantization and optimization to enable on-device inference, reducing cloud dependence and latency.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个实操项目展示在普通硬件上运行大型语言模型：在 Mac 上用 4.3 GB RAM 运行 80B 的 Qwen，在 iPhone 上运行 35B。该方法依赖低内存量化与优化，使本地推理成为可能，降低对云端的依赖并降低延迟。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/2a3a548edec40d7f62988210a5273a0adac41dc033d291c080d9ebfe1ba39f43/leonickson1/Swiftlet' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/2a3a548edec40d7f62988210a5273a0adac41dc033d291c080d9ebfe1ba39f43/leonickson1/Swiftlet' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(91) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.databricks.com/blog/managing-ai-coding-costs-scale' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Managing AI Coding Costs at Scale</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49214468' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 305</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49214468' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 262</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49214468'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Databricks outlines practical methods to control AI-assisted coding costs at scale, including model selection, caching, and workflow optimizations that curb runaway spend while preserving developer productivity. The guidance helps teams balance AI benefits with budget discipline.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Databricks 给出在大规模场景下控制 AI 编码成本的实用方法，包括模型选择、缓存和工作流优化，以在不牺牲开发效率的前提下降低支出。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49157128' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(92) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.marlerblog.com/case-news/taylor-farms-has-rewritten-its-cyclospora-statement-four-times-in-sixteen-days-it-still-has-not-said-what-changed-at-that-plant-after-2013-or-why-two-thousand-negative-tests-should-mean-an/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Taylor Farms has rewritten its cyclospora statement four times in sixteen days</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Taylor Farms 就环孢子虫声明在十六天内四次改写</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49157128' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 303</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49157128' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 259</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49157128'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Taylor Farms has updated its cyclospora-related statement four times over sixteen days, signaling ongoing uncertainty about test results and disclosure timing. The evolving messaging draws attention to how food-safety communications intersect with regulatory scrutiny and public trust. Such opacity can hinder consumer confidence and highlight pressure on food producers to provide timely, transparent updates.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Taylor Farms 在十六天内将关于环孢子虫的声明改了四次，显示对检测结果和信息披露时间的持续不确定。消息不断演变凸显了食品安全沟通与监管审查之间的张力，以及公众信任的影响。此类不透明可能削弱消费者信心，并凸显企业在透明更新方面的压力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.bannerbear.com/direct/y0aJ23zRDdqMxX4OGl/requests/000/155/553/685/KPbegp4noQkMwrnkzlVkN03vE/126c7504d0db2e66bcc581f09cf56c3c81fc79ea.png' data-full='https://images.bannerbear.com/direct/y0aJ23zRDdqMxX4OGl/requests/000/155/553/685/KPbegp4noQkMwrnkzlVkN03vE/126c7504d0db2e66bcc581f09cf56c3c81fc79ea.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49079995' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49079995' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(93) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.redblobgames.com/pathfinding/heuristics/differential.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Improving Heuristics for A* Pathfinding</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>改进 A* 寻路的启发式方法</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49079995' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 303</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49079995' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 31</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49079995'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Differential heuristics improve A* pathfinding by reusing search information across related problems, reducing work without sacrificing optimality. The approach can dramatically speed up path planning in games and robotics, especially on large grids with varying scales.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>差分启发式在 A* 寻路中的应用，复用近似问题的搜索信息，显著减少计算量，不牺牲最优解。这对游戏开发和机器人路径规划等需要高效寻路的场景尤为有用。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An update retracts a prior claim that an App Store rejection was correct, indicating the original judgment was mistaken. The note underscores the ambiguity in app-review outcomes and the value of careful reporting.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>此更新撤销了此前关于某次 App Store 拒绝“正确性”的说法，表示原本的判断有误。该说明凸显应用商店审核结果的模糊性以及准确报道的重要性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(95) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.promptarmor.com/resources/atlassian-rovo-exfiltrates-data' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Atlassian Rovo Exfiltrates Data, Bypassing Controls</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Atlassian 的 Rovo 数据外泄，绕过安全控制</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49185983' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 298</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49185983' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 136</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49185983'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Security analysis shows Atlassian Rovo can exfiltrate data by bypassing controls, highlighting gaps in data leakage protections. The report suggests mitigations and heightened monitoring to prevent similar abuse.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>安全分析指明 Atlassian 的 Rovo 能绕过控制进行数据外泄，暴露数据保护中的漏洞。报告提出相应的缓解措施和加强监控的建议。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49184746' data-tags='AI,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49184746' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(96) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nytimes.com/2026/08/05/technology/google-researchers-ai-startup.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Jeff Dean leaving Alphabet</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Jeff Dean 将离开 Alphabet</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49184746' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 297</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49184746' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49184746'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Jeff Dean, a foundational figure in Google’s AI efforts, is leaving Alphabet. The departure could affect Google AI’s momentum, talent strategy, and partnership dynamics as Alphabet/Google pivots toward new leadership models or spinouts. The timing comes amid widespread industry shifts in AI research and commercialization.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作为 Google AI 核心人物的 Jeff Dean 将离开 Alphabet。此举可能影响 Google AI 的发展势头、人才策略与伙伴关系，在 Alphabet/Google 推进新领导模式或新创企业时尤为关键。当前正值 AI 研究与商业化领域的行业变动期。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49184355' data-tags='Science,Space,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49184355' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(97) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nso.edu/press-release/nsf-inouye-solar-telescope-enables-major-discovery-of-a-hidden-solar-process/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Scientists discover Kelvin-Helmholtz Instability on the surface of the Sun</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>科学家在太阳表面发现Kelvin-Helmholtz不稳定性</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49184355' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 296</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49184355' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 60</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49184355'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers report Kelvin-Helmholtz instability on the Sun&#x27;s surface, revealing turbulent shear-driven structures in solar plasma. The finding informs models of energy transport, magnetic activity, and space weather forecasting, aided by high-resolution solar telescopes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>科学家在太阳表面观测到Kelvin-Helmholtz不稳定性，揭示太阳等离子体中的湍流剪切结构。此发现能帮助改进能源传输、磁活动与空间天气预测的模型，并得益于高分辨率太阳望远镜的观测。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://nso1.b-cdn.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/PR-WP-Banner.jpg' data-full='https://nso1.b-cdn.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/PR-WP-Banner.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(98) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openreview.net/challenge?redirect=%2Fforum%3Fid%3DklU4737opt' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Position: LLMs Can&#x27;t Jump</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>大型语言模型不能轻易跳跃推理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49181083' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 294</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49181083' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 195</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49181083'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Challenges the notion that LLMs can perform complex multi-step reasoning with jumps; argues for incremental reasoning, tool use, and constraints to prevent brittle leaps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>质疑大型语言模型能跳跃式完成复杂推理的说法，强调分步推理、工具辅助与约束以避免脆弱的跳跃式结论。</p>

</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(100) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.africanews.com/2026/08/04/ai-fuels-more-than-half-of-cybercrime-in-africa-as-digital-scams-surge-interpol/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI fuels more than half of cybercrime in Africa as scams surge – Interpol</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI推动非洲网络犯罪占比超半数，数字诈骗激增——INTERPOL</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49175826' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 292</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49175826' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 241</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49175826'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Interpol reports that AI-enabled tools are underpinning more than half of cybercrime in Africa as digital scams surge. The rise underscores how accessible AI improves criminals’ capabilities and broadens risk for individuals and businesses, prompting calls for stronger regional collaboration and practical defenses.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>INTERPOL数据显示，AI 助力的网络犯罪在非洲占比超过一半，数字诈骗案件持续上升。这一趋势提升个人和企业的风险，推动加强区域协作、情报共享与网络防护措施。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.euronews.com/articles/stories/09/86/16/37/1024x538_cmsv2_22bead50-7add-5dea-a3fe-b0aa30cab0eb-9861637.jpg' data-full='https://images.euronews.com/articles/stories/09/86/16/37/1024x538_cmsv2_22bead50-7add-5dea-a3fe-b0aa30cab0eb-9861637.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49220339' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 291</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49220339' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 449</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49220339'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A cluster of suicides within the US military cyber command prompts scrutiny of mental health support and workplace culture in high-stress tech units. The reporting highlights systemic pressures and the need for better wellness resources and leadership attention.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美军网络司令部出现自杀事件群聚，引发对高压技术单位心理健康支持与工作文化的关注。报道强调系统性压力与对更好健康资源、领导力关注的需求。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(102) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://queue.acm.org/detail.cfm?id=3807963' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Eight Myths on Software Engineering and GenAI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>关于软件工程与生成式AI的八大误解</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49176830' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 287</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49176830' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 247</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49176830'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Eight common myths about applying GenAI to software engineering are debunked, from overestimating productivity to ignoring debugging. The piece urges engineers to pair AI with disciplined practices and robust verification. Expect nuanced gains rather than a silver-bullet sunset.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文揭穿了将生成式 AI 应用于软件工程的八大常见误解，包括高估生产力与忽略调试等问题。文章强调应将 AI 与规范化实践和强验证相结合，传递的是对收益的细致认知而非一夜之间的全能解决方案。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(103) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.greyblake.com/blog/branchless-rust/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Branchless Rust: Making a Filter 4x Faster by Removing an If</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Branchless Rust：通过去除一个分支实现筛选速度提升4x</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49151991' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 282</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49151991' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 104</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49151991'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Rust blog post demonstrates a branchless approach that removes a single if, yielding a fourfold speedup for a filter. The technique reduces branch mispredictions and can improve vectorization, illustrating a practical micro-optimization for systems code.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇 Rust 博客展示了通过去除一个条件分支实现分支无关执行，从而使筛选器速度提升4倍。该做法减少分支预测失败并提升向量化潜力，凸显了面向系统代码的实用微优化。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>TIME is creating a separate website experience for AI bots, embedding ads directly into that bot-facing surface. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>TIME 为 AI 机器人提供独立的网站体验，将广告直接嵌入机器人端界面。这标志着在 AI 驱动互动的商业化中出现新的模式，超越传统读者网站的广告模式。广告可信度、用户信任以及机器人驱动内容的商业模式成为新的讨论点。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(116) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theregister.com/security/2026/08/07/water-system-controllers-dont-belong-on-the-internet-says-ex-nsa-chief-after-suspected-iran-attacks/5285070' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Water system controllers don&#x27;t belong on the internet, says ex-NSA chief</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>前NSA主管：水系统控制器不应连上互联网</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49216362' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 250</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49216362' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 160</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49216362'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An ex-NSA chief warns that water system controllers belong on isolated networks, not the internet, highlighting vulnerability to cyberattacks on critical infrastructure. The discussion emphasizes network segmentation, robust monitoring, and incident response as essential defenses for OT assets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>前NSA主管警告称，水系统控制器应置于隔离网络中，不应直连互联网，强调对关键基础设施的网络分段、监控与事件响应等必要防护。</p>
