<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49154237' data-tags='Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49154237' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(118) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wired.com/story/ice-dna-collection-fbi-codis/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ICE Collected Nearly 1M People&#x27;s DNA Last Year–Including Young Children</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ICE去年收集了近100万人的DNA样本</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49154237' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 248</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49154237' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 107</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49154237'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ICE reported collecting DNA from nearly one million people last year, including young children, raising civil-liberties and policy concerns about biometrics and data retention. The disclosure prompts scrutiny of how genetic data is stored, shared, and used in enforcement contexts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ICE去年从近100万名个人身上采集DNA样本，甚至包括幼童，这引发关于生物识别数据用途、隐私保护和执法权力边界的担忧。公开数据引发对生物信息存储、共享与长期保留的监管与问责问题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.wired.com/photos/6a6ced459baed1318e005118/191:100/w_1280,c_limit/073126-SECURITY-ice-data-collection.jpg' data-full='https://media.wired.com/photos/6a6ced459baed1318e005118/191:100/w_1280,c_limit/073126-SECURITY-ice-data-collection.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49166874' data-tags='Security,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49166874' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(119) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.aikido.dev/blog/keyv-and-friends-compromised-in-npm-supply-chain-attack' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Keyv and friends compromised in active Shai-Hulud supply chain attack</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Keyv及相关包在Shai-Hulud供应链攻击中被入侵</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49166874' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 247</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49166874' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 133</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49166874'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Security researchers report the Keyv package and related dependencies were compromised in an active npm supply-chain attack nicknamed Shai-Hulud. Attackers likely gained access to maintainer credentials and published malicious package versions, underscoring the ongoing risk of open-source dependencies. Remediation includes rotating tokens, auditing downstream dependencies, and verifying integrity of published modules.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>安全研究人员指出，Keyv及相关依赖在名为Shai-Hulud的npm供应链攻击中被入侵。攻击者可能获取维护者凭证并发布了带恶意代码的版本，凸显开源依赖持续带来的风险。应对措施包括轮换令牌、审计下游依赖、并核验发布包的完整性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/642adcaf364024654c71df23/6a71ca1f4e190f1129a4d78a_Social_ShaiHulud-Template-Light_16x9.png' data-full='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/642adcaf364024654c71df23/6a71ca1f4e190f1129a4d78a_Social_ShaiHulud-Template-Light_16x9.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49232221' data-tags='Politics,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49232221' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(120) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techcrunch.com/2026/08/09/historian-jill-lepore-says-the-tech-industry-is-led-by-bad-readers-who-are-undermining-democracy/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Silicon Valley misreads science fiction and undermines democracy</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>硅谷误读科幻，削弱民主</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49232221' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 247</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49232221' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 198</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49232221'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Historian Jill Lepore argues that Silicon Valley misreads science fiction, fostering technocratic thinking and undermining democratic norms by prioritizing fast innovation over democratic deliberation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>历史学家 Jill Lepore 指出硅谷误读科幻，导致技术精英主义思维抬头，因强调快速创新而削弱民主协商与治理的深度。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/GettyImages-491300440.jpg?w=1024' data-full='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/GettyImages-491300440.jpg?w=1024' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49145590' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(121) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pudding.cool/2026/07/essential-words/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How the words we teach English language learners changed</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们教给英语学习者的单词如何改变</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49145590' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 246</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49145590' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 190</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49145590'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An exploration of how teaching essential words to English learners has evolved, highlighting shifts in frequency and utility. The data-driven piece shows how vocabulary priorities change with pedagogy, technology, and global needs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章探讨了对英语学习者教授的“核心单词”体系如何随时间演变，强调词汇的频率与实用性变化。通过数据驱动的呈现，展示了在教学、技术与全球需求推动下，词汇教学的优先级如何调整。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pudding.cool/2026/07/essential-words/assets/social-facebook.jpg' data-full='https://pudding.cool/2026/07/essential-words/assets/social-facebook.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49170001' data-tags='Security,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49170001' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(122) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://this.weekinsecurity.com/online-advertising-giant-adform-was-hacked-proving-once-again-why-ad-blockers-are-necessary/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Online ad giant Adform was hacked, proving once again why ad blockers are needed</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>广告巨头 Adform 被黑，再次证明广告拦截器的重要性</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49170001' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 245</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49170001' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 102</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49170001'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Adform, a major online advertising platform, suffered a cyberattack exposing the complexity and risks of ad tech supply chains. The breach disrupted campaigns and reinforced why many tech-savvy users rely on ad blockers for privacy, security, and performance. It also highlights the cascading risk across ad networks from vendor-side breaches.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>知名在线广告平台 Adform 遭遇网络攻击，暴露了广告科技供应链的复杂性与风险。此次入侵影响广告投放并再次凸显了技术精通用户对广告拦截器在隐私、安全与性能方面的作用，同时暴露了来自供应商端的连锁风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.ghost.io/c/ed/a2/eda2c6f7-faef-48b4-9ed4-86a4fa4dca68/content/images/size/w1200/2026/08/david-pupaza-heNwUmEtZzo-unsplash-1.jpg' data-full='https://storage.ghost.io/c/ed/a2/eda2c6f7-faef-48b4-9ed4-86a4fa4dca68/content/images/size/w1200/2026/08/david-pupaza-heNwUmEtZzo-unsplash-1.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49152255' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(123) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.makeuseof.com/i-stopped-trusting-usb-c-cable-labels-started-testing-with-meter-instead/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I stopped trusting USB-C cable labels and started testing them</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我不再信任 USB-C 数据线标签，开始用万用表测试</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49152255' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 243</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49152255' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 228</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49152255'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>USB-C cables are frequently mislabeled about power and data specs; testing with a multimeter reveals reliability gaps. The post argues for consumer diligence and stronger standards to prevent compromised performance or safety.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>USB-C 数据线常被错误标注功率和数据规格，用万用表测试后暴露可靠性缺口。文章呼吁提高标准并增强消费者核验，以避免性能不足或安全隐患。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static0.makeuseofimages.com/wordpress/wp-content/uploads/wm/2026/07/usb-testing-tool.jpg?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=crop' data-full='https://static0.makeuseofimages.com/wordpress/wp-content/uploads/wm/2026/07/usb-testing-tool.jpg?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=crop' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49096439' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(124) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pareto_front' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Pareto Front</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>帕累托前沿</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49096439' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 242</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49096439' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 97</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49096439'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An explainer on the Pareto front in multi-objective optimization, illustrating trade-offs and how to reason about competing goals in design and ML systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>关于多目标优化中的帕累托前沿的入门解读，展示不同目标之间的权衡以及在设计与机器学习系统中的决策思路。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/b/b7/Front_pareto.svg/1280px-Front_pareto.svg.png' data-full='https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/b/b7/Front_pareto.svg/1280px-Front_pareto.svg.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49097961' data-tags='Programming,Culture,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49097961' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(125) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://stackoverflow.blog/2026/07/29/developers-are-attached-to-tools-because-tools-encode-trust/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Developers are attached to tools because tools encode trust</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>开发者依赖工具，因为工具编码信任</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49097961' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 240</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49097961' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 129</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49097961'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Developers grow attached to tools because those tools encode trust—promises about behavior, reliability, and security. Once trust is established, teams keep using the same tools even as new options emerge, shaping workflows and ecosystem choices. The piece reflects on how tooling design subtly steers engineering decisions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>开发者之所以依赖工具，是因为工具把信任编码进去——对行为、可靠性与安全性的承诺。一旦建立信任，团队就会继续使用相同的工具，即使出现新的替代方案，也会被工作流和生态系统取向所左右。文章反映了工具设计如何在潜移默化中引导工程决策。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.stackoverflow.co/images/jo7n4k8s/production/29bbae9c01de534b803084e4f0b9a77f9cd90e5c-12000x6300.jpg?w=1200&amp;h=630&amp;auto=format' data-full='https://cdn.stackoverflow.co/images/jo7n4k8s/production/29bbae9c01de534b803084e4f0b9a77f9cd90e5c-12000x6300.jpg?w=1200&amp;h=630&amp;auto=format' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49152023' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49152023' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(126) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/rust-lang/rust-project-goals/blob/main/src/2026/move-trait.md' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Rust project goals: Immobile types and guaranteed destructors</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Rust 项目目标：不可移动的类型与保证析构函数</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49152023' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 236</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49152023' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 100</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49152023'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Rust project goals discuss immobile (unmovable) types and guaranteed destructors, likely addressing pinned types and safe drop semantics to enable safer self-referential and low-level abstractions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文档聚焦不可移动类型与保证析构，涉及固定型与安全析构语义，旨在提升自引用及底层抽象的安全性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/3ea59731459cd782a40c5f2c5dbd8f99ce10531266254f181e8f53640bd82b4c/rust-lang/rust-project-goals' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/3ea59731459cd782a40c5f2c5dbd8f99ce10531266254f181e8f53640bd82b4c/rust-lang/rust-project-goals' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49084324' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(127) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://unattributed.cc/note-taking-and-personal-knowledge-management' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Note-Taking and Personal Knowledge Management</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>笔记与个人知识管理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49084324' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 236</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49084324' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 90</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49084324'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Note-taking is more than just collecting words; it&#x27;s about personal knowledge management. The piece outlines practical methods for organizing notes, linking ideas, and designing retrieval-friendly systems to boost learning and creativity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>好的笔记不仅是记录信息，更是管理知识的体系。本文提供将笔记分解为可复用的原子笔记、建立双向链接与定期回顾的做法，帮助提高检索与创新效率。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(128) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://os8088.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Os8088: A powerful Mac-like OS for the IBM XT, 286, 386</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Os8088：为 IBM XT/286/386 提供的强大 Mac 风格操作系统</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49226923' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 235</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49226923' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 151</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49226923'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Os8088 shows a Mac-like experience on IBM XT/286/386 compatible hardware, blending nostalgic UI with modern features. The project demonstrates retro computing revival and how modern UI ideas can map onto older machines. For enthusiasts, it highlights how UI design can transcend hardware generations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Os8088 在 IBM XT/286/386 兼容机上提供类似 Mac 的界面与体验，展示复古硬件也能承载现代设计理念。该项目体现了复古计算的复兴，以及将现代 UI 粹汇映射到旧硬件的可能性。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49223845' data-tags='Energy,Business,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49223845' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(129) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://newrepublic.com/post/214111/amazon-data-center-biggest-pollution-source-entire-country' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Amazon Is Creating the Biggest Pollution Source in the Country</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>亚马逊正成为全国最大的污染源</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49223845' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 235</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49223845' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 151</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49223845'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that Amazon&#x27;s growing cloud footprint is reshaping the nation&#x27;s pollution profile, with data centers drawing power from regional grids that rely heavily on fossil fuels. The scale of AWS infrastructure makes corporate cloud emissions a material climate risk, prompting calls for stronger clean-energy commitments and policy scrutiny.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章指出，亚马逊不断扩张的云计算基础设施正在重塑全国的污染格局，数据中心对电力的需求大多来自以化石燃料为主的区域电网。云端巨头的规模意味着其排放成为重要的气候风险，这也促使人们对更严格的清洁能源承诺和政策监管提出诉求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.newrepublic.com/8f7acb5e8746b79f60cadd801a4b9b90be7df0e6.jpeg?w=1200&amp;h=630&amp;crop=faces&amp;fit=crop&amp;fm=jpg' data-full='https://images.newrepublic.com/8f7acb5e8746b79f60cadd801a4b9b90be7df0e6.jpeg?w=1200&amp;h=630&amp;crop=faces&amp;fit=crop&amp;fm=jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49232110' data-tags='Science,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49232110' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(130) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.techeblog.com/tom-stanton-supersonic-trebuchet/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tom Stanton&#x27;s supersonic trebuchet breaks sound barrier with gravity alone</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>汤姆·斯坦顿的超音速投石机仅靠重力突破声障</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49232110' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 234</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49232110' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 74</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49232110'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tom Stanton&#x27;s gravity-powered trebuchet achieves supersonic release speeds, beating the sound barrier using only gravitational potential energy. It&#x27;s a clever demonstration of physics and energy transfer at macro scales.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>汤姆·斯坦顿的重力驱动投石机在仅靠重力势能的情况下实现超音速发射，突破音速。这个演示展示了宏观尺度的能量储存与动量传递的物理原理。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.techeblog.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/09001255/tom-stanton-supersonic-trebuchet.jpg' data-full='https://images.techeblog.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/09001255/tom-stanton-supersonic-trebuchet.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49230477' data-tags='Privacy,Security,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49230477' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(131) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theatlantic.com/technology/2026/05/ai-wearable-surveillance-countermeasures/687203/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Everything you do is being recorded</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你所做的一切都在被记录</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49230477' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 232</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49230477' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 183</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49230477'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Examines pervasive AI-powered surveillance and data collection in modern life, and discusses countermeasures, privacy preserving techniques, and design considerations for mitigating tracking. The piece warns about the trade-offs between convenience and privacy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨当今生活中被 AI 驱动的广泛监控与数据收集，并讨论应对措施、隐私保护技术，以及降低追踪风险的设计要点。文中强调方便性与隐私之间的权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.theatlantic.com/thumbor/a_6tjTSlGfhmNqMHD5cosV_-kVQ=/0x43:2000x1085/1200x625/media/img/mt/2026/05/2026_05_15_Recording_arms_race_1/original.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.theatlantic.com/thumbor/a_6tjTSlGfhmNqMHD5cosV_-kVQ=/0x43:2000x1085/1200x625/media/img/mt/2026/05/2026_05_15_Recording_arms_race_1/original.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49099336' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49099336' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(132) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.science.org/content/article/odd-shapes-hidden-dense-amazon-rainforest-reveal-sprawling-ancient-civilization' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Amazonian civilization had estimated 3M people in 3% of forest area</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>亚马逊文明在占森林3%面积的区域内估计拥有约300万人口</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49099336' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 230</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49099336' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 171</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49099336'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New research estimates that a dense Amazonian civilization may have housed about 3 million people within just 3% of the forest area, challenging assumptions about pre-Columbian populations. The study leverages lidar and archaeological data to reveal urban-scale networks and sophisticated land use, suggesting a more complex human footprint in the region than previously believed.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新研究估计，亚马逊文明在森林仅占3%的区域内可能居住有约300万人，挑战了对前哥伦布时期人口的认知。通过激光雷达与考古数据，揭示了城镇规模的网络和复杂的土地利用，显示该区域的人类足迹比以往想象的要复杂。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49198464' data-tags='Hardware,Startups'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49198464' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(133) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://provenmetal.com' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Launch HN: ProvenMetal (YC S26) delivers circuit boards in days instead of weeks</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN: ProvenMetal (YC S26) 在几天内交付电路板</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49198464' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 229</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49198464' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 155</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49198464'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ProvenMetal, YC S26 alum, delivers circuit boards in days instead of weeks, speeding hardware prototyping. The service could shorten iteration cycles for hardware startups and enable faster time-to-market.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ProvenMetal（YC S26）在几天内交付电路板，显著加速硬件原型开发。该服务有望缩短硬件初创企业的迭代周期并提升上市速度。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://provenmetal.com/opengraph-image.png?opengraph-image.1ijtenr-ahy8x.png?dpl=dpl_7PsCFD8HpVYXuB2f3Maqj7oGShJJ' data-full='https://provenmetal.com/opengraph-image.png?opengraph-image.1ijtenr-ahy8x.png?dpl=dpl_7PsCFD8HpVYXuB2f3Maqj7oGShJJ' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49184216' data-tags='Web,DevOps,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49184216' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(134) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://weli.dev/blog/the-valley-of-webhooks/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Valley of Webhooks</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Webhook 的谷地</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49184216' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 227</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49184216' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 94</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49184216'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Examines the complexities and failure modes of webhooks in modern app ecosystems, offering patterns to mitigate latency, retries, security, and reliability in event-driven systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨现代应用生态中 Webhook 的复杂性与潜在失效点，提出降低延迟、处理重试、增强安全性与提升可靠性的设计模式。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49148128' data-tags='Security,Hardware,Privacy,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49148128' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(135) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://linuxiac.com/eu-age-verification-project-mandates-hardware-bound-attestation/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>EU Age Verification Project Mandates Hardware-Bound Attestation</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>欧盟年龄验证计划强制硬件绑定证明</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49148128' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 226</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49148128' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 189</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49148128'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The EU&#x27;s age-verification initiative will require hardware-bound attestations to prove user age, tying age checks to trusted execution environments or secure hardware. The move raises privacy and interoperability questions for browsers and device makers, while aiming to curb underage access to regulated services.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧盟的年龄验证计划将要求硬件绑定的证明来核验用户年龄，这意味着设备需要在可信执行环境中产生可验证的 attestations。此举引发隐私、互操作性和设备厂商合规成本方面的担忧，同时旨在降低未成年人对受监管服务的访问。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://linuxiac.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/eu-digital-identity.jpg' data-full='https://linuxiac.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/eu-digital-identity.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49154228' data-tags='AI,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49154228' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(136) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/lyogavin/airllm' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AirLLM 70B inference with single 4GB GPU</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>单张 4GB 显卡即可推理 AirLLM 70B</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49154228' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 225</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49154228' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 81</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49154228'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AirLLM&#x27;s 70B parameter model can run inference on a single 4GB GPU thanks to compression techniques and efficient runtime tricks. This showcases how accessible large-language models are becoming for edge devices and consumer hardware, though trade-offs in speed and quality remain.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AirLLM 的 70B 模型能够在单张 4GB 显卡上进行推理，展示了将大型语言模型带到边缘设备的可行性，但在速度与质量方面仍存在取舍。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/de58ad2c833144078c33247ac8e4fe1609ac4d55f241484fa161c5f003f674e9/lyogavin/airllm' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/de58ad2c833144078c33247ac8e4fe1609ac4d55f241484fa161c5f003f674e9/lyogavin/airllm' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49172834' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49172834' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(137) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://textslashplain.com/2026/08/04/security-is-hard-yall/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Web Security is Too Hard</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>网页安全真的太难了</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49172834' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 224</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49172834' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 117</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49172834'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A concise take emphasizes that modern web security is a balancing act between usability, performance, and protection, with misconfigurations and vendor complexity driving risk. The post argues for pragmatic defaults, better abstraction, and focusing on repeatable practices rather than chasing perfect security.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一种简明观点，强调现代网页安全在易用性、性能和保护之间需要平衡，配置错误与厂商生态的复杂性放大了风险。主张采用务实的默认设置、改进抽象，并将重点放在可重复的安全实践上，而非追求完美防护。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://textslashplain.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/image-41.png' data-full='https://textslashplain.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/image-41.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49220350' data-tags='Energy,Business,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49220350' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(138) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nytimes.com/2026/08/08/climate/amazon-data-center-texas-pollution.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>New Amazon Data Center Is Set to Have the Most Polluting Power Plant in the U.S.</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>新建的亚马逊数据中心将由美国污染最严重的发电厂供电</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49220350' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 223</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49220350' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 301</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49220350'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The new Texas facility is powered by a plant described as the most polluting in the U.S., highlighting the energy choices behind hyperscale computing. The arrangement underscores the tension between corporate climate pledges and real-world energy supply, potentially drawing regulatory scrutiny.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>报道称这座德州新数据中心将由美国污染最严重的发电厂供电，凸显超大规模云计算在能源选择上的矛盾。此举揭示企业减排承诺与现实能源供应之间的张力，可能引发监管关注。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49135457' data-tags='Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49135457' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(139) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://carlkolon.com/reading/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Carl&#x27;s Required Reading</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Carl 的必读书单</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49135457' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 221</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49135457' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 30</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49135457'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Carl shares a personal, hand-picked reading list that shapes his approach to software, systems, and critical thinking. The selections blend classical and modern perspectives, offering practical takeaways for developers aiming to level up.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>卡尔分享了一份个人精心挑选的读书清单，帮助塑造他在软件、系统与批判性思维方面的理解。清单将经典与现代观点结合，为希望提升的开发者提供可落地的建议。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://carlkolon.com/assets/media/carl_reading.webp' data-full='https://carlkolon.com/assets/media/carl_reading.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49156683' data-tags='Career,AI,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49156683'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A forum thread aggregating current software and tech openings as of August 2026, highlighting remote roles, AI/ML, backend, and cloud-focused positions. It serves as a snapshot of hiring demand and market signals for engineers and developers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个聚合2026年8月在招的技术职位的讨论串，强调远程岗位、AI/ML、后端和云计算相关职位。提供了工程师招聘需求与市场信号的快照。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49193173' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49193173' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(141) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/Universal-Debloater-Alliance/universal-android-debloater-next-generation/issues/1426' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>On non-rooted Android 17, ADB uninstall of system apps fails</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在非 root 的 Android 17 上，ADB 卸载系统应用失败</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49193173' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 220</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49193173' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 185</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49193173'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>On non-rooted Android 17 devices, ADB cannot uninstall system apps. The limitation highlights how device integrity and built-in protections constrain user customization, affecting bloatware removal and device ownership.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在未获得 root 的 Android 17 设备上，使用 ADB 卸载系统应用会失败。这体现了设备安全性对自定义能力的限制，去除预装应用等需求将受阻，除非使用 root 或厂商工具。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/e9822c9748d6213d904ce16956b43dd8a64127341622fc6161fe57b961b16418/Universal-Debloater-Alliance/universal-android-debloater-next-generation/issues/1426' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/e9822c9748d6213d904ce16956b43dd8a64127341622fc6161fe57b961b16418/Universal-Debloater-Alliance/universal-android-debloater-next-generation/issues/1426' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49164184'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Windows users may encounter an installation flow that feels surreal and counterintuitive. The anecdote highlights quirks in software delivery on Windows and why UX and security should keep pace with platform complexity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作为 Windows 用户，遇到的安装流程显得超现实且难以理解。这一现象暴露了 Windows 生态中的软件分发在用户体验与安全方面的挑战。</p>

</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49203105' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(144) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.troyhunt.com/welcoming-the-nepalese-government-to-have-i-been-pwned/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Welcoming the Nepalese Government to Have I Been Pwned</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>欢迎尼泊尔政府加入Have I Been Pwned</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49203105' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 214</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49203105' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 35</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49203105'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Nepalese government joins Have I Been Pwned, expanding the reach of data-breach alerts to government accounts and related citizen services. This broadens public cybersecurity visibility and could improve risk awareness and incident response across agencies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>尼泊尔政府加入 Have I Been Pwned，扩大政府账户及相关公民服务的数据泄露警报覆盖范围，有望提升公民与政府的网络安全可见性、风险意识与响应能力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.ghost.io/c/fb/33/fb3391dc-723d-4e74-b95a-d641b5feb38e/content/images/2026/07/Nepal-Flag.png' data-full='https://storage.ghost.io/c/fb/33/fb3391dc-723d-4e74-b95a-d641b5feb38e/content/images/2026/07/Nepal-Flag.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49211438' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(145) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=UufMqwyO7pY' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Iceberg Collapses and Flips over in Ilulissat, Greenland (July 25, 2026) [video]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>格陵兰伊鲁利萨特冰山坍塌翻转（2026年7月25日）[视频]</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49211438' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 212</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49211438' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 61</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49211438'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A massive iceberg collapsed and flipped near Ilulissat, Greenland, captured in a dramatic video. The event underscores the increasing calving activity in a warming climate and its potential effects on sea level and local ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>格陵兰伊卢利萨特附近一座庞大冰山坍塌并翻转，画面震撼。此类快速崩解事件反映出在全球变暖背景下的冰山活动增加，对海平面和沿岸生态可能产生影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49232253' data-tags='Health,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49232253' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(146) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://theconversation.com/taxi-drivers-rarely-die-of-alzheimers-how-complex-mental-maps-and-spatial-reasoning-protect-your-brain-286650' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Taxi drivers rarely die of Alzheimer&#x27;s</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>出租车司机罹患阿尔茨海默病的风险较低</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49232253' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 210</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49232253' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 159</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49232253'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A study indicates taxi drivers&#x27; rich, complex spatial navigation and mental maps may protect against Alzheimer&#x27;s, highlighting how environmental cognitive training can influence brain resilience.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究显示，出租车司机复杂的空间导航和地图记忆可能提升大脑对阿尔茨海默病的抵抗力，强调环境中的认知训练对脑部韧性的潜在影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.theconversation.com/files/752403/original/file-20260805-60-8x6hvk.jpg?ixlib=rb-4.1.1&amp;rect=0%2C161%2C2121%2C1060&amp;q=45&amp;auto=format&amp;w=1356&amp;h=668&amp;fit=crop' data-full='https://images.theconversation.com/files/752403/original/file-20260805-60-8x6hvk.jpg?ixlib=rb-4.1.1&amp;rect=0%2C161%2C2121%2C1060&amp;q=45&amp;auto=format&amp;w=1356&amp;h=668&amp;fit=crop' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49208393' data-tags='Security,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49208393' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(147) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.cloudflare.com/kitesurf/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Kitesurf: Agent-first browser that runs in V8 isolates</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Kitesurf：在 V8 isolation 中运行的代理优先浏览器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49208393' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 209</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49208393' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 59</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49208393'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Kitesurf experiments with an agent-first browser running in V8 isolates to maximize isolation and security. The model could influence how browsers or edge runtimes partition work and enforce sandboxed computation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Kitesurf 采用以代理为核心的浏览器设计，将代码运行在 V8 isolates 上，以增强隔离与安全性。此思路可能影响浏览器和边缘计算环境的分区与沙箱化实现。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.cloudflare.com/_emdash/api/media/file/01KZBWYMKT77FDVB07PHA3R5WM.png' data-full='https://blog.cloudflare.com/_emdash/api/media/file/01KZBWYMKT77FDVB07PHA3R5WM.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49202716' data-tags='Science,Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49202716' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(148) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.sciencedaily.com/releases/2026/08/260803080917.htm' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Bioengineered chewing gum may offer a way to fight HPV and other microbes</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>生物工程口香糖可能帮助对抗 HPV 等微生物</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49202716' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 208</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49202716' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 61</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49202716'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers have developed bioengineered chewing gum that can deliver antimicrobial compounds to the mouth, potentially targeting HPV and other microbes. If scalable, this could offer a convenient, noninvasive delivery method to complement vaccines or therapies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员开发的生物工程口香糖可在口腔释放抗微生物成分，可能对 HPV 等微生物起到抑制作用。若规模化应用，这种便捷、非侵入式的给药方式或成为疫苗或治疗方案的有力补充。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.sciencedaily.com/images/1920/young-woman-biting-chewing-gum.webp' data-full='https://www.sciencedaily.com/images/1920/young-woman-biting-chewing-gum.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49150561' data-tags='Politics,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49150561' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(149) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.modelrepublic.org/articles/the-reporters-at-this-news-site-are-ai-bots.-openai%E2%80%99s-super-pac-appears-to-be-using-it-to-advance-its-political-agenda' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenAI&#x27;s super PAC is funding AI-generated news site attacking industry critics</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI的超级政治行动委员会资助AI生成的新闻站点攻击行业批评者</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49150561' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 206</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49150561' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 106</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49150561'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A political action committee linked to OpenAI funds an AI-generated news site that targets industry critics, raising concerns about AI-driven political narratives and media manipulation. The arrangement underscores the evolving blend of political influence, funding transparency, and machine-generated content.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>与OpenAI相关的超级政治行动委员会资助了一家以AI生成内容为主的新闻站点，用以攻击行业批评者。这暴露了AI驱动的舆论影响、媒体素养与政治动员之间的新矛盾，以及透明度的缺口。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://framerusercontent.com/images/654omgMZMMZgaGluHRcTrUOXN90.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630' data-full='https://framerusercontent.com/images/654omgMZMMZgaGluHRcTrUOXN90.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49183665' data-tags='Design,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49183665' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(150) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sightlessscribbles.com/disability-dongle/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The &quot;Disability Dongle&quot;: Why Silicon Valley Hates Me and You</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>《Disability Dongle》：硅谷为何讨厌我和你</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49183665' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 204</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49183665' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 198</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49183665'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative critique of Silicon Valley’s relationship with disability tech, arguing that neglecting accessibility stifles innovation and excludes a large user base. The piece calls for more inclusive design and a rethink of how products are built from the ground up. It reframes disability tech as essential, not a side concern.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对硅谷与残障科技关系的挑衅性批评，认为忽视无障碍设计会抑制创新并让大量潜在用户被排除在外。呼吁从根本上推动更包容的设计与开发方式，将残障科技提升为核心要素，而非边缘事物。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49231809' data-tags='Web,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49231809' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(153) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.w3.org/Provider/Style/URI' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cool URIs Don&#x27;t Change (1998)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>稳定的 URI 不会改变（1998）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49231809' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 202</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49231809' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 48</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49231809'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A foundational memo urging that URIs be stable, permanent identifiers for resources. It argues against design patterns that rely on mutable URLs, explaining how changing links breaks navigation, indexing, and caching—lessons that still influence modern web architecture.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这份1998年的基石性备忘录强调 URI 应保持稳定、永久指向资源，反对依赖可变链接的设计模式。链接变动会破坏导航、搜索索引与缓存，这些经验至今仍深刻影响现代 Web 架构。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.w3.org/Icons/WWW/w3c_home.gif' data-full='https://www.w3.org/Icons/WWW/w3c_home.gif' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49189234' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(154) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://chipsandcheese.com/p/nvidias-vera-whitepaper-has-a-thread' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nvidia’s Vera Whitepaper Has a Thread Loose</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>NVIDIA 的 Vera 白皮书存在一个线程问题</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49189234' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 201</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49189234' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 46</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49189234'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Nvidia&#x27;s Vera whitepaper is critiqued for a threading issue that could undermine its concurrency claims. The note highlights how small threading mistakes can ripple into performance and security concerns in ambitious hardware/software proposals.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 Nvidia Vera 白皮书中的线程实现问题进行评述，指出这类小错可能影响并发设计的安全性与性能表现。文章强调在雄心勃勃的硬件/软件方案中，严谨的线程实现有多么关键。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!o1nY!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F4bf7fee6-a173-429e-87e9-7c1fba499e5a_1895x1493.png' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!o1nY!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F4bf7fee6-a173-429e-87e9-7c1fba499e5a_1895x1493.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49223079' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(155) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://hackaday.com/2026/08/08/want-energy-efficiency-dude-youre-getting-a-dell/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Can Intel finally beat ARM on performance per Watt?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>英特尔能否最终在每瓦性能上超越 ARM？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49223079' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 201</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49223079' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 165</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49223079'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New benchmarks evaluate whether Intel can finally beat ARM on performance-per-watt, given architectural and process improvements. The debate matters for data centers and mobile devices as power efficiency continues to drive processor choices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新基准评估在面对架构与工艺改进的情况下，英特尔是否最终能在单位功耗性能上超越 ARM。这一话题对数据中心与移动设备的处理器选型具有重要影响，因为功耗效率持续推动车型选择。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://hackaday.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/XPS-v-Neo-Jeff-feat.jpg' data-full='https://hackaday.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/XPS-v-Neo-Jeff-feat.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49221939'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A playful reminder that smartphone use during bathroom breaks can fragment attention and waste time. It suggests taking a true break by setting the device aside and using the moment for rest or reflection.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这条看似轻松的提醒指出，在厕所使用手机会分散注意力、影响姿势并浪费时间。建议把手机放下，给自己一个真正的休息时刻，以提升专注和效率。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://nate.spot/static/og-image.png' data-full='https://nate.spot/static/og-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49183750' data-tags='Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49183750' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(157) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cnelecar.com/blog/oracle-always-free-arm-limits-cut-2026/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Oracle cut its Always Free ARM limits to 2 OCPU / 12GB, enforced Aug 18</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Oracle 将 Always Free ARM 限制降至 2 OCPU / 12GB，8月18日生效</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49183750' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 198</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49183750' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 128</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49183750'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Oracle is reducing its Always Free ARM tier to 2 OCPU and 12GB RAM, with enforcement beginning August 18. The tighter free tier tightens access for hobbyists and startups, nudging users toward paid plans or other clouds. This move signals how cloud providers are recalibrating free tiers to curb abuse while keeping learning within reach.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Oracle 将 Always Free ARM 免费额度降至 2 OCPU/12GB，8 月 18 日起执行。这一调整缩窄了对业余开发者和初创公司的免费资源，促使用户转向付费计划或考虑其他云服务。这一举动反映出云服务商在限制滥用的同时，仍希望保持学习门槛的可及性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.cnelecar.com/blog/oracle-always-free-arm-limits-cut-2026/oracle-always-free-arm-vm-cut-in-half.png' data-full='https://www.cnelecar.com/blog/oracle-always-free-arm-limits-cut-2026/oracle-always-free-arm-vm-cut-in-half.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(158) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pudding.cool/2026/06/mow/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why some people mow a lawn better than others</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为什么有些人修剪草坪比其他人好</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49172550' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 198</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49172550' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 189</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49172550'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A data-driven look at lawn mowing reveals how technique, timing, and mower settings impact cut quality and efficiency. The piece uses measurement to illustrate skill differences and offers practical tips for better mowing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一篇数据驱动的观察，揭示修剪草坪的技巧、时机与草坪机设置如何影响修剪质量与效率。通过量化比较，呈现不同人之间的技巧差异，并给出提升修剪效果的实用建议。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49213029' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 198</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49213029' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 192</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49213029'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI outlines how to respond to evolving cyber capabilities, emphasizing collaboration with policymakers and safety measures to mitigate risks.</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49221711' data-tags='Programming,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49221711' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(160) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.getutm.app/2026/introducing-triton-directx-11-driver-for-qemu/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Triton: DirectX 11 Driver for QEMU</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Triton：为 QEMU 提供 DirectX 11 驱动</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49221711' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 195</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49221711' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 42</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49221711'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Triton brings DirectX 11 support to QEMU, enabling Windows-era GPU acceleration in virtualized guests. This lowers the barrier for running graphically intensive apps in virtualization, potentially affecting dev/test workflows and VM graphics performance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Triton 将 DirectX 11 支持带入 QEMU，使虚拟机来宾能够实现图形加速。这降低了在虚拟化环境中运行图形密集应用的门槛，可能影响开发与测试流程及虚拟机的图形性能。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.getutm.app/assets/images/logo.png' data-full='https://blog.getutm.app/assets/images/logo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49200439' data-tags='Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49200439' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(161) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mastodon.gamedev.place/@rygorous/117047697255584965' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>My phone detects going on a run as “someone snatching my phone and running off”</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我的手机把跑步误判为“有人抢走我的手机并跑开”</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49200439' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 193</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49200439' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 338</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49200439'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A personal experience shows smartphone motion sensors misclassifying a run as theft, underscoring the fragility of automated activity detection and the noise in sensor data.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>个人经历显示智能手机的运动传感器会把跑步误判为盗窃，凸显自动化活动检测的脆弱性以及传感数据的噪声问题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://mastodon.gamedev.place/packs/assets/logo-DXQkHAe5.svg' data-full='https://mastodon.gamedev.place/packs/assets/logo-DXQkHAe5.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(162) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.statsignificant.com/p/how-hollywood-stopped-making-movies' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How Hollywood stopped making movies in Hollywood</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>好莱坞已不再在好莱坞拍电影</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49082937' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 193</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49082937' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 239</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49082937'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Hollywood stopped making movies in Hollywood. The piece traces how production moved away from Hollywood hubs due to costs and incentives, and discusses the economic and cultural consequences for the local industry and communities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>好莱坞不再在本土拍摄电影，行业制作基地的转移改变了传统生产格局。文章分析成本、激励政策和全球化如何推动这场迁移，以及对本地经济和行业生态的影响。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49183850' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(165) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.qwencloud.com/models/qwen-image-3.0-pro' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Qwen Image 3.0 Pro</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Qwen Image 3.0 Pro 模型发布</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49183850' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 191</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49183850' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 53</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49183850'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Qwen Image 3.0 Pro advances image generation capabilities with higher fidelity, faster inference, and improved safety controls, expanding options for developers to deploy AI on-device or in the cloud. The upgrade heightens competition in the image-model space and pressures smaller models to keep pace. Responsible licensing and guardrails will matter as adoption grows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Qwen Image 3.0 Pro 在图像生成方面实现了更高保真度、推理速度提升和更完善的安全控制，为开发者在设备端或云端部署 AI 提供更多选项。升级将进一步加剧图像模型领域的竞争，也促使更小的模型跟进。随着应用广泛，许可与治理同样重要。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://img.alicdn.com/imgextra/i3/O1CN01eBklSt1jT2S77Xekm_!!6000000004548-2-tps-224-224.png' data-full='https://img.alicdn.com/imgextra/i3/O1CN01eBklSt1jT2S77Xekm_!!6000000004548-2-tps-224-224.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(166) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.niemanlab.org/2026/08/americas-largest-newspaper-chain-usa-today-co-partners-with-palantir-to-analyze-audience-data/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>USA Today Co., partners with Palantir to analyze audience data</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>USA Today 与 Palantir 合作分析受众数据</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49210589' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 191</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49210589' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 80</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49210589'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>USA Today teams with Palantir to analyze audience data, aiming to tailor content and advertising based on deeper insights. The partnership signals media outlets’ continued push into enterprise analytics to monetize digital readership while raising privacy questions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>USA Today 与 Palantir 合作分析受众数据，意在基于深度洞察定制内容与广告。此举体现媒体在数字读者变现方面加码数据分析能力，同时也引发隐私与数据治理的讨论。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49209572' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49209572' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(167) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.sciencealert.com/radical-study-suggests-life-on-earth-arose-from-non-living-matter-twice' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Radical Study Suggests Life on Earth Arose Twice</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>极端研究表明地球的生命可能起源两次</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49209572' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 191</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49209572' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 114</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49209572'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A radical study suggests life on Earth may have originated more than once, challenging singular-origin narratives. If corroborated, this could reshape our understanding of early biochemistry, planetary conditions, and the likelihood of life elsewhere.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一项激进研究提出地球生命可能起源不止一次，这一结论若成立，将改变我们对地球早期生物化学与生命普遍性可能性的理解。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.sciencealert.com/images/2026/08/two-blue-colorful-bubbles-on-a-black-background.jpg' data-full='https://www.sciencealert.com/images/2026/08/two-blue-colorful-bubbles-on-a-black-background.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49228458'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post notes that the original URL for a 2011 prediction will disappear in 11 years, underscoring link rot risk and the need for durable archiving of long-term forecasts. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该条目指出一项2011年的预测原始链接将在11年后不可用，凸显链接失效问题以及对长期预测进行可持续归档的必要性。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49213097' data-tags='Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(171) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.lse.ac.uk/research/research-for-the-world/economics/tax-cuts-for-the-wealthy-only-benefit-the-rich-debunking-trickle-down-economics' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tax cuts for the wealthy only benefit the rich (2023)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>对富人的减税只会让富人受益（2023）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49213097' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 188</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49213097' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 206</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49213097'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 2023 assessment from the London School of Economics argues that tax cuts for the wealthy do little for broad growth and primarily benefit the rich. The piece challenges trickle-down claims and highlights inequality implications.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>伦敦经济学院2023年的评估称，针对富人的减税对总体经济增长帮助有限，主要让富人受益。该观点对“滴漏效应”理论提出质疑，并强调不平等的后果。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.lse.ac.uk/all-images/rftw/davidhope-jan23-facebook1200x630-xfcb73d4c.x21ff7b18.jpg' data-full='https://www.lse.ac.uk/all-images/rftw/davidhope-jan23-facebook1200x630-xfcb73d4c.x21ff7b18.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(173) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.sdss.org/black-hole-mapper-release-20/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>An all-sky map of half a million supermassive black holes</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>覆盖全天空的50万颗超大质量黑洞分布图</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49211921' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 186</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49211921' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 40</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49211921'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The SDSS Black Hole Mapper Release 2.0 presents an all-sky catalog of about 500,000 supermassive black holes, enabling statistical studies of AGN populations and galaxy evolution. The dataset helps cross-match SMBH demographics with host galaxies and cosmic structure.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>SDSS 的黑洞映射器在 Release 2.0 中公布覆盖全天空的约50万颗超大质量黑洞目录，便于对活跃星系核和星系演化进行大规模统计研究。该数据集方便将黑洞人口与宿主星系及宇宙结构进行对比分析。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.sdss.org/wp-content/uploads/2022/09/sdss-new-logo-72dpi.png' data-full='https://www.sdss.org/wp-content/uploads/2022/09/sdss-new-logo-72dpi.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49229174' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(174) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gukov.dev/math/2026/08/02/new-magic-hexagons.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>There Are Magic Hexagons of Every Order</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>存在着各种阶数的神奇六边形</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49229174' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 185</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49229174' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 31</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49229174'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This piece surveys the existence and construction of magic hexagons for all orders, extending classic results beyond small cases. It highlights patterns, symmetries, and potential open questions in combinatorial design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章探讨了神奇六边形在所有阶数下的存在性与构造，扩展了经典结果到更高阶数。内容聚焦于对称性、结构模式，以及组合设计中的未解问题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://gukov.dev/assets/svg/magic-hexagons/MagicHexagon-Order21-sum_zero.svg' data-full='https://gukov.dev/assets/svg/magic-hexagons/MagicHexagon-Order21-sum_zero.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
</div>
