<p class='hn-title'>(175) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://seg6.space/posts/center-div/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We finally learned to center a div, then browsers added sidebars</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们终于学会居中一个 div，结果浏览器又加入了侧边栏</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49176055' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 184</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49176055' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 162</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49176055'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Web developers celebrated finally centering a div, only for browsers to introduce sidebars and new layout features that reshape design rules.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>开发者终于掌握了让 div 居中的技巧，紧接着浏览器又引入侧边栏等新特性，重新改变了布局规则。文章强调了 CSS 与浏览器发展过程中的趣味性与演进性。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49220392' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(176) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theverge.com/tech/976880/microsoft-edge-extensions-ad-blockers-mv2-mv3' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Microsoft Edge is about to lock out older ad blockers, just like Chrome did</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Microsoft Edge 将像 Chrome 一样封锁旧版广告拦截插件</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49220392' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 183</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49220392' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 181</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49220392'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Edge will drop support for MV2 extensions, forcing users to MV3 and narrowing the capabilities of traditional ad blockers. The move mirrors Chrome’s policy and could degrade privacy protections while reshaping the ad-tech ecosystem.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Edge 将取消对 MV2 版扩展的支持，迫使用户和开发者改用 MV3，从而削弱传统广告拦截插件的能力。这与 Chrome 的政策类似，可能降低隐私保护效果并改变广告生态。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/chorus/uploads/chorus_asset/file/24385268/STK148_Microsoft_Edge_1.jpg?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0%2C10.732984293194%2C100%2C78.534031413613&amp;w=1200' data-full='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/chorus/uploads/chorus_asset/file/24385268/STK148_Microsoft_Edge_1.jpg?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0%2C10.732984293194%2C100%2C78.534031413613&amp;w=1200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49180407'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A local Hacker News meetup in Helsinki brings together developers and enthusiasts for talks and networking. It’s a chance to hear regional projects, share learnings, and strengthen the local tech community.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>赫尔辛基的 Hacker News 聚会聚集本地开发者与爱好者，进行主题演讲和社交网络交流。为了解地区项目、分享经验、加强本地技术社区提供了机会。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(178) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://changelog.complete.org/archives/44456-celebrating-45-years-of-kermit-with-the-first-new-c-kermit-release-in-15-years-and-working-with-a-decades-old-c-codebase' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Celebrating 45 Years of Kermit with the First New C-Kermit Release in 15 Years</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>庆祝 Kermit 45 年：15 年来首次发布 C-Kermit 的新版本</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49158474' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 180</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49158474' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 51</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49158474'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Marks 45 years of Kermit with the first new C-Kermit release in 15 years, updating a decades-old file transfer tool with modern fixes and compatibility tweaks. It shows how long-lived open-source codebases can stay relevant when actively maintained, even as legacy constraints accumulate.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该版本是对 C-Kermit 的首次新发布，经过 15 年久仍在维护，加入现代修复与兼容性更新。体现了久远开源代码在持续维护下仍能保持相关性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(179) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ssh.place' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: ssh ssh.place</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：ssh.place</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49149805' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 180</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49149805' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 110</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49149805'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ssh.place offers a browser-based SSH experience with a simple URL-driven interface, enabling quick remote shells without a local client. It illustrates how lightweight, on-demand SSH can streamline demos, ephemeral environments, and remote debugging.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ssh.place 提供浏览器内的 SSH 体验，采用简单的 URL 驱动界面，实现快速远程 shell，省去本地客户端的需求。它展示了轻量化按需 SSH 在演示、临时环境与远程排错中的潜在价值。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>LinkedIn 动态屏蔽插件</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49223475' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 177</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49223475' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 106</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49223475'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A browser extension that blocks LinkedIn’s feed to curb endless scrolling and boost productivity. It illustrates how users customize their social networks and stay focused, while small tools like this gain traction in the browser ecosystem.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一个浏览器扩展，封锁 LinkedIn 的动态流，帮助用户降低刷屏诱惑、提升专注和生产力。它体现了用户对社交网络的自定义控制，以及在浏览器生态中日益活跃的生产力工具。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49176697' data-tags='Privacy,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49176697' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(182) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mysk.blog/2026/08/04/webkit-proxy-icloud-private-relay-ip-leak/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>IP and DNS Leaks in WebKit Affecting Proxy Browsers and iCloud Private Relay</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>WebKit 的 IP 与 DNS 泄漏影响代理浏览器与 iCloud Private Relay</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49176697' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 176</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49176697' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 33</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49176697'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A WebKit-based flaw can leak IP and DNS data, even when using proxy browsers and iCloud Private Relay. The leak undermines privacy and can expose user traffic to network observers. Mitigation requires patches and careful configuration updates.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>WebKit 基于的实现中存在 IP 与 DNS 泄漏，即使使用代理浏览器或 iCloud Private Relay 也可能暴露用户真实地址。这类漏洞削弱了隐私保护，可能让用户流量被网络观察者看到。需要应用修补与谨慎的配置调整来降低风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://mysk.blog/og-image.png' data-full='https://mysk.blog/og-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(183) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://textlog.cc/about' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: textlog – A quiet, text-only microblogging platform, open-source, no JS</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：textlog——一个文本为主、无前端依赖的开源微博客</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49208458' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 176</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49208458' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 72</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49208458'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Textlog offers a quiet, text-only microblogging experience with no JavaScript, emphasizing simplicity and privacy. The project shows how a minimal, open-source platform can support social interaction without web app complexity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>textlog 提供极简的文字微博客体验，无需 JavaScript，强调隐私与带宽效率。该开源项目展示了在保持轻量的前提下实现社交功能的可能性。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49200390' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(184) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nbcnews.com/business/media/federal-communications-commission-scraps-limit-broadcast-tv-ownership-rcna587641' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Federal Communications Commission scraps limit on broadcast TV ownership</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>联邦通信委员会取消广播电视所有权限额</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49200390' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 175</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49200390' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 112</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49200390'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The FCC scrap(s) the cap on broadcast TV station ownership, potentially increasing consolidation in local media markets and affecting competition and diversity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>联邦通信委员会取消广播电视所有权的上限，可能加剧本地市场的集中度，并对竞争与媒体多样性产生影响。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49210077' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(185) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.fastcompany.com/91576086/ai-psychosis-is-the-new-leadership-blind-spot-ai-leadership-blind-spots' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI psychosis is the new leadership blind spot</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 病态化成领导力的新盲点</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49210077' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 174</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49210077' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 106</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49210077'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Fast Company piece argues AI-driven decision making exposes new leadership blind spots, including overreliance on automation and misinterpretation of model outputs. It calls for governance, guardrails, and human-in-the-loop oversight.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《Fast Company》指出 AI 驱动的决策带来新的领导力盲点，如对自动化的过度依赖与对模型输出的误解。文章呼吁建立治理框架、边界和人工介入的监督机制。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49225139' data-tags='Open Source,Space,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49225139' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(186) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://eclipsefan.org/?v=2&amp;t=max&amp;layers=eclipse%2Cbesselian%2Cumbra-live%2Cshadow-3d%2Ccloud-projection%2Cosm&amp;lat=43.4623&amp;lon=-3.8099&amp;opacity=besselian%3A0.2%2Cumbra-live%3A0.2&amp;zoom=6&amp;palier=minute' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Open-source interactive map for the Aug 12 total solar eclipse</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用于8月12日日全食的开源交互式地图</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49225139' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 174</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49225139' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 55</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49225139'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An open-source interactive map provides a multi-layer visualization for the August 12 total solar eclipse, including eclipse geometry, umbra paths, live shadow, and cloud projections. The project helps skywatchers plan where and when to view the event with a community-driven tool. It also highlights how open-source geospatial tools can support large, time-sensitive astronomical events.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个开源的交互式地图提供8月12日日全食的多层可视化，包括日全食几何、影子路径、实时遮云等。该工具便于观测者据地理位置规划观测点和时间。它还凸显了开源地理信息工具在时效性强的天文事件中的应用潜力。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GNU Hurd 2026年第二季度更新</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49146183' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 171</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49146183' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 113</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49146183'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GNU Hurd releases updates for 2026 Q2, outlining progress, fixes, and remaining challenges in the kernel-free UNIX-like OS project. The notes reflect ongoing efforts to revive the project after decades of development.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GNU Hurd 2026年第二季度更新公布进展、修复与面临的挑战，概述该内核未完成的UNIX风格操作系统的持续努力。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49149326' data-tags='Show HN,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49149326' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(188) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/pg83/shitty' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Shitty – fast terminal. Memory-unsafe and faster than yours</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Shitty——快速的终端，内存不安全且比你用的更快</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49149326' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 171</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49149326' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 154</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49149326'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Shitty is a fast, memory-unsafe terminal aimed at squeezing maximum speed, trading safety for performance. It&#x27;s a provocative proof-of-concept that highlights what you can achieve by stripping away safety checks, while cautioning against using it in production.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Shitty 是一个追求极致速度、且不保证内存安全的快速终端。通过牺牲安全性来提升性能，这个概念验证展示了在剥离安全检查后能达到的极端性能，同时提示在生产环境中不要使用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/014a4584c70dc8578cafde95a956bbe29188913eb9014b6d8d4c16802b350426/pg83/shitty' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/014a4584c70dc8578cafde95a956bbe29188913eb9014b6d8d4c16802b350426/pg83/shitty' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49186720' data-tags='AI,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49186720' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(189) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.01395' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Sycophantic AI Decreases Prosocial Intentions and Promotes Dependence (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>拍马屁型人工智能会降低利他意愿并增加依赖感（2025）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49186720' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 168</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49186720' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 99</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49186720'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 2025 study shows that sycophantic AI—the kind that flatters users—reduces prosocial intentions and increases dependence on the AI. The finding raises concerns about the social and psychological impact of flattering assistants in everyday interactions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>2025 年的研究显示，拍马屁型人工智能会削弱人们的利他意愿并增加对 AI 的依赖。此发现引发对日常交互中过度奉承型 AI 可能带来的社会与心理影响的关注。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49173984' data-tags='Show HN,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49173984' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(190) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://deepgrove.ai/maple-preview' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Maple-Preview – Ternary 20B MoE running at 120 tok/s on a iPhone</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Maple-Preview 在 iPhone 上以 120 tok/s 运行的 20B MoE</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49173984' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 167</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49173984' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 51</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49173984'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Maple-Preview demonstrates on-device inference for a 20B MoE model running at 120 tokens per second on an iPhone. This showcases mobile AI capabilities and paves the way for private, latency-sensitive apps without cloud dependence.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Maple-Preview 展示了在 iPhone 上直接执行一个 20B MoE 模型，速率约为 120 token/s。此成就体现了移动端 AI 推理的潜力，并为无需云端依赖的低延迟应用铺平了道路。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49221864' data-tags='Open Source,Security,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49221864' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(191) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://social.treehouse.systems/@mgorny/117058483039362779' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Gentoo bugzilla closed due AI bot scraper overload</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Gentoo 的 Bugzilla 因 AI 爬虫过载而关闭</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49221864' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 166</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49221864' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 109</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49221864'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Gentoo’s Bugzilla was shut down after an overload of automated AI-powered scraping requests, illustrating how AI tooling can disrupt open-source maintenance. The incident could push maintainers to harden rate limits and rethink data-access policies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Gentoo 的 Bugzilla 因 AI 爬虫的泛滥请求而被关闭，揭示了 AI 工具对开源维护工作流的冲击。此事可能促使维护者加强限流、引入防护措施或重新审视数据访问策略。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://social.treehouse.systems/packs/assets/logo-DXQkHAe5.svg' data-full='https://social.treehouse.systems/packs/assets/logo-DXQkHAe5.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49160437' data-tags='Science,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49160437' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(192) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.mcgill.ca/oss/article/critical-thinking/dunning-kruger-effect-probably-not-real' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Dunning-Kruger effect may just be a data artefact (2020)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>邓宁-克鲁格效应可能只是数据伪影（2020）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49160437' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 165</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49160437' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 175</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49160437'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 2020 critique argues the Dunning-Kruger effect may arise from data artefacts and methodological biases rather than a universal cognitive bias. If true, conclusions about competence exaggeration depend on how data is gathered and interpreted; replicability is crucial.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>2020 年的一项批评指出，邓宁-克鲁格效应可能源自数据伪影与方法论偏差，而非普遍的人类认知偏差。若成立，关于能力高估的结论将取决于数据收集与解读方式，重复性研究至关重要。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.mcgill.ca/oss/files/oss/styles/wysiwyg_large/public/light-bulb-1042480_1920_1.jpeg?itok=nDOISjZp' data-full='https://www.mcgill.ca/oss/files/oss/styles/wysiwyg_large/public/light-bulb-1042480_1920_1.jpeg?itok=nDOISjZp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49119751' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(193) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://handwritten.blog/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: A Handwritten Blogging Platform</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：一个手写风格的博客平台</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49119751' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 165</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49119751' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 80</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49119751'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Handwritten Blogging Platform experiments with a handwriting-centric publishing experience, turning posts into handwritten-looking pages. It explores alternative UI approaches for writing and publishing on the web, inspiring creators who want a more personal touch.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个手写博客平台在写作与发布方面采用手写风格的界面，将帖子呈现为看起来像手写的页面。它探索在网络上进行创作与发布的替代交互方式，或为寻求个性化表达的创作者提供灵感。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49220313' data-tags='Space,Data,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49220313' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(194) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/gadgets/2026/08/europes-free-satellite-service-just-made-it-easier-to-track-wildfires/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Europe&#x27;s free satellite service just made it easier to track wildfires</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>欧洲的免费卫星服务让野火监测更易</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49220313' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 161</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49220313' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 14</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49220313'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>EU&#x27;s Copernicus data free service now enables easier wildfire tracking, improving early detection and situational awareness for authorities and communities. The freely accessible satellite imagery and analytics reduce response times and support better risk planning.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧盟的 Copernicus 免费卫星数据现在让野火监测更高效，提升了相关部门和公众的早期发现与态势感知能力。开放的数据和分析工具可缩短响应时间，帮助更好地进行风险管理。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/2026-08-03-00_00_2026-08-03-23_59_Sentinel-2_L2A_Wildfires-1-2560x1440.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/2026-08-03-00_00_2026-08-03-23_59_Sentinel-2_L2A_Wildfires-1-2560x1440.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49228663' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(196) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/jmarshall23/msword' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Microsoft Word for Windows 1.1a, Native X64 Port</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Windows 版 Word 1.1a 的原生 X64 端口</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49228663' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 158</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49228663' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 66</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49228663'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An open-source effort delivers a native x64 port of Windows Word 1.1a, reviving a historic word processor on modern hardware. It highlights the challenges and decisions involved in porting legacy software to 64-bit systems and modern toolchains.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在 GitHub 上出现了 Windows 版 Word 1.1a 的原生 X64 端口，旨在让这款历史悠久的文字处理程序在现代计算机上以原生性能运行。该工程揭示了对早期软件架构的研究兴趣，也为复刻遗留应用提供了性能参考。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/b64ef1191a7ddc3d34772721b10c75b50c3c1bb40f7d302574c3cca4f363fae4/jmarshall23/msword' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/b64ef1191a7ddc3d34772721b10c75b50c3c1bb40f7d302574c3cca4f363fae4/jmarshall23/msword' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49227365' data-tags='Health,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49227365' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(197) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://academic.oup.com/sleep/article/46/Supplement_1/A34/7181621' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Melatonin impairs morning cognition in healthy young adults (2023)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>褪黑激素会损害健康年轻人早晨认知（2023）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49227365' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 157</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49227365' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 171</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49227365'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 2023 study finds melatonin intake in healthy young adults impairs morning cognitive performance, challenging the belief that melatonin is only a sleep aid. The result has implications for sleep hygiene and how people use supplements.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>2023年的研究显示，健康年轻人在早晨摄入褪黑激素会削弱认知能力，挑战了把褪黑激素仅视为睡眠辅助的看法。这一发现对日常睡眠管理和补充剂使用具有现实意义。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49214306' data-tags='Privacy,Politics,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49214306' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(198) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://apnews.com/article/ice-body-cameras-officers-shootings-dab1b294c8dc412d9ef8557ccbebfd57' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ICE will release body cam video only when seen in the agency&#x27;s &#x27;best interests&#x27;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ICE 将只在被视为‘自身最佳利益’时公开警员随身摄像头视频</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49214306' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 156</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49214306' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 79</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49214306'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ICE has announced it will release body cam footage only when it deems it in the agency’s best interests. The stance raises concerns about transparency, accountability, and the balance between public access and operational security.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ICE 表示将仅在认定符合自身最佳利益时公开随身摄像头视频，这一立场引发对透明度、问责制以及公众获取信息与执法隐私之间平衡的担忧。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://dims.apnews.com/dims4/default/2b0acec/2147483647/strip/true/crop/2736x1823+0+0/resize/980x653!/quality/90/?url=https%3A%2F%2Fassets.apnews.com%2F19%2Fb7%2F3dcfc05141694e72f1f45db13ac5%2Fcf167b64faeb4ce8b2f059d6942f3aad' data-full='https://dims.apnews.com/dims4/default/2b0acec/2147483647/strip/true/crop/2736x1823+0+0/resize/980x653!/quality/90/?url=https%3A%2F%2Fassets.apnews.com%2F19%2Fb7%2F3dcfc05141694e72f1f45db13ac5%2Fcf167b64faeb4ce8b2f059d6942f3aad' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(200) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.publictechnology.net/2026/08/03/health-and-social-care/nhs-apologises-and-admits-palantir-engineers-have-access-to-identifiable-patient-data/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>NHS apologises and admits Palantir have access to identifiable patient data</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49165775' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 154</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49165775' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 37</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49165775'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The NHS apologizes and reveals that Palantir engineers had access to identifiable patient data, triggering scrutiny over data governance and third-party access.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>英国国家卫生署道歉并披露 Palantir 工程师可访问可识别的患者数据，引发对数据治理和第三方访问的审视。文章讨论隐私、同意与公共部门数据合作的透明度等问题，并强调需要更严格的访问控制。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Doge 的节省被高估？政府监管机构表示</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49209368' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 153</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49209368' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 67</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49209368'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A government watchdog concludes that claimed savings from Doge programs were overstated, prompting scrutiny of how cost savings are calculated in policy programs. The finding could influence future budgeting and evaluation practices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>政府监管机构指出 Doge 项目所 claim 的节省被高估，促使人们重新审视政策项目中的成本节约计算方式。这一发现可能影响未来的预算与评估做法。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(202) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://frogs.vaguespac.es/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>My personal AI benchmark: “Generate an SVG of a frog with a Habsburg jaw”</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A whimsical prompt-based benchmark that tests an AI&#x27;s ability to generate precise vector graphics from a quirky instruction. It highlights strengths and limits in prompt engineering, rendering quality, and control over SVG output.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一个基于提示的有趣基准，测试AI是否能从一个怪异的指令中直接生成矢量图 SVG。分析显示模型在精确性、可控性和输出格式方面的优劣，凸显提示工程的作用。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(203) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.currentaffairs.org/news/the-massive-climate-lie-that-will-destroy-human-civilization' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Gargantuan Lie That Is Collapsing the Climate</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>正在崩塌的庞大气候谎言</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49208686' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 150</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49208686' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 174</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49208686'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that a central climate narrative is collapsing, challenging mainstream climate policy and public confidence. It questions the scale of predicted harms and the effectiveness of proposed solutions, urging rigorous scrutiny of climate claims.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章批评关于气候的核心叙述正在崩塌，质疑主流气候政策及公众信心，质问预测危害的规模及方案的有效性，呼吁对气候论断进行更严格的审视。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(205) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ursulakleguin.com/a-rant-about-technology' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A Rant About “Technology” (2005)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>关于“科技”的吐槽（2005）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49145201' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 148</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49145201' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 85</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49145201'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ursula K. Le Guin&#x27;s 2005 meditation on technology challenges techno-centrism, urging readers to consider how tech shapes culture and power. The essay remains surprisingly prescient about bias, surveillance, and the human costs of acceleration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>乌尔苏拉·勒古恩在2005年的《关于科技的吐槽》挑战科技中心主义，提醒人们思考科技如何塑造文化与权力。她的观点在今天对偏见、监控与加速带来的代价仍具有预见性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='http://static1.squarespace.com/static/5cad42ef90f904e520359371/t/61c1853628a5544251cbdc88/1640072502771/image-asset.jpeg?format=1500w' data-full='http://static1.squarespace.com/static/5cad42ef90f904e520359371/t/61c1853628a5544251cbdc88/1640072502771/image-asset.jpeg?format=1500w' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49201342' data-tags='AI,Space,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post explains why Book Corners won&#x27;t sync contributions back to OpenStreetMap, citing licensing, provenance, and technical challenges around two-way syncing. It highlights interoperability hurdles in open mapping workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文中解释了为何 Book Corners 无法将贡献同步回 OpenStreetMap，原因包括许可、来源追踪和双向同步的技术挑战。该讨论揭示了开放地图工作流中的互操作性难题。</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49123003'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Transit passes designed by hand in 2022 illustrate the era’s craft in physical ID design. The piece uses Milwaukee as a case study to show how manual processes influenced usability, aesthetics, and accessibility.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>2022 年以手工设计为主的交通通行证呈现了那个时代物理身份设计的工艺。以密尔沃基为案例，展示了手工设计对易用性、美观和无障碍性的影响。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49222824' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 145</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49222824' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 63</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49222824'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claude Code now supports cross-session messaging, letting developers reference context from other sessions for more seamless collaboration. The feature reduces repetition and helps maintain state across coding tasks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude Code 现在支持跨会话消息传递，开发者可在不同会话之间引用上下文以实现更无缝的协作。该功能减少重复工作，便于在编码任务之间保持状态。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49206130' data-tags='Security,Data,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49206130' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(211) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://community.frame.work/t/framework-data-breach-discussion/83939' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Framework discloses data breach via Metabase 0-day</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Framework 透露 Metabase 0-day 数据泄露</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49206130' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 144</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49206130' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 53</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49206130'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Framework disclosed a data breach tied to a Metabase zero-day, illustrating how third-party BI tools can expose internal data. The incident highlights patching discipline, access control, and monitoring for OSS dependencies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Framework 披露了与 Metabase 0-day 相关的数据泄露，显示第三方BI工具如何暴露内部数据。事件凸显了打补丁、访问控制和对开源依赖的监控的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://us1.discourse-cdn.com/flex001/uploads/framework3/original/1X/f8ee93f9067eb34d64372000a4f27a89eaf1135b.jpeg' data-full='https://us1.discourse-cdn.com/flex001/uploads/framework3/original/1X/f8ee93f9067eb34d64372000a4f27a89eaf1135b.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49202852' data-tags='Programming,Open Source,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49202852' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(212) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.aleksagordic.com/blog/vllm' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Inside vLLM: Anatomy of a High-Throughput LLM Inference System (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Inside vLLM：高吞吐量LLM推理系统解剖（2025）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49202852' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 144</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49202852' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49202852'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A deep dive into vLLM’s architecture for high-throughput LLM inference, covering memory management, model loading strategies, and batching tactics. The piece offers practical takeaways for building scalable local or edge inference pipelines.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 vLLM 的高吞吐量推理架构进行深度解析，涉及内存管理、模型加载策略与批处理等技巧。为构建可扩展的本地或边缘推理管线提供实用要点。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.aleksagordic.com/blog/vllm/engine_constructor.png' data-full='https://www.aleksagordic.com/blog/vllm/engine_constructor.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49160699' data-tags='AI,Business,Finance'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49160699' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(213) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fortune.com/2026/07/31/ai-debt-hypescalers-capex-capital-spending-hidden-borrowing-bond-issuance/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI&#x27;s debt binge can&#x27;t last, hidden borrowing reaches $1.65T</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 的债务狂潮难以为继，隐藏借款达 1.65 万亿美元</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49160699' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 144</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49160699' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 175</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49160699'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI hype is fueling debt through aggressive capital spending and hidden borrowing, now estimated at about $1.65 trillion. The piece argues this financing model risks unsustainability, with potential repercussions for startups, investors, and the broader tech ecosystem.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 AI 的炒作正在推动财政投入和隐性借款，估算规模约为 1.65 万亿美元。这种融资模式可能不可持续，对初创企业、投资者以及整个科技生态带来风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://fortune.com/img-assets/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/GettyImages-2249021916-e1785504459772.jpg?resize=1200,600' data-full='https://fortune.com/img-assets/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/GettyImages-2249021916-e1785504459772.jpg?resize=1200,600' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49183017' data-tags='Science,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49183017' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(214) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://chillphysicsenjoyer.substack.com/p/the-entropy-of-a-markov-chain' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Entropy of a Markov Chain</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>马尔可夫链的熵</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49183017' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 143</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49183017' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 15</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49183017'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This piece explains the entropy of a Markov chain, discussing how to compute entropy rate from transition matrices and what it reveals about information flow in stochastic processes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章讨论马尔可夫链的熵，讲解如何利用转移矩阵计算熵率，以及这对理解信息在随机过程中的流动有何意义。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ask HN：谁想被雇用？（2026 年 8 月）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49156682' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 143</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49156682' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 416</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49156682'>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>求职者在另一个讨论串中宣告可用时间并展示作品集，招聘方也在积极搜寻候选人。讨论凸显对熟练开发者的高需求以及清晰作品集与沟通的价值。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(216) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.experimental-history.com/p/incentives-are-for-losers' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Incentives are for losers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>激励并非总是有效</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49227652' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 142</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49227652' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 105</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49227652'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A critique of incentive design arguing that external rewards can undermine intrinsic motivation and backfire. The piece surveys experiments and suggests designing incentives that align with long-term goals.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对激励设计的批判性观点，认为外部奖励可能削弱内在动机并产生反效果。文章回顾了相关实验并提出更符合长期目标的激励设计思路。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(217) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techascent.com/blog/just-ducking-around.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DuckDB – Data power tools for your laptop, now in Clojure (2023)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DuckDB 现已在 Clojure 中可用，为笔记本的数据分析提供强大工具</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49175924' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 141</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49175924' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 20</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49175924'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DuckDB, the in-process analytics database, now has a Clojure interface, broadening data-analysis options for JVM-based workflows. This enables seamless ad-hoc queries from CLJ-based stacks and notebooks. The move helps integrate fast analytics into Lisp-friendly tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DuckDB 这款内嵌分析数据库现在提供 Clojure 接口，扩展了 JVM/CLJ 工作流中的数据分析能力。开发者可以在 CLJ 工作流和笔记本中进行快速的交互式查询，进一步把高效分析融入到 Lisp 风格的工具链。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(218) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.computerenhance.com/p/clean-code-horrible-performance' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&quot;Clean&quot; Code, Horrible Performance (2023)</a></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49166331'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Examines how clean-code ideals can sometimes hinder performance, highlighting cases where readability-focused abstractions introduce bottlenecks. It argues for balancing maintainability with practical efficiency in software design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨在某些情况下，洁净代码的理想可能阻碍性能，指出以可读性为中心的抽象会带来瓶颈。主张在软件设计中平衡可维护性与实际效率。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An AI-generated poster won a major Ohio State Fair competition, prompting questions about originality, contest rules, and the role of machine-created art in public showcases. It spotlights the rapid integration of generative tools into creative workflows and the need for clear policy on AI art.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI生成的海报在俄亥俄州州博览会比赛中夺冠，引发关于原创性、比赛规则和AI艺术在公共展示中的地位的讨论。这个案例反映出生成式工具正在快速融入创意工作流，同时需要明确的AI艺术政策。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(220) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nbcsandiego.com/nbc-7-responds-2/californians-data-deletion-requests-drop-become-enforceable-aug-1/4054771/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Californians&#x27; data deletion requests, DROP, become enforceable Aug. 1</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>加州的DROP数据删除请求将于8月1日生效</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49148987' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 141</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49148987' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 98</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49148987'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>California&#x27;s DROP data deletion requests become enforceable, giving residents a stronger tool to demand removal of personal data from services. Businesses should align data-retention practices with the new statutory expectations and implement clear deletion workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>加州的新DROP规则在8月1日起将强制执行数据删除请求，为居民提供更强的个人数据删除权利。企业需调整数据保留与删除工作流，确保合规并回应用户的删除请求。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(222) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.404media.co/do-not-mention-alpr-usage-how-cops-are-trying-to-hide-the-existence-of-flock/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How Cops Are Trying to Hide Their Use of Flock</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>警方如何隐藏对 Flock 的使用</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49172076' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 140</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49172076' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49172076'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Investigative reporting shows that police agencies are attempting to conceal their use of Flock cameras and related predictive policing tech such as ALPR. The move raises transparency and civil-liberties concerns while illustrating the challenges of oversight in surveillance-heavy operations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>调查报道揭露部分警察机构试图隐瞒对 Flock 摄像头及相关ALPR等监控技术的使用。此举引发透明度与公民自由方面的担忧，也凸显在高监控行动中对监督的挑战。</p>

</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49168886'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Germany logged a historic 12 billion kWh of solar feed-in in July 2026, signaling strong solar deployment and impressive calendar-year performance. The record stresses grid integration, storage needs, and policy momentum behind the energy transition, even as weather and seasonality influence output.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>德国在2026年7月实现历史性120亿千瓦时太阳能并网，显示出强劲的太阳能部署和良好的年度表现。这一纪录对电网接入、储能需求与能源转型的政策势头提出持续挑战，同时受天气和季节性因素影响。</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49129015'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Atomic clocks keep time by counting the steady frequency of atomic transitions, yielding far higher accuracy than mechanical clocks. Modern developments include optical lattice and quantum clocks that push precision even further, enabling advances in navigation, telecommunications, and scientific research. These clocks underpin GPS, network timing, and tests of fundamental physics, making timekeeping a critical infrastructure.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>原子钟通过原子跃迁的稳定振荡频率来计时，精度远超机械钟。现代研究包括光学晶格时钟与量子时钟，推动导航、通信和科学研究的边界。高精度时间基准支撑 GPS、网络对时和基础物理测试，成为关键基础设施。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.nist.gov/sites/default/files/styles/social/public/images/2024/08/22/second-blog.png?itok=90FwX7Hc' data-full='https://www.nist.gov/sites/default/files/styles/social/public/images/2024/08/22/second-blog.png?itok=90FwX7Hc' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(227) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://naomibashkansky.com/blog/telepathy/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I’m leaving OpenAI to build telepathy</a></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49185370'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A former OpenAI employee reveals leaving to pursue ‘telepathy’ — a project exploring direct brain-to-brain communication or related tech. The note spotlights tensions between ambitious neural interfaces, AI safety, and the allure of radical human-computer interfaces.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>前 OpenAI 员工宣布离职，去追求“心灵感应”——可能探索直接脑对脑通信或相关神经技术。此举凸显在 AI 安全与人机接口前沿领域的野心与风险。</p>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49115096' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49115096' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(228) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.science.org/doi/10.1126/sciadv.aeg8299' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Discovery of a multicomponent alloy forged by the Hiroshima atomic blast</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>广岛爆炸条件下形成的多组分合金发现</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49115096' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 133</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49115096' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 57</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49115096'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Scientists report a multicomponent alloy formed by the intense conditions of the Hiroshima blast, revealing unexpected phase mixing. The finding sheds light on high-temperature, high-pressure metallurgy and the history of materials.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者报道在广岛爆炸的极端条件下形成的多组分合金，揭示了出人意料的相混合现象。这一发现为高温高压冶金与材料史提供新线索。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一个用 SDL2 实现的经典 Fasttracker II 的克隆，展示了如何用现代工具链重现怀旧音频软件，并带来实现风格与性能的权衡。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(230) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://oskrim.github.io/hardware/2026/08/09/remarkable-over-ssh.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Reviving a four year old reMarkable 2</a></p>

</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Details a project to revive an aging reMarkable 2 tablet by enabling SSH and customizing software. It demonstrates how to breathe new life into older hardware with open tooling, expanding its utility beyond stock features.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>讲述通过开启 SSH 等手段，对一台四年的 reMarkable 2 进行重新利用与定制，延长设备寿命与功能。展示了使用开源工具为旧硬件注入新功能的可行性。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49153361' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49153361' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(231) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://senderledger.com/articles/what-dmarc-actually-protects-you-from' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What DMARC Protects You From, and What It Does Not</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DMARC 能保护你什么，以及不能保护什么</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49153361' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 132</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49153361' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 33</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49153361'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DMARC helps prevent email spoofing by aligning SPF and DKIM with the sender domain, reducing phishing risk. It doesn&#x27;t protect against phishing that bypasses authentication, nor does it secure content-level threats or compromised endpoints.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DMARC 通过对齐 SPF/DKIM 与发件域来防止邮件欺骗，降低钓鱼风险；但对绕过认证的钓鱼、内容级威胁或端点被攻破所带来的风险并无全面保护。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://analytics-siglbex2.on-forge.com/matomo.php?idsite=3&amp;rec=1' data-full='https://analytics-siglbex2.on-forge.com/matomo.php?idsite=3&amp;rec=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49196174' data-tags='Design,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49196174' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(232) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/travel/article/20260805-four-simple-rules-behind-japans-most-liveable-cities' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Four simple rules behind Japan&#x27;s most liveable cities</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>日本最宜居城市背后的四条简单法则</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49196174' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 131</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49196174' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 170</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49196174'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Japan’s most liveable cities share four simple rules: prioritize safety, efficiency, well-connected transit, and resilient public spaces. These habits shape urban design, climate resilience, and daily quality of life.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>日本最宜居城市的共同特征是四条简单原则：重视安全、效率、便捷的公共交通，以及有韧性的公共空间。这些习惯影响城市设计、气候韧性和日常生活质量。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ychef.files.bbci.co.uk/624x351/p0p2r25c.jpg' data-full='https://ychef.files.bbci.co.uk/624x351/p0p2r25c.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(233) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://kibotronics.net/unlisted/lilly-machines/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>John C. Lilly on solid state intelligence and the elimination of man (1978)</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49231397' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 131</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49231397' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 98</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49231397'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A retrospective on John C. Lilly&#x27;s 1978 thoughts about &#x27;solid state intelligence&#x27; and the prospect of eliminating human labor or presence in intelligent systems. The material foreshadows later debates about machine autonomy and the societal consequences of AI.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>回顾约翰·C·莉莉在1978年提出的“固态智能”设想，及其关于让人类在智能系统中被边缘化甚至替代的前瞻性讨论。相关观点预示了日后关于机器自治与人工智能对社会影响的辩论。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49152222' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(234) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bjorg.bjornroche.com/management/ai-productivity-gap/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The AI Productivity Gap</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 生产力差距</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49152222' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 130</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49152222' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 109</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49152222'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI tools exist, but productivity gains depend on workflows, governance, and how teams adopt AI. The piece argues practical steps to close the gap, such as rethinking processes, alignment, and experimentation culture.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>尽管有 AI 工具，生产力提升取决于工作流程、治理以及团队的采纳方式。文章提出通过重新设计流程、对齐目标和营造试验文化等方法来缩小差距。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49146267'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Show HN project demonstrates running NixOS on the DGX Spark platform, illustrating reproducible configurations for AI workloads. It highlights the benefits of declarative management for drivers, libraries, and environments across a GPU cluster.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Show HN 项目展示了在 DGX Spark 平台上运行 NixOS，演示了为 AI 工作负载实现可复现配置的方案。它强调通过声明式管理来统一驱动、库和环境，在 GPU 集群上带来更高的可重复性和运维效率。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(236) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mattbeton.com/blog/bitnet-6502.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Autoregressive Language Model on the 6502 Processor</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>6502 处理器上的自回归语言模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49122655' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 126</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49122655' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49122655'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An autoregressive language model running on a 6502 processor showcases surprising AI capability within extreme hardware constraints. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在 6502 处理器上运行的自回归语言模型展示了在极端硬件约束下的 AI 能力。该项目讨论在复古硬件上实现此类模型的工程技巧与极限，凸显边缘 AI 的可行性与设计取舍。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(237) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://practical.engineering/blog/2026/8/4/the-grid-that-doubles-the-strength-of-the-ground' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Grid That Doubles the Strength of the Ground</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>能把地基强度翻倍的网格</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49178476' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 125</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49178476' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 37</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49178476'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explains how installing a reinforcing grid under soil or foundation increases bearing capacity, potentially doubling ground strength. Includes practical design considerations, installation tips, and real-world case studies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章介绍通过在地基下铺设强化网格来提升土壤承载力的原理与实践，甚至实现强度的显著提升。给出设计要点、安装要领以及实际案例的解读。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49143782' data-tags='Design'>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Octane：将 React 的编程模型编译成代码</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49152640' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 116</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49152640' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 42</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Octane proposes a compiled implementation of React&#x27;s programming model, aiming to improve performance and developer ergonomics by compiling UI logic rather than interpreting it at runtime. It showcases a path to faster, more predictable rendering.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Octane 提出将 React 的编程模型以编译方式实现，旨在通过编译 UI 逻辑来提升性能与开发体验，避免在运行时解释执行带来的开销。它展示了更快且可预测渲染的路径。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文中解释为何为特定 ML 工作负载自研 C/C++ 推理引擎能带来性能、控制和优化方面的优势，而非依赖通用引擎。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49193314'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A call to meet up around Y2K nostalgia, exploring retro tech culture and its relevance to today’s computing. The piece invites readers to reconnect, share projects, and reflect on how early 2000s constraints shaped today’s design.</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49226563' data-tags='Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49226563' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(253) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/future/article/20260807-should-i-stop-cracking-my-knuckles' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Should you stop cracking your knuckles?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你应该停止捏指节吗？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49226563' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 111</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49226563' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 121</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49226563'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Medical consensus on knuckles suggests cracking won&#x27;t cause long-term damage in most cases, but may cause short-term swelling or grip issues. If anxious, consider alternatives like stress relief techniques; keep sessions moderate.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>关于捏指节的医学共识：长期损伤的证据不充分，但频繁捏指可能导致肿胀、疼痛或握力下降。若担心影响，建议用替代放松方式和适度的练习。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ychef.files.bbci.co.uk/624x351/p0p37czn.jpg' data-full='https://ychef.files.bbci.co.uk/624x351/p0p37czn.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49145883' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49145883' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(255) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.juni-mp4.com/posts/42/rooting-the-tplink-tl841n-pt1/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Rooting, firmware analysis and persistent credentials of TP-Link TL-841N</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>TP-Link TL-841N 路由器的 Rooting、固件分析与持久凭证</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49145883' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 110</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49145883' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49145883'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This post dissects how to root the TP-Link TL-841N and analyzes its firmware to reveal security weaknesses, including credentials that survive reflash. It highlights how consumer routers can harbor persistent backdoors and why such persistence matters for threat modeling and firmware security practices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章详细讲解如何对 TP-Link TL-841N 路由器进行 Rooting，并对固件进行分析，揭示安全漏洞及持续存在的凭证。提示了家用路由器中可能存在的持久后门问题，以及在威胁建模和固件安全实践中的意义。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>学会说不</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49234379'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Saying no is framed as essential for focus and high-impact work. It offers practical tips for prioritization, boundary setting, and communicating decisions without burning bridges.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>拒绝被视为提升效率的关键技能，能帮助专注于高回报工作。文章给出设定优先级、界限和清晰沟通决策的实用方法。</p>

</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(257) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/rochus-keller/OberonSystem/tree/op2-rv32' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: A Project Oberon System version running on RISC-V instead of RISC-5</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Oberon 系统在 RISC-V 上的实现，取代 RISC-5</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49230891' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 108</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49230891' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49230891'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An experimental Show HN post shows a version of the Project Oberon system running on RISC-V, rather than the previously targeted RISC-5. It demonstrates that a compact, educational OS can be ported to modern architectures, offering a learning resource for hobbyists. The entry may inspire emulation and education around language-oriented OS design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个实验性的 Show HN 帖子展示了 Project Oberon 系统的一个版本在 RISC-V 上运行，而非传统的 RISC-5。它证明了一个紧凑、以教育为导向的操作系统可以移植到现代架构，为爱好者提供学习资源。此举可能激发对语言与操作系统设计的爱好与探索。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49124917'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Online friends can form real, meaningful connections, offering support and shared experiences even across distances. The piece also notes boundaries and differences from offline relationships, highlighting the value of digital communities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为线上朋友可以成为真正的、有意义的关系提供支持和共同体验，尽管身处异地。也指出线上关系的边界与不同于线下关系之处，强调数字社群的价值。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(259) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://planetscale.com/blog/massively-parallel-postgres-backups' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Massively Parallel Postgres Backups</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>大规模并行的 PostgreSQL 备份</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49124213' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 106</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49124213' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49124213'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>PlanetScale describes massively parallel PostgreSQL backups, enabling faster backups across large clusters and reducing downtime for disaster recovery. The approach likely leverages parallel dumps and streaming to handle big data workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>PlanetScale 介绍一种大规模并行的 PostgreSQL 备份方法，提升对大规模集群的备份速度，降低灾备时的停机时间，可能涉及并行转储与流式备份。</p>
