<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49235378' data-tags='Security,Open Source,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49235378' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(260) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://phrack.org/issues/72/19#article' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Hacker&#x27;s Renaissance (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>黑客的文艺复兴</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49235378' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 105</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49235378' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 74</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49235378'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>There&#x27;s a renewed energy in hacker culture, fueled by open tooling, community labs, and responsible disclosure. It&#x27;s shaping security research into a collaborative, ethics-focused movement rather than a lone-wolf craft.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>黑客社区正呈现复兴态势，得益于开源工具、社区实验室和负责任披露文化。安全研究变得更具协作性和伦理性，不再只是个体的技术探索。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://phrack.org/images/p40ylogo.jpg' data-full='https://phrack.org/images/p40ylogo.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49166918' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49166918' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(261) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nobel_disease' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nobel Disease</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>诺贝尔疾病（Nobel Disease）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49166918' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 104</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49166918' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 109</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49166918'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Nobel Disease describes how some eminent scientists drift toward esoteric, abstract viewpoints after achieving prestige. The concept warns against over-specialization in research leadership.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>诺贝尔疾病描述了部分著名科学家在获得声望后易走向深奥、抽象的观点，警示研究领导层过度专业化的风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://en.wikipedia.org/static/images/icons/enwiki-25.svg' data-full='https://en.wikipedia.org/static/images/icons/enwiki-25.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(262) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://virtuallyfun.com/2026/08/03/windows-xp-2002-for-the-itanium-unbridled-rage/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Windows XP 2002 for the Itanium: Unbridled rage</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Itanium 的 Windows XP 2002：怒火难平</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49162086' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 102</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49162086' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 53</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49162086'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A retrospective on Windows XP 2002 for Itanium, illustrating brutal compatibility gaps, scarce drivers, and poor performance that frustrated early adopters. The episode underscores how exotic architectures can derail software ecosystems without broad software support.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>回顾 Isanium 架构下的 Windows XP 2002，揭示了极差的兼容性、稀缺的驱动与糟糕的性能，使早期尝鲜者备感挫折。这一事件凸显了在缺乏广泛软件生态支持时，异构架构往往会崩塌整个软件生态。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49202690' data-tags='Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49202690' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(264) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://roge.onwrite.app/what-is-a-product' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What is a product?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>什么是产品？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49202690' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 99</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49202690' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 63</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49202690'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Product is the value delivered to users by solving a real problem, not simply a bundle of features. It reframes product work as decisions about outcomes, trade-offs, and learning from user feedback. The takeaway for builders is to focus on measurable impact and alignment with user needs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>产品是通过解决真实问题为用户创造的价值，而不仅仅是一堆功能。产品管理被视为对结果、权衡与从用户反馈中学习的决策过程。核心在于关注可衡量的影响并与用户需求保持一致。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1532298229144-0ec0c57515c7?crop=entropy&amp;cs=tinysrgb&amp;fit=max&amp;fm=jpg&amp;ixid=M3w5NjE5NDZ8MHwxfHNlYXJjaHwyN3x8cHJvZHVjdHxlbnwxfHx8fDE3ODYwNTA1ODF8MA&amp;ixlib=rb-4.1.0&amp;q=80&amp;w=1080' data-full='https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1532298229144-0ec0c57515c7?crop=entropy&amp;cs=tinysrgb&amp;fit=max&amp;fm=jpg&amp;ixid=M3w5NjE5NDZ8MHwxfHNlYXJjaHwyN3x8cHJvZHVjdHxlbnwxfHx8fDE3ODYwNTA1ODF8MA&amp;ixlib=rb-4.1.0&amp;q=80&amp;w=1080' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49156071' data-tags='Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49156071' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(265) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.sltrib.com/news/environment/2026/08/03/utah-sets-solar-power-record/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Utah produced more power from solar than any other source in May, a new first</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>五月份太阳能发电首次超越其他任何发电来源</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49156071' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 99</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49156071' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 69</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49156071'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>For the first time in May, solar power topped every other energy source in Utah&#x27;s electricity mix. The milestone underscores the rapid diffusion of solar in the state and has implications for grid planning, storage needs, and clean-energy policy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>五月份，犹他州太阳能发电首次超过其他任何发电来源，成为主要能源。此里程碑凸显太阳能在该州的快速普及，并对电网规划、储能需求与清洁能源政策产生影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.sltrib.com/resizer/v2/CA3N5ZPHGFHP5JVNGP7SHFVA6Q.JPG?auth=c1884c111e05fef3567c28b2e44098a0ebab0a9c6fb29c3972509c7016f1bcaa&amp;width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=88&amp;smart=true' data-full='https://www.sltrib.com/resizer/v2/CA3N5ZPHGFHP5JVNGP7SHFVA6Q.JPG?auth=c1884c111e05fef3567c28b2e44098a0ebab0a9c6fb29c3972509c7016f1bcaa&amp;width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=88&amp;smart=true' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49225918' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(266) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.maryland.gov/dnr/2026/08/05/dnr-closes-additional-areas-of-cunningham-falls-state-park-following-second-beaver-attack/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Maryland Closes More of Cunningham Falls State Park After Second Beaver Attack</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>马里兰州坎宁厄姆瀑布州立公园在发生第二起海狸攻击后再度关闭部分区域</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49225918' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 99</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49225918' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 61</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49225918'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Maryland closes additional areas of Cunningham Falls State Park after a second beaver attack, prioritizing visitor safety. Wildlife incidents like this can trigger temporary closures and affect park access.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>马里兰州在发生第二起海狸攻击后，继续关闭坎宁厄姆瀑布州立公园的部分区域，强调游客安全。此类野生动物事件可能导致临时闭园，影响游客进入与活动安排。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.maryland.gov/dnr/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2019/08/agencyTitle-generic.png' data-full='https://news.maryland.gov/dnr/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2019/08/agencyTitle-generic.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(267) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://yogthos.net/posts/2026-08-03-splat-painter.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Painting with Gaussians</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49182695' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 98</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49182695' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 19</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49182695'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Showcases a painting approach that uses Gaussian distributions or kernels to generate brushstrokes and textures, blending math with creative process. Includes visuals and perhaps code for reproducing generative art.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>展示一种以高斯分布/高斯核为基础的绘画方法，通过数学模型生成笔触与纹理，将艺术与统计方法结合，配有示意图与实现思路。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49154127' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(268) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/garagehq/nightcrawler/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Nightcrawler – A local AI pentesting agent running on a smartphone</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Nightcrawler——在智能手机上运行的本地 AI 渗透测试代理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49154127' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 98</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49154127' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 30</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49154127'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Nightcrawler is a self-contained AI pentesting agent that runs on a smartphone, enabling on-device security testing without cloud access. It demonstrates how edge AI can empower rapid, private security assessments and may inspire a new class of mobile security tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Nightcrawler 是一个在智能手机上本地运行的 AI 渗透测试代理，支持在设备上进行安全评估，无需云端。这体现了边缘人工智能在移动安全工具中的潜力，或将催生一类新型本地安全测试工具。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/62405989d010464bb0787bd5164766b300ed7f0aec2975dc2772ed6eb3b9b6df/garagehq/nightcrawler' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/62405989d010464bb0787bd5164766b300ed7f0aec2975dc2772ed6eb3b9b6df/garagehq/nightcrawler' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(269) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.janestreet.com/can-you-reverse-engineer-an-asic/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Can you reverse engineer an ASIC?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你可以反向工程ASIC吗？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49200933' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 97</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49200933' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 59</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49200933'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post surveys the practicality, techniques, and limits of reverse engineering ASICs, touching on security, IP protection, and industry implications.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章探讨了反向工程ASIC的可行性、方法与局限，涉及安全、知识产权保护及行业影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.janestreet.com/can-you-reverse-engineer-an-asic/puzzle.gif' data-full='https://blog.janestreet.com/can-you-reverse-engineer-an-asic/puzzle.gif' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49166442' data-tags='AI,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49166442' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(270) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02412' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why Large Language Models Fail at Tabular Prediction</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为什么大型语言模型在表格预测上会失败</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49166442' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 97</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49166442' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 32</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49166442'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The paper analyzes why LLMs struggle with predicting structured tabular data, citing distribution shifts, error accumulation, and reasoning limits. It discusses implications for when to rely on LLMs versus using specialized models or hybrid approaches.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>论文分析了大语言模型在表格数据预测中的局限，指出分布偏移、错误累积以及推理能力的局限等原因。讨论了在何时应依赖 LLM、何时应采用专门模型或混合方法的意义。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49133850' data-tags='Hardware,Science,Privacy,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49133850' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(271) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://zeus.ugent.be/blog/25-26/12urenloop-uwb/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Building a local positioning system to track runners using Ultra-Wideband</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用超宽带构建本地定位系统追踪跑者</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49133850' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 97</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49133850' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49133850'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers describe a local positioning system built on Ultra-Wideband to track runners, delivering high-precision location data for endurance events. The setup enables real-time tracking, safety monitoring, and performance analytics that GPS often struggles with indoors or under canopy. It shows how accessible localization tech can be for sports events.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者展示了基于超宽带的本地定位系统，用于追踪跑者并提供高精度定位。该系统实现了实时追踪、安全监控和比赛分析，克服了GPS在室内或遮挡情形下的局限。这也展示了定位技术在体育赛事中的实用性与可获取性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pics.zeus.gent/JA9I7ZXKXfOpGQyOoT1GsavWAn467bp3LzJj6Xq1.png' data-full='https://pics.zeus.gent/JA9I7ZXKXfOpGQyOoT1GsavWAn467bp3LzJj6Xq1.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49183079' data-tags='Science,Space'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49183079' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(272) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://rubinobservatory.org/news/rubin-new-window-cosmos-field' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Rubin Observatory&#x27;s first LSST Camera release: 500k galaxies in the COSMOS field</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>鲁宾天文台首个 LSST 相机数据：COSMOS 区域50万星系</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49183079' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 96</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49183079' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 15</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49183079'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Rubin Observatory releases first LSST camera data: a COSMOS-field catalog of 500k galaxies, enabling large-scale structure studies and cross-survey science; showcases data volume and processing pipelines.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>鲁宾天文台发布首个 LSST 相机数据，COSMOS 区域包含约 50 万星系，为大尺度结构研究及跨调查科学提供新数据，展示了庞大数据与处理流程。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.noirlab.edu/media/archives/images/screen640/noirlab2618a.jpg' data-full='https://storage.noirlab.edu/media/archives/images/screen640/noirlab2618a.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49170915' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(273) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.16763' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>When AI Benchmarks Plateau: A Systematic Study of Benchmark Saturation</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 基准测试的拐点：基准饱和的系统性研究</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49170915' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 96</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49170915' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 95</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49170915'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A systematic study analyzes how AI benchmarks saturate, showing diminishing returns and their impact on progress claims. The work argues for rethinking evaluation design and adopting more robust, diverse benchmarks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一项系统性研究分析了AI基准的饱和现象，显示收益递减及其对进展声称的影响。研究主张重新设计评估方法，采用更强健、更多样化的基准。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(274) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.warp.dev/blog/introducing-the-warp-agent-cli-coding-agent' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Warp Agent CLI</a></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49171766'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Warp.dev launches the Warp Agent CLI, a coding agent accessible from the terminal. It supports automated coding tasks, task orchestration, and deeper integration with Warp&#x27;s AI tooling. This lowers the entry barrier for building AI-assisted workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Warp Agent CLI 推出，能在终端中访问的编码助手。它支持自动化编码任务、任务编排，以及与 Warp 的AI工具更深的整合，为构建AI辅助工作流降低了门槛。</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49129545'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A thoughtful look at how tracing word histories reveals migration, culture, and shifts in knowledge. The piece ties etymology to modern NLP and knowledge graphs, illustrating why origins matter for understanding concepts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨追溯词源如何揭示迁徙、文化与知识演变。文章将词源学与现代 NLP、知识图谱联系起来，说明语言起源对理解概念的重要性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(276) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/johnsonjh/cpm386' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>CP/M-386 – CP/M for 386 protected mode, derived from CP/M‑68K</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49149898' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 91</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49149898' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 46</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49149898'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>CP/M-386 brings CP/M to 386-class hardware in protected mode, derived from CP/M-68K. It serves as a nostalgic platform for retro computing and a testbed for understanding legacy OS concepts on modern CPUs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>CP/M-386 将经典 CP/M 移植到 386 架构的保护模式，源自 CP/M‑68K。它为复古计算爱好者提供一个怀旧平台，同时帮助学习在现代 CPU 上实现遗留操作系统的思路。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(278) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://propelmusic.co.nz/articles/the-sound-went-quiet-nz-music-media' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>New Zealand lost its music media, and what we&#x27;re building to replace it</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在无全局解释器锁的 Python 上扩展 NumPy 的规模</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49112023' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 87</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49112023' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>

<p class='hn-title'>(281) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://medium.com/@jonas.eschenburg/stv-a-video-codec-for-the-atari-st-6e46355c50e4' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>STV: A full-motion video codec for the Atari ST</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>STV：为 Atari ST 的全速视频编解码器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49113256' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 87</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49113256' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49113256'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>STV is a full-motion video codec for the Atari ST, pushing modern video capabilities onto vintage hardware. The project highlights the engineering tricks required to render video within the machine&#x27;s limits.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>STV 是为 Atari ST 设计的全速视频编解码器，将现代视频能力带到老旧硬件上。该项目展示了在设备限定条件下实现视频渲染所需的工程技巧。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49230022'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A retrospective on the Alpha 21264, highlighting its architectural innovations and strong server performance that positioned it as a landmark RISC processor of the era. The piece revisits design choices and its influence on later generations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>回顾 DEC Alpha 21264，强调其在架构创新与服务器性能方面的突出表现，使其成为当时 RISC 处理器的里程碑。文章回顾了设计取舍及对后续代处理器设计的影响。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49235011' data-tags='AI,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49235011' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(283) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.economist.com/britain/2026/08/06/the-tragedy-of-the-commons-ai-edition' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The tragedy of the commons, AI edition</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI时代的公地悲剧</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49235011' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 86</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49235011' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 51</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49235011'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI introduces shared resources—compute, data, and access to models—that can be overused without governance. The column weighs policy and market options to prevent a classic tragedy of the commons in AI development.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI 时代的公地包括计算资源、数据和模型访问等共享要素，若缺乏治理容易被过度利用或垄断。文章探讨通过政策与市场手段避免AI发展中的公地悲剧。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49182685' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49182685' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(284) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://build2.org/blog/faster-than-ninja.xhtml' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Faster Than Ninja</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>比忍者还快</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49182685' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 85</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49182685' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 30</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49126716' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(285) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cacm.acm.org/blogcacm/goodharts-law-comes-for-every-benchmark-you-trust/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Goodhart&#x27;s Law Comes for Every Benchmark You Trust</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>戈德哈特定律也来侵蚀你信任的每一个基准</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49126716' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 85</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49126716' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 35</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49126716'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Goodhart&#x27;s Law warns that optimizing a benchmark makes it a target, which can erode generalization and mislead progress. The piece argues for robust, multi-metric evaluation to prevent overfitting benchmarks in ML research.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>戈德哈特定律提醒：把基准当作目标来优化会削弱泛化并扭曲进展。文章主张采用多元评测和更健壮的评估体系，防止过度拟合基准。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(291) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04221' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Zigbee vs. Matter over Thread:Understanding IoT Protocol Performance in Practice</a></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49176983'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A practical comparison of Zigbee and Matter over Thread highlights throughput, reliability, and power usage in real deployments. Matter over Thread often offers better interoperability and scalability, though Zigbee remains strong in mature ecosystems for certain use cases.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在实际部署中对 Zigbee 与运行在 Thread 上的 Matter 进行对比，聚焦吞吐、可靠性与功耗。Matter over Thread 在互通性与可扩展性方面通常优于 Zigbee，但 Zigbee 在某些成熟生态中仍有优势。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(296) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://zaneham.github.io/voyager-fds-emulator/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Voyager 1 FDS Computer Emulator</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49221679' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 81</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49221679' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49221679'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A software emulator recreates Voyager 1&#x27;s Flight Data System, letting researchers run and test the original software stack on modern hardware. This preserves a critical slice of deep-space missions and aids education and experimentation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个软件仿真器重现 Voyager 1 的飞行数据系统，允许在现代硬件上运行并测试原始软件栈。这为深空任务遗产保护以及教育和实验提供了平台。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49178608' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49178608' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(298) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29377' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Zero-Mem: Zero-Token Memory Operations for LLM Agents</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Zero-Mem：面向 LLM 代理的零令牌内存操作</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49178608' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 80</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49178608' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49178608'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Introduces a memory technique that doesn&#x27;t consume tokens, enabling LLM agents to remember and reason over long sessions without bloating prompts. The approach promises lower latency and a smaller memory footprint for persistent AI agents.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提出一种不消耗令牌的内存操作方法，使 LLM 代理在长会话中仍能记忆和推理，降低提示体积与延迟。该思路有望提升长时运行代理的内存效率与能源消耗。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(299) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/xoreaxeaxeax/repsych' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Psychological Warfare in Reverse Engineering (2015)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>逆向工程中的心理战（2015）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49214775' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 77</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49214775' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49214775'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 2015 examination of the psychology involved in reverse engineering, exploring how attackers exploit cognitive biases and social dynamics. The work highlights the human element in security research and why technical tools alone aren’t enough.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一篇关于逆向工程中心理战的研究，探讨攻击者如何利用认知偏差和社交因素影响防守。强调安全研究中的人因要素，单靠工具不足以应对威胁。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/fd4332f002f6420b207508d7afb10de56c22ffc1377b06ac76b532a2a32a0741/xoreaxeaxeax/REpsych' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/fd4332f002f6420b207508d7afb10de56c22ffc1377b06ac76b532a2a32a0741/xoreaxeaxeax/REpsych' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(303) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://secretspec.dev/blog/where-env-went-wrong/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Where .env Went Wrong</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>.env Went Wrong 的原因与对策</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49124291'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores common pitfalls with .env usage, including secret leakage, mis-scoped variables, and dev/prod divergence. It offers safer patterns like secrets managers, environment-specific configs, and CI-bound handling to prevent issues in deployment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>讨论 .env 使用中的常见陷阱，如机密泄露、变量作用域错误以及开发/生产环境差异。给出更安全的做法，如使用机密管理工具、按环境分配置，以及在 CI 环境中安全处理。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49230630' data-tags='Finance,Business,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49230630' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(304) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=NufJ7g63KSY' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why Wall Street is ignoring big tech&#x27;s debt [video]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>华尔街为何忽视科技巨头的债务</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49230630' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 76</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49230630' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 138</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49230630'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explains why investors and credit markets have largely ignored heavyweight debt from Big Tech, despite rising interest costs. The talk argues that high growth, strong cash flows, and AI-driven monetization keep debt manageable, while market structure and risk appetite drive resilience.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>阐明了为何华尔街对科技巨头的债务反应冷却——尽管融资成本上升，投资者仍相对乐观。文中指出高增长、强劲现金流以及 AI 驱动的盈利前景使得债务负担可控，同时市场结构和风险偏好也在支撑其耐受性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为什么一切都在内核中？</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49123231'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues that kernel-level approaches dominate certain workloads and designs, discussing tradeoffs between performance, safety, and complexity. The piece prompts readers to rethink where computation should live in modern systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>讨论内核在现代系统中的主导地位及其优劣，探讨将计算放在内核中的性能、安全与复杂性权衡。促使读者重新思考架构分层。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(307) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://xvw.lol/en/articles/oop-refl.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Guarded Methods in OCaml (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OCaml 的受保护方法</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49157770'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This piece examines how to implement guarded methods within OCaml’s object system, balancing safety with OCaml’s functional roots. It presents patterns for maintaining invariants and preventing runtime errors in OO code.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文探讨在 OCaml 的对象系统中实现受保护的方法，兼顾安全性与函数式特性。提出通过设计模式维护不变性、避免运行时错误的做法。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49169227' data-tags='Politics,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49169227' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(308) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='http://observationalepidemiology.blogspot.com/2026/07/its-not-fear-of-ai-communism-its-fear.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>It&#x27;s not a fear of &quot;AI communism&quot;; it&#x27;s a fear of competitive market capitalism</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>不是害怕‘AI共产主义’，而是害怕竞争性市场资本主义</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49169227' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 75</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49169227' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 69</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49169227'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A critique arguing fears about AI stem from concerns about how AI accelerates competitive market capitalism rather than ideological fear. It discusses policy, labor, and innovation incentives amid automation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇评论认为对AI的担忧更多源自AI加剧市场资本主义竞争的现实，而非政治意识形态。文章探讨自动化扩张对政策、劳动市场与创新激励的影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49157997' data-tags='Programming,DevOps,Startups'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49157997' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(309) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://hoplite.sh' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Launch HN: Hoplite (YC S26) – Effortlessly deploy cloud coding agents</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Launch HN：Hoplite（YC S26）— 轻松部署云端编码代理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49157997' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 75</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49157997' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 61</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49157997'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Hoplite aims to simplify deploying cloud-based coding agents, enabling teams to spin up agents that can write, run, and test code in the cloud with minimal setup.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该产品旨在让云端编码代理的部署变得极简，强调自动化编程任务、持续集成/部署和实验的无缝体验，同时提示成本与安全性权衡。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49160631' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(310) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://status.digdir.no/incidents/d7hvqmf2yr3l' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DDoS against Norwegian government IT infrastructure – status</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>挪威政府 IT 基础设施遭 DDoS 攻击：最新状态</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49160631' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 75</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49160631' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 53</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49160631'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A status update shows DDoS activity affecting Norwegian government IT systems, with responders routing traffic through scrubbing centers and applying mitigations. The incident highlights resilience challenges for public-sector networks under sustained assault.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>最新状态显示挪威政府 IT 系统正遭受 DDoS 攻击，响应者通过洗涤中心路由流量并实施缓解措施。事件凸显公部门网络在持续攻击下的韧性挑战。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49154441' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49154441' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(311) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://admiralcloudberg.medium.com/reaping-the-whirlwind-inside-the-potomac-river-midair-collision-0475416f2b0f' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Potomac River Midair Collision</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>波托马克河上空相撞事故调查</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49154441' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 75</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49154441' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 23</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49154441'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A deep dive into a Potomac River midair collision, tracing contributing factors such as airspace coordination and human/system failures. It discusses lessons for safety reforms, automation, and how modern traffic-management tools could prevent recurrence.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对波托马克河上空相撞事件进行深入分析，追溯涉及的空域协同、人为与系统失误等因素。讨论针对安全改革、自动化以及现代流量管理工具如何防止类似事件再次发生的教训。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://miro.medium.com/v2/da:true/resize:fill:1200:632/g:fp:0.5:0.82/0*8T1IvpBp6ZwFuDkr' data-full='https://miro.medium.com/v2/da:true/resize:fill:1200:632/g:fp:0.5:0.82/0*8T1IvpBp6ZwFuDkr' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49149163' data-tags='Science,Culture,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49149163' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(312) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nature.com/articles/s44184-025-00171-5' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Unexpected events and prosocial behavior: the Batman effect (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>意外事件与亲社会行为：蝙蝠侠效应</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49149163' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 74</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49149163' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 23</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49149163'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Research on the Batman effect shows surprising link between unexpected events and prosocial behavior, suggesting identity framing can boost cooperation. The findings offer practical implications for education, management, and public policy on fostering cooperative behavior.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究表明，意外事件会提升亲社会行为，蝙蝠侠效应暗示身份认同能增强自控与合作。这对教育、管理与公共政策在激励协作方面有现实意义。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.springernature.com/m685/springer-static/image/art%3A10.1038%2Fs44184-025-00171-5/MediaObjects/44184_2025_171_Fig1_HTML.png' data-full='https://media.springernature.com/m685/springer-static/image/art%3A10.1038%2Fs44184-025-00171-5/MediaObjects/44184_2025_171_Fig1_HTML.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49148176' data-tags='Science,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49148176' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(313) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bubble_memory' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Bubble Memory</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>泡沫存储</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49148176' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 72</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49148176' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 23</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49148176'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Bubble memory was an early data storage technology using magnetic bubbles; explains how it worked and why it faded with semiconductor memory rising.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>泡沫存储是一种早期的数据存储技术，利用磁性“泡泡”来存储信息，介绍其工作原理及为何被半导体存储所替代。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/f/f1/Bubble_memory_module.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=index&amp;utm_content=thumbnail_unscaled' data-full='https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/f/f1/Bubble_memory_module.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=index&amp;utm_content=thumbnail_unscaled' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49106935' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(314) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ei3lh.eu/2025/11/20/replacing-the-kobo-libra-h2o-battery/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Replacing the Kobo Libra H2O Battery</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>更换 Kobo Libra H2O 电池</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49106935' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 72</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49106935' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49106935'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A practical guide to replacing the Kobo Libra H2O’s battery, detailing the tools, steps, and safety notes for extending an aging e-reader’s life. It highlights common battery-degradation signs and the trade-offs of DIY repairs versus replacement.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提供 Kobo Libra H2O 电池更换的实用指南，列出所需工具、操作步骤与安全要点，帮助延长老旧电子阅读器的使用寿命。文中还点出电池衰减的常见信号以及自助维修与更换之间的权衡。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49204546' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49204546' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(315) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.shreveporttimes.com/story/news/local/louisiana/2026/07/28/is-new-orleans-using-ai-to-answer-911-calls-instead-of-human-dispatchers-impacts-emergencies-crime/91065014007/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>New Orleans is testing Carbyne’s AI-powered Emergency Call Triage software</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>新奥尔良正在测试 Carbyne 的 AI 驱动紧急呼叫分诊软件</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49204546' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 71</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49204546' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 116</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49204546'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New Orleans is piloting Carbyne’s AI-powered emergency call triage to automatically route calls by urgency. Early results suggest faster initial assessment and lighter dispatcher loads, but accuracy, bias, and accountability remain key concerns before wider rollout. The test highlights the trade-offs between speed and human oversight in critical services.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新奥尔良正在试点 Carbyne 的 AI 驱动紧急呼叫分诊系统，以自动根据紧急程度分流电话。初步结果显示可提升初步评估速度并减轻调度员工作量，但准确性、偏见与问责仍是扩大部署前的关键考量。此举凸显在关键服务中对速度与人工监督之间的权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.shreveporttimes.com/gcdn/authoring/authoring-images/2026/04/03/USAT/89452965007-xxx-a-iassisted-911-centers-th-3.jpg?crop=4366,2456,x0,y191&amp;width=3200&amp;height=1801&amp;format=pjpg&amp;auto=webp' data-full='https://www.shreveporttimes.com/gcdn/authoring/authoring-images/2026/04/03/USAT/89452965007-xxx-a-iassisted-911-centers-th-3.jpg?crop=4366,2456,x0,y191&amp;width=3200&amp;height=1801&amp;format=pjpg&amp;auto=webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49145259' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49145259' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(316) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ykiko.me/en/articles/692886292/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Thoroughly Understanding C++ ABI (2024)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>透彻理解 C++ ABI</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49145259' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 71</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49145259' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 65</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49145259'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An in-depth guide to C++ ABI reviews the nitty-gritty of binary interfaces, name mangling, and calling conventions. Understanding ABI stability matters for libraries, binary compatibility, and cross-compiler interoperability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文深入讲解 C++ 的 ABI（应用二进制接口）的细节，包括调用约定、名字改写和二进制兼容性。掌握 ABI 的稳定性对库开发、跨编译器互操作性至关重要。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.ykiko.me/en/articles/692886292/featured.png' data-full='https://www.ykiko.me/en/articles/692886292/featured.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49163466' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(317) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://omnistrate.com/blog/byoc-anywhere-the-spectrum-of-bring-your-own-cloud-deployments' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>BYOC Anywhere: The Spectrum of Bring Your Own Cloud Deployments</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>BYOC Anywhere：自带云部署的全谱</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49163466' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 71</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49163466' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 11</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49163466'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>BYOC Anywhere surveys the spectrum of Bring-Your-Own-Cloud deployments from on-prem to cloud, highlighting governance, security, and portability advantages. It provides patterns for building vendor-agnostic, auditable architectures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>BYOC Anywhere 探讨从本地到边缘再到云端的自带云部署全景，强调治理、安全与可移植性。给出构建无厂商依赖、可审计架构的模式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://omnistrate.com/og/byoc-anywhere-the-spectrum-of-bring-your-own-cloud-deployments.png' data-full='https://omnistrate.com/og/byoc-anywhere-the-spectrum-of-bring-your-own-cloud-deployments.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(318) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lists.securityfocus.com/hyperkitty/list/bugtraq@securityfocus.com/thread/CHKLXLA7SJEWLDFHWXB3QU57ADOXGL2E/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Bugtraq is back</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49176947' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 70</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49176947' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49176947'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Security mailing list Bugtraq returns, reviving a forum for responsible disclosure and security research. The revival signals renewed momentum for coordinated vulnerability disclosure in the community.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Bugtraq 安全邮件列表重新上线，为漏洞披露与安全研究提供新的讨论平台。此举预示着社区协作与信息共享的回归与增强。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://secure.gravatar.com/avatar/1f3655ac20a44d8747f5df60fd09c5f0.jpg?s=120&amp;d=mm&amp;r=g' data-full='https://secure.gravatar.com/avatar/1f3655ac20a44d8747f5df60fd09c5f0.jpg?s=120&amp;d=mm&amp;r=g' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49141236' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(319) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/alfikpl/ao486' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ao486: x86-compatible Verilog core implementing all features of a 486 SX (2014)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ao486：实现 486 SX 全特性的 x86 兼容 Verilog 核</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49141236' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 70</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49141236' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49141236'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ao486 is an open-source Verilog core that implements the full 486 SX ISA, offering a hardware recreation of late-80s/early-90s PC CPUs for education and emulation. It serves as a teaching tool and a platform for retro-computing experiments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ao486 是一个开源 Verilog 核，完整实现 486 SX 指令集，提供对晚80年代/早90年代 PC CPU 的硬件再现，适合作为教学工具与复古计算实验的平台。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/37f561ec0476248f02b1ecf9638e555ca9bc54fc0790240809ba78a87f34dade/alfikpl/ao486' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/37f561ec0476248f02b1ecf9638e555ca9bc54fc0790240809ba78a87f34dade/alfikpl/ao486' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49198843' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49198843' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(320) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/V4bel/Zapscape' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Zapscape (CVE-2026-64561): Guest-to-Host Escape in KVM/x86</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Zapscape：KVM/x86 的 Guest-to-Host Escape 漏洞(CVE-2026-64561)</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49198843' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 69</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49198843' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49198843'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A critical vulnerability in KVM/x86 enables guest VMs to escape to the host, compromising virtualization isolation. It underscores urgent need for patches and defense-in-depth in virtualization environments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>KVM/x86 中存在一处关键漏洞，允许来宾虚拟机逃逸到宿主机，威胁虚拟化隔离。强调需要紧急修补与加强虚拟化环境的多层防护。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/77a2b56321fe9c0710ca3cca8a59f3fa634857bbf18f0a65ed2d9a4e07d9ba23/V4bel/Zapscape' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/77a2b56321fe9c0710ca3cca8a59f3fa634857bbf18f0a65ed2d9a4e07d9ba23/V4bel/Zapscape' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49158287' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49158287' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(321) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://hamvocke.com/blog/task-runners/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Use Task Runners for Common Coding Tasks</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用任务运行器完成常见编码任务</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49158287' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 69</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49158287' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 27</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49158287'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A practical guide advocating task runners to automate common coding chores—linting, testing, building, scaffolding—to improve consistency and speed up development. It also weighs when to adopt task runners versus heavier build systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇指南主张通过任务运行器自动化常见编码任务（如 lint、测试、构建和脚手架），并比较不同工具的优劣，提升开发效率与工作流一致性。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49234279' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(322) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.startingfromnix.com/p/if-experience-is-the-goal-then-you' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>If experience is the goal, then you always win</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>若目标是经验，那么你总是赢</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49234279' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 69</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49234279' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49234279'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>If experience is the goal, you’re wired to win because practice and challenging tasks build resilience and judgment. The piece argues for deliberate exposure to hard problems as a driver of growth.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>如果经验是目标，那么通过挑战性任务、反思实践来积累经验，就会持续获胜。文章倡导以实践驱动成长的职业路径。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(323) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.404media.co/software-giant-sap-stops-most-travel-and-hiring-because-of-ais-soaring-cost/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Software Giant SAP Stops Most Travel and Hiring Because of AI&#x27;s Soaring Cost</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>软件巨头 SAP 因 AI 成本飙升而停止大多数出差与招聘</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49229412' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 69</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49229412' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 52</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49229412'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reports that SAP is curbing travel and hiring as AI-related costs surge, signaling how large software companies manage expenses amid rising compute and data costs. The piece reflects on broader implications for corporate spending and talent strategy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>报道称 SAP 因 AI 相关成本激增而削减出差和招聘，显示大型软件公司在成本上升时的支出管理策略。文章还就企业支出与人才规划的广泛影响进行讨论。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(324) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/SwagSoftware/KisakCOD' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>KisakCOD – Open-source reimplementation of Call of Duty 4 Multiplayer</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49159970' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 68</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49159970' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49159970'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>KisakCOD is an open-source reimplementation of Call of Duty 4’s multiplayer, enabling community-driven fixes and cross-platform play. It showcases how fan-led efforts preserve and extend classic games when official support wanes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>KisakCOD 是对 Call of Duty 4 多人模式的开源重实现，允许社区驱动的修复与跨平台游玩。当官方支持减少时，玩家自发的努力展示了如何让经典游戏得以延续。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49119508' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49119508' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(325) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25973' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>k-Coloring is Faster than Computing the Chromatic Number</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>k-着色比求解色数更快</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49119508' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 68</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49119508' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 15</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49119508'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers show that determining k-colorability of a graph can be faster than computing the exact chromatic number, revealing nuanced gaps between decision problems and optimization; implications for graph algorithms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究表明，判定一个图是否可被 k 着色的时间可以比精确求解色数更短，这揭示了判定问题与优化问题之间的细微差异，对图算法有重要启示。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49127983' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(326) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.blog/engineering/architecture-optimization/dont-stop-early-case-folding-source-code-at-memory-speed/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Don&#x27;t stop early: Case-folding source code at memory speed</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>别早停：在内存速度下对源代码进行大小写折叠优化</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49127983' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 67</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49127983' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 27</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49127983'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A deep-dive on optimizing case-folding in source code with attention to memory-speed characteristics. The piece argues for pushing certain optimizations deeper into the pipeline and avoiding premature early exits in compiler design to squeeze performance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章深入探讨在内存速度下对源代码进行大小写折叠的优化。作者提出应将某些优化推向更低层级、避免过早放弃优化，以提升编译器性能。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(327) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.stackdump.com/posts/petri-net-sequencer' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Petri Nets as a Music Sequencer</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用 Petri 网作为音乐序列器</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49158934'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Shows how Petri nets can model music sequencing, offering a formal framework for rhythm and melody generation and potentially new composition tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>展示了如何用 Petri 网对音乐序列进行建模，提供了一种形式化的节奏与旋律生成框架，并可能催生新的作曲工具。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(328) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.thisandthat.chat/blog/most-tech-revolutions-made-work-worse-for-employees/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Most tech revolutions made work worse for employees</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>大多数科技变革让员工的工作变得更糟</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49170076' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 67</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49170076' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 31</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49170076'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A critique arguing that most tech revolutions have worsened work conditions for employees, despite productivity gains. It calls for thoughtful design of tech adoption and stronger worker protections.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>批评认为大多数科技变革在提高生产力的同时，往往让员工的工作环境变得更差。文章呼吁更周全地设计技术采用并加强劳动者保护。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(329) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.teknogeek.io/posts/what-happened-to-hackerone/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What Happened to HackerOne?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>HackerOne 到底发生了什么？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49238561' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 67</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49238561' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 19</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49238561'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>HackerOne, once a dominant bug-bounty marketplace, has faced market headwinds and leadership shifts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>HackerOne 作为主流的漏洞赏金平台，近来遭遇市场挑战与组织调整。文章聚焦发生了什么变化——客户流失、来自自建计划的竞争、以及安全研究经济学的变化——以及这对漏洞赏金生态的影响。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49158141' data-tags='Open Source,Programming,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49158141' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(330) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/MikeLuu99/searxng-rust' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>SearXNG in Rust</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>没有人愿意再花钱做 Myst 式的游戏</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49189726' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 65</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49189726' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 115</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49189726'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that modern production budgets make Myst-style puzzle adventures economically unviable, even for beloved franchises. It explores pressure on designers to deliver high production values and the potential shift toward indie reboots or smaller scope projects.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为如今制作 Myst 风格的解谜游戏成本高昂，盈利空间受限，即便是受欢迎的系列也难以负担高额制作预算。探讨设计师在追求高制作水准压力下，向独立重启或缩小规模的转变。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49169973' data-tags='Science,Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49169973' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(340) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.science.org/content/blog-post/preventing-misfolding-preventing-folding' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Preventing Misfolding by Preventing Folding</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>通过阻止折叠来防止错误折叠</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49169973' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 62</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49169973' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49169973'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post argues that preventing folding at early stages can reduce misfolding in proteins, offering strategies for design and intervention. The viewpoint has implications for protein engineering, neurodegenerative disease research, and understanding folding biology. It highlights the nontrivial link between folding pathways and misfolding outcomes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章提出通过在早期阶段阻止折叠来减少蛋白质错折叠，从而探索设计与干预策略。此思路对蛋白质工程、神经退行性疾病研究以及理解折叠生物学具有重要启示。强调了折叠途径与错折叠结果之间的密切联系。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49237873' data-tags='Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49237873' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(341) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.dpreview.com/news/panasonic-did-what-apple-sony-and-nikon-couldnt-overturn-a-red-raw-video-patent/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Japanese court overturns Red RAW video patent</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>日本法院推翻了 Red RAW 视频专利</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49237873' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 62</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49237873' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49237873'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Japanese court overturned the Red RAW video patent, potentially clearing obstacles for camera makers to implement RAW video without licensing barriers. The decision has implications for open formats and licensing costs in the imaging stack.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>日本法院推翻了 Red RAW 视频专利，可能消除相机厂商在实现 RAW 视频时的许可风险，降低合规成本。事件对开放格式的使用和未来专利授权生态具有重要影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.dpreview.com/wp-content/uploads/2020/12/8323309263-V_RAPTOR_XL_Selects_Camera_w_Family_Bckgrnd.jpeg' data-full='https://www.dpreview.com/wp-content/uploads/2020/12/8323309263-V_RAPTOR_XL_Selects_Camera_w_Family_Bckgrnd.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49145037' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(342) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sharedobject.blog/posts/vp8-combinatorial-logic/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Video2NAND – Abusing video codecs for great computational power</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Video2NAND：利用视频编解码器实现强大计算能力</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49145037' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 61</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49145037' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 11</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49145037'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Video2NAND demonstrates using video codecs as a form of programmable logic, repurposing codecs like VP8 to perform combinational logic. This challenges conventional silicon-centric thinking and could influence edge computing and hardware acceleration concepts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Video2NAND 展示了将视频编解码器作为可编程逻辑的一种实现方式，改写了像 VP8 这样的编解码器以执行组合逻辑。这挑战了以硅为中心的传统思维，并可能影响边缘计算与硬件加速的未来思考。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>勒索软件组织直接盯上 IT 经理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49234724' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 60</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49234724' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ransomware operators are increasingly targeting mid-level IT staff who have ammunition-like access to networks. The trend underscores the need for deeper internal security measures, not just executive protection.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>勒索软件攻击者正越来越多地针对具备实际系统访问权限的中层 IT 人员。趋势强调内部安全措施、培训和最小权限的必要性。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49183076' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 57</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49183076' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 28</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49183076'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This piece argues that AI progress is not primarily limited by intelligence growth but by data, tooling, and system design. It emphasizes focusing on data pipelines, evaluation, and mechanistic interpretability to unlock real efficiency gains.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为人工智能的进步并非主要受智力增速限制，而是受数据、工具链与系统设计等因素约束。核心在于加强数据管线、评估方法与可解释性，以实现在生产中的效率提升。</p>
