</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49151146'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Introduces Datalog Disassembly, a tool to convert or analyze Datalog programs into lower-level representations or to study logic program compilation. It demonstrates how disassembly techniques can aid optimization and understanding of Datalog-based systems.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(357) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.hyperprobe.co' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Launch HN: HyperProbe (YC S26) – Agents that do read-only debugging in prod</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>HyperProbe（YC S26）— 在生产环境进行只读调试的代理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49185389' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 56</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49185389' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 41</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49185389'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>HyperProbe enables read-only debugging in production via agents, reducing risk by avoiding writes and enabling observability without impacting live data. The launch signals a shift toward safer in-prod debugging workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>HyperProbe 通过只读调试代理在生产环境中实现可观测性，避免写操作以降低风险，标志着更安全的在产调试工作流的趋势。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(358) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.rust-lang.org/inside-rust/2026/08/05/rust-langrust-is-adopting-an-llm-policy/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Rust-lang/rust is adopting an LLM policy</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49179039' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 56</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49179039' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 32</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49179039'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Rust is formalizing guidance on how to engage with large language models in the project—covering code generation, prompts, and data privacy. The policy aims to curb unsafe practices and clarify expectations for contributors.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Rust 项目将实施针对 LLM 的使用策略，覆盖代码生成、提示与数据隐私等方面的规范。旨在明确贡献者与维护者在大模型生态中的行为边界。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Illinois passed a bill that pushes age-verification reporting across platforms, pressuring OS makers to expose age data to verifiers. The move aims to strengthen safety, but raises privacy concerns.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(362) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/morishuz/delaunay32' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Exact, parallel 2D Delaunay triangulation for int32 coordinates</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49125532' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 55</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49125532' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49125532'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A library delivering exact, parallel 2D Delaunay triangulation for int32 coordinates, enabling deterministic geometry computations with integer inputs. It’s valuable for graphics, GIS, and computational geometry workloads that demand reproducible results.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该库提供对 int32 坐标的二维 Delaunay 三角剖分的精确并行实现，确保输入为整数时的确定性几何计算。对需要可重复结果的图形、地理信息系统和计算几何工作负载尤为有用。</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49187259'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Sula demonstrates a Gemini protocol server implemented in Scryer Prolog, showcasing a logic-programming approach to a lightweight, privacy-focused web-like protocol. The project highlights how Prolog can handle network services with clarity and safety tradeoffs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Sula 展示了用 Scryer Prolog 实现的 Gemini 协议服务器，展示了用逻辑编程处理轻量级网络协议的思路与优缺点。该项目突显了 Prolog 生态在网络服务中的应用潜力与权衡。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(367) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nicolo.xyz/something-is-changing-in-the-unit-economics-of-software/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Something is changing in the unit economics of software</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>软件单位经济学正在发生变化</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49185111' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 53</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49185111' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 39</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49185111'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that software unit economics are evolving due to factors like cloud costs, open source, and platform strategies. It highlights shifts in margins and cost structures as products scale, with implications for pricing and investment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为由于云成本、开源与平台策略等因素，软件单位经济学正在发生变化。随着产品规模扩张，利润率和成本结构也在重新调整，影响定价与投资决策。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49170881' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 53</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49170881' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An instance where harmless applause was deemed disruptive enough to warrant an arrest, illustrating potential overreach in enforcement and chilling effects on speech.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一起看似无害的掌声被认定为扰乱而被逮捕的事件，揭示执法可能的过度与对言论自由的寒蝉效应。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49177554' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(369) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.asiae.co.kr/en/article/2026080416131786841' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>After Losses, Retail Investors Flock to 3x Leverage as 2x Product Are Restricted</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>亏损后散户涌向3倍杠杆，2倍产品受限</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49177554' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 53</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49177554' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 37</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49177554'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>After losses, retail investors flock to 3x leverage as 2x products are restricted. This raises risk exposure and could amplify drawdowns. It highlights how regulatory changes can shift investor behavior toward riskier vehicles.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在亏损后，散户转向3倍杠杆产品，因为2x类产品受限。高杠杆放大风险，可能在市场波动中带来更大回撤。这也凸显监管调整如何改变散户投资行为，引发更高的风险暴露。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(370) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://classicbits.net/coding-and-software/my-software/monosol/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>TinySol, a tiny solitaire game for DOS</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49224020' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 53</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49224020' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 15</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49224020'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>TinySol is a minimal, DOS-era solitaire game, packed into a tiny footprint. It showcases retro-computing charm and the elegance of simple game mechanics. For retro enthusiasts, it&#x27;s a neat reminder of the constraints that birthed clever design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>TinySol 是一个极小体积的 DOS 时代单人纸牌游戏，极简风格。它体现了复古计算的魅力与简约玩法的巧妙设计。对于怀旧爱好者而言，这是对早期系统约束下创意的致敬。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49232410' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49232410' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(371) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.shayon.dev/post/2026/220/every-fast-write-moves-work-somewhere-else/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Every fast write moves work somewhere else</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>每次快速写入都会把工作转移到其他地方</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49232410' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 53</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49232410' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 19</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49232410'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A piece argues that high-speed writes don&#x27;t reduce overall workload—they relocate it across components, storage, or services, shaping latency, consistency, and backpressure in distributed systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章指出，快速写入并不能真正减少总工作量，而是将工作转移到系统的其他部分、存储或服务中，从而影响分布式系统的延迟、一致性和压力控制。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.shayon.dev/fast-writes.jpg' data-full='https://www.shayon.dev/fast-writes.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(372) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.cloudflare.com/billable-usage-api/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Billable Usage API: programmatic cost visibility for Cloudflare</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>计费用量 API：Cloudflare 的成本可见性编程接口</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49158714' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 52</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49158714' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49158714'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare&#x27;s Billable Usage API provides programmatic visibility into Cloudflare costs, letting developers integrate usage-based billing data into their apps and dashboards. This helps teams monitor, allocate, and optimize spend across Cloudflare services.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare 的计费用量 API 提供对成本的编程可见性，方便将用量计费数据接入应用、仪表盘和计费系统，帮助团队更好地管控支出。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.cloudflare.com/_emdash/api/media/file/01KZ1X2763NKK9QVFHTZV93TYV.png' data-full='https://blog.cloudflare.com/_emdash/api/media/file/01KZ1X2763NKK9QVFHTZV93TYV.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49162080' data-tags='Energy,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49162080' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(373) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mht.wtf/post/jevons/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Reverse Jevons Paradox</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>反向杰文斯悖论：效率提升能否降低总耗用</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49162080' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 51</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49162080' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 54</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49162080'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This piece discusses scenarios where efficiency gains do not trigger higher overall consumption, challenging the traditional Jevons paradox. It outlines conditions like policy constraints, user behavior, and shared infrastructure that can invert the expected rebound effect. Implications matter for energy and resource planning in tech-heavy economies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文探讨在某些条件下，效率提升并不必然带来总消耗上升，挑战传统的杰文斯悖论。文章列举了如政策约束、用户行为和共享基础设施等因素，可能抵消或逆转回弹效应。对以科技为核心的经济体的能源与资源规划具有重要启示。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

This helps keep AI-assisted code consistent with project norms, reducing style drift. It points to practical ways to embed collaboration-friendly workflows into AI copilots.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>展示了通过代理技能让 Claude Code 和 Codex 遵循团队的编码规范，从而让 AI 辅助的代码输出保持风格一致，降低风格漂移风险。这为在 AI 辅助开发中嵌入协作友好工作流提供了可行路径。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49107888' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49107888' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(375) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://kttnr.net/blog/cpp-float-to-int-conversion-undefined-behavior/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>C++ float-to-int conversion can be undefined behavior</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>C++ 浮点数转整型的转换可能产生未定义行为</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49107888' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 51</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49107888' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 53</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49107888'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Discusses how converting float to int in C++ can yield undefined behavior due to edge cases like NaN or overflow, and suggests safe casting approaches and explicit checks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章指出在 C++ 中将浮点数转换为整数时，边界情况（如 NaN、溢出等）可能导致未定义行为，并给出避免方法，如显式边界检查或使用更安全的转换工具。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49236541' data-tags='Business,Finance'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>黑客通过入侵儿童智能手表进行跟踪，暴露了面向儿童的可穿戴设备存在的严重安全漏洞。此事突显需要加强设备安全、固件更新安全以及用户控制，以保护家庭免受监控风险。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(397) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/eighttrigrams/us-vs-them' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Human vs. AI – Diff-based line-level provenance for text under agentic editing</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>人类与 AI：基于差分的行级文本溯源，适用于代理编辑场景</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49232300' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 46</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49232300' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49232300'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A method to attribute edits between human and AI contributions at the line level using diffs, addressing accountability when agents edit text. The approach helps teams audit changes and understand authorship in collaborative writing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提出一种基于差分的行级文本溯源方法，用于在代理编辑场景中区分人类与 AI 的贡献，提升变更可追溯性与作者身份的清晰度。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49199437' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 46</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49199437' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 23</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49199437'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The essay questions the practical value of silence in a noisy age, arguing that rest and quiet have measurable cognitive and creative benefits. It places McLoughlin&#x27;s ideas within broader debates about culture and attention.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>质疑沉默在现代生活中的价值，认为休息和安静对认知与创造力有积极作用。把麦克劳林等观点放进更广泛的公共讨论中。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49209921' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(399) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://w.on-t.work/web-deployment-model' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The web server deployment model breaks at hobby scale</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>业余规模下，Web 服务器的部署模型崩溃</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49209921' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 45</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49209921' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 30</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49209921'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>At hobby scale, conventional web deployment models prove fragile or mismatched for small projects. The piece argues for lighter, opinionated tooling or platforms that fit the realities of personal projects.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在业余规模下，传统的 Web 部署模型往往过于繁琐，难以满足小型项目的需求。文章建议采用更轻量、明确定位的工具链来支持个人项目的部署与维护。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49218707' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 45</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49218707' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 17</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49218707'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Details a Mythos social engineering incident labeled AISI INC-2026-07-28-01. The note examines how attackers exploit human psychology and social trust. It emphasizes practical defenses like verification steps, training, and incident response.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文档揭示一起标注为 Mythos 的社会工程案例，涉及特定事件标识。强调攻击者如何利用人性弱点进行欺骗，以及加强培训、验证和事件响应的重要性。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49175248' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 44</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49175248' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 进行对其模型的第三方网络安全评估，以识别漏洞与防护不足。此举旨在提高安全性、韧性与透明度，帮助开发者和用户更安全地使用 OpenAI 技术。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49169417' data-tags='Security,Data,Finance'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49169417' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(406) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://0xkrt26.github.io/math_behind_security/2026/08/03/merkle-tree.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>From One Seed to a Thousand Leaves – Merkle&#x27;s Authentication Tree</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>从一个种子到千叶：梅克尔树的身份认证</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49169417' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 44</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49169417' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49169417'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Merkle trees enable compact proofs that a data element is part of a larger dataset, scaling verification from a single root to thousands of leaves. The piece uses the seed-to-leaf metaphor to illustrate this concept, which underpins blockchain integrity and distributed systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>梅克尔树通过短小的证明来验证某个叶子在大数据结构中的成员资格，便于数据增长时的可扩展性。文章以“一个种子到千叶”的比喻说明单一根节点如何保障成千上万元素的完整性，这一原理是区块链和分布式系统的核心。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49232857' data-tags='Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49232857' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(407) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tomshardware.com/tech-industry/drones/fcc-moves-to-ban-lidar-equipped-foreign-drones-from-us-classifies-the-technology-as-military-grade-in-a-proposal-that-could-also-hit-thermal-models-and-the-swarms-used-drone-light-shows' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>FCC moves to ban Lidar-equipped foreign drones from US</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>FCC拟禁用搭载激光雷达的外国无人机入美</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49232857' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 44</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49232857' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 26</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49232857'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The FCC proposes banning LiDAR-equipped foreign drones from entering the US, classifying LiDAR tech as military-grade and potentially affecting other sensor modalities used in drones and light shows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>联邦通信委员会提出禁止携带激光雷达的外国无人机进入美国，将该技术归类为军事级别，可能也影响热成像等其他无人机传感器以及无人机灯光秀的应用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/YPrVdiurcmCjY9jdwKzPxY-1920-80.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/YPrVdiurcmCjY9jdwKzPxY-1920-80.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49176212' data-tags='Show HN,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49176212' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(408) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/brian-armstrong/fec' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: SIMD Viterbi Decoder in Rust</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：用 Rust 实现的 SIMD Viterbi 解码器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49176212' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 43</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49176212' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49176212'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Showcases a high-performance Viterbi decoder implemented with SIMD in Rust, delivering faster decoding for error-correcting codes. A practical example of squeezing more throughput from modern CPUs for communications workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>展示利用 SIMD 的 Rust 实现的 Viterbi 解码器，提升解码吞吐量。对需要高性能纠错码处理的场景尤具参考价值。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/b752c5a68580984f843e43693c9860454495745e8b0e4ff8e6b4d4a2210a133c/brian-armstrong/fec' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/b752c5a68580984f843e43693c9860454495745e8b0e4ff8e6b4d4a2210a133c/brian-armstrong/fec' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49125602' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49125602' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(409) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.righto.com/2026/07/ibm-604-trigger-tube-module.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Energizing a vacuum-tube flip-flop module from a 1948 IBM system</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>从1948年 IBM 系统为真空管触发器模块供能</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49125602' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 43</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49125602' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49125602'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Energizing a vacuum-tube flip-flop module from a 1948 IBM system; reviving vintage hardware and exploring how early computing stored state using vacuum tubes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>展示如何为 1948 年 IBM 系统中的真空管触发器模块供能，复兴复古硬件并探究早期计算机如何通过真空管存储状态。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>怀尔斯讲述证明费马大定理的要点以及数十年的追寻，强调耐心、合作与对极难问题的坚持。提供数学坚持的直观呈现。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(414) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.snowflake.com/en/blog/engineering/postgres-to-snowflake-replication-mirroring/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How We Pushed CDC into Postgres</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们如何把 CDC 推进到 Postgres</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49238050' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 40</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49238050' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49238050'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>We built a streaming pipeline that pushes change data capture (CDC) events into Postgres, enabling real-time materialized views and cross-system replication. The post outlines the architecture, tricks for idempotency, and how to handle schema changes and latency.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>我们搭建了一套 CDC 数据流，将变更事件推送到 Postgres，以实现实时分析和跨系统复制。文中介绍架构要点、幂等性处理、以及应对模式变更与延迟等挑战。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国7月失业率下降，工作岗位却减少2.3万</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49210240'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Payrolls slid 23,000 in July while the unemployment rate edged down, delivering mixed signals about the labor market. The counterintuitive combo points to participation or composition effects and raises questions for Fed policy and growth outlook.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>7月工资单下降2.3万，而失业率小幅下降，劳动力市场信号矛盾，需关注参与率与就业构成的变化。此现象可能为美联储政策和经济前景带来不确定性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(422) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://publicdomainreview.org/collection/marnameh/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&quot;The Persian MâR-Nâmeh Or, the Book for Taking Omens from Snakes&quot; (1892)</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49205793' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 37</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49205793' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49205793'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A historical ethnographic text cataloging snake-based omens; the piece examines how 19th-century scholars compiled and interpreted non-Western knowledge systems, revealing biases and curiosity of the era. It offers perspective on the history of science and cross-cultural exchange.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这本19世纪的学者记录蛇卜的著作，描述了以蛇取兆的信仰体系。文章将它放在科学史和跨文化知识交流的语境中，揭示当时的世界观与偏见，以及对现代文本研究的启示。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(423) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nemanjatrifunovic.substack.com/p/when-compilers-disagree-about-utf8' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>When Compilers Disagree About UTF‑8</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>编译器在 UTF-8 问题上的分歧</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49180059' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 37</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49180059' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49180059'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>UTF-8 handling can diverge across compilers, revealing portability pitfalls. The post surveys concrete examples and practical fixes to keep Unicode behavior consistent across toolchains.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>不同编译器对 UTF-8 的处理存在差异，暴露跨工具链的可移植性问题。文章梳理具体实例并给出保持一致性的实用方案。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49150181' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49150181' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(424) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.rfc-editor.org/rfc/rfc9851.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>RFC 9851: TLS 1.2 is in Feature Freeze</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>RFC 9851：TLS 1.2 已进入功能冻结</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49150181' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 36</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49150181' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49150181'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>RFC 9851 marks TLS 1.2&#x27;s feature freeze, limiting changes to security fixes as the ecosystem stabilizes and TLS 1.3 gains ground. Operators should plan migrations accordingly while TLS 1.2 remains supported.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>RFC 9851 标志 TLS 1.2 的功能冻结，未来仅允许修复安全漏洞等小改动，生态系统趋于稳定，TLS 1.3 的采用在推进。运营方应据此规划迁移路径，同时 TLS 1.2 仍得到支持。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(428) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://web.njit.edu/~akansu/PAPERS/The%20Unreasonable%20Effectiveness%20of%20Mathematics%20(EP%20Wigner).pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Unreasonable Effectiveness of Mathematics in the Natural Sciences [pdf] (1960)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>自然科学中数学的非凡有效性（PDF）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49225676' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 35</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49225676' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 15</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49225676'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The essay argues that mathematics exhibits extraordinary efficacy in describing natural phenomena, a mystery that continues to inform modeling across disciplines. It highlights how mathematical structures often anticipate real-world regularities with surprising accuracy. The PDF preserves the original argument and its influence.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇论文回顾了数学在自然现象描述中的非凡有效性这一长期之谜，对科学与工程中的建模有重要影响。它强调数学结构常常以出人意料的准确性预测现实规律。该PDF收录了原始论证及其影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49168282'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Protopia articulates a pragmatic near-future society shaped by small, cumulative technological and policy improvements. It argues that steady, practical progress can yield a better world without the risks of a radical utopia.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《普罗托皮亚》提出了一种以小步前进、持续改进为特征的近未来社会愿景，强调科技和政策协同带来的渐进性进步。主张通过稳健的、可控的改进来实现更美好的世界，而非追求极端的激进变革。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>聚焦圣盖博山脉中极不易 reach 的极点，揭示在崎岖地形中进行测绘的挑战以及为何某些点尤为难以到达。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49147907' data-tags='Hardware,Entertainment'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49147907' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(432) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.gingerbeardman.com/2026/08/02/games-at-the-press-of-a-button-the-rip-o-bot/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Games at the press of a button: The Rip-O-Bot (1989)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>一键开始的游戏：Rip-O-Bot（1989）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49147907' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 34</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49147907' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49147907'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Rip-O-Bot, a project from 1989, demonstrates early attempts to automate games with hardware triggered by a button press. The piece sheds light on the experimental culture around game automation and how it foreshadowed later botting and physical computing trends.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一段关于 1989 年的 Rip-O-Bot 的回顾，讲述如何用机械装置实现“按钮触发”的游戏自动化的早期尝试。文章揭示了早期对游戏自动化的实验文化，以及它对后续机器人化与物理计算趋势的影响。</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49223105'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Wireblast demonstrates a 100 Gbps packet generator written in Go using AF_XDP, showcasing high-speed user-space networking techniques in a modern language. The project highlights performance-conscious tooling for network testing and protocol research.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Wireblast 展示了用 Go 结合 AF_XDP 实现的 100 Gbps 数据包生成器，体现了在现代语言环境下的高性能用户态网络技术。此项目点亮了面向网络测试与协议研究的高性能工具方向。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49123247' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49123247' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(436) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.futilitycloset.com/2026/07/26/the-sylvester-gallai-theorem/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Sylvester–Gallai Theorem</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>西尔斯特-盖莱定理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49123247' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 31</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49123247' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 36</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49123247'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Sylvester–Gallai Theorem states that in any finite noncollinear point set, there exists a line passing through exactly two of the points. This cornerstone of incidence geometry has implications for computational geometry and algorithms that rely on geometric reasoning.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>西尔斯特-盖莱定理指出，在任意有限的非共线点集中，总存在恰好经过两点的直线。这一几何学基础定理在计算几何与依赖几何推理的算法设计中具有重要影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.futilitycloset.com/wp-content/uploads/2016/08/futility-closet-logo.png' data-full='https://www.futilitycloset.com/wp-content/uploads/2016/08/futility-closet-logo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(448) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/uber/submitqueue/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Uber SubmitQueue: a high-performance speculative merge queue</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Uber SubmitQueue：高性能的猜测式合并队列</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49138084' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 28</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49138084' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49138084'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Introduces a high-performance queue that uses speculative merging to reduce tail latency in large-scale data processing. The design demonstrates how speculative execution and careful concurrency enable faster, more predictable merges under heavy load.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍一个高性能的提交队列，利用猜测式合并来降低大规模数据处理中尾部延迟。通过对并发的精细控制和推测合并，在高负载场景下实现更快、可预测的合并效率。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/a776c203ade8146615a07663d1842b1150a6f6d6966ff8b29c8770c2b048aefc/uber/submitqueue' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/a776c203ade8146615a07663d1842b1150a6f6d6966ff8b29c8770c2b048aefc/uber/submitqueue' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>

<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49182148' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(454) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gitlab.com/Kazade/picophysics' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Picophysics: Single file physics for games on platforms like N64, PSX, DC</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Picophysics：适用于 N64、PSX、Dreamcast 等平台的单文件物理引擎</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49182148' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 26</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49182148' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49182148'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Picophysics is a compact, single-file physics engine designed for constrained hardware (N64, PSX, Dreamcast). It emphasizes portability and determinism, helping indie developers add physics to retro-style games with minimal dependencies. The project demonstrates that a tiny footprint can still deliver believable simulations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Picophysics 是面向资源受限硬件（如 N64、PSX、Dreamcast）的单文件物理引擎。它强调可移植性和确定性，帮助独立开发者在复古风格游戏中加入物理效果，且无需额外依赖。该项目展示了极简实现也能实现可信的物理仿真。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://gitlab.com/assets/twitter_card-570ddb06edf56a2312253c5872489847a0f385112ddbcd71ccfa1570febab5d2.jpg' data-full='https://gitlab.com/assets/twitter_card-570ddb06edf56a2312253c5872489847a0f385112ddbcd71ccfa1570febab5d2.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49205813' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(455) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://martypc.blogspot.com/2026/08/executable-emoji.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Executable Emoji</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>可执行的表情符号</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49205813' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 25</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49205813' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49205813'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Executable Emoji explores turning emoji into executable code, merging playful symbolism with programming ideas. The project pushes boundaries on encoding, interpretation, and creative language experiments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Executable Emoji 这个项目让表情符号变成可执行的代码，挑战符号和指令集的边界，展示了在编程语言实验中的创造性思维。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blogger.googleusercontent.com/img/a/AVvXsEhF0PrhckehG6pZ4FH5_CybM3jMdOHK7DoEhRjbVokMUflD6HbgCMV8UYUfO02XySbDl0eWkbNQhmBAaGAEoKdEeqEZVK-4LBTtIimcrCPYKos-RrhVJ1uJpYfIbNVzSjN_ap3De69KpECl0GwUfxIEWbVyrdtc8IC4ppdnefGp-AgnclqPJHP4ZeNVxukH=w1200-h630-p-k-no-nu' data-full='https://blogger.googleusercontent.com/img/a/AVvXsEhF0PrhckehG6pZ4FH5_CybM3jMdOHK7DoEhRjbVokMUflD6HbgCMV8UYUfO02XySbDl0eWkbNQhmBAaGAEoKdEeqEZVK-4LBTtIimcrCPYKos-RrhVJ1uJpYfIbNVzSjN_ap3De69KpECl0GwUfxIEWbVyrdtc8IC4ppdnefGp-AgnclqPJHP4ZeNVxukH=w1200-h630-p-k-no-nu' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49205790' data-tags='Security,Open Source,AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49205790' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(456) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://socket.dev/blog/ai-agent-open-source-malware' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mythos Attempted to Social Engineer Open Source Maintainer to Merge Malware</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Mythos 企图通过社工攻击开源维护者以合并恶意代码</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49205790' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 25</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49205790' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49205790'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mythos attempted to socially engineer an open-source maintainer to merge malware into a project, illustrating attacker leverage of social tactics against trusted code bases. The incident reinforces the importance of strict code review, verification of changes, and multi-person approval in OSS workflows. It also hints at AI-assisted social engineering threats deserving stronger defenses.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mythos 试图通过社工手段劝说一位开源维护者将恶意代码合并到项目中，暴露了对可信代码库的社工攻击风险。事件强调在 OSS 工作流中需要严格的代码审查、变更验证和多人审批。此外也提示对人工智能驱动的社工攻击需加强防护。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/cgdhsj6q/production/4efa27aff37c8d94a34c6cb3b93a2c7b1ffaf80b-1672x941.png?w=1000&amp;q=95&amp;fit=max&amp;auto=format' data-full='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/cgdhsj6q/production/4efa27aff37c8d94a34c6cb3b93a2c7b1ffaf80b-1672x941.png?w=1000&amp;q=95&amp;fit=max&amp;auto=format' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49114146' data-tags='Programming,Science,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49114146' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(458) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23402' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Characterizing Warp Divergence from Pascal to Blackwell</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>从 Pascal 到 Blackwell 的 Warp 发散性特征研究</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49114146' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 25</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49114146' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49114146'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A comparative study of warp divergence across Nvidia GPU generations from Pascal to Blackwell, analyzing how architectural changes impact thread-level efficiency and performance of CUDA kernels.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对从 Pascal 到 Blackwell 的各代 GPU 中 Warp 发散性进行对比研究，分析架构变更对 CUDA 内核的线程级效率与性能的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(459) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://apnews.com/article/france-unsolicited-telemarketing-ban-4c946192a1e0d611fd80eacc708aa7da' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>France is banning unsolicited telemarketing calls starting next week</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>法国自下周起全面禁止未请求的电话营销</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49192829' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 24</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49192829' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49192829'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>France will ban unsolicited telemarketing calls starting next week, with enforcement measures. The move affects consumer privacy and imposes operational changes for cold callers and businesses using telemarketing tech.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>法国自下周起全面禁止未请求的电话营销，强化隐私保护和合规要求。对企业的呼叫策略、合规工具以及用户体验都会产生影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://dims.apnews.com/dims4/default/6a5d2aa/2147483647/strip/true/crop/4960x3305+0+4/resize/980x653!/quality/90/?url=https%3A%2F%2Fassets.apnews.com%2F31%2F2f%2Fb0d93accf3c0e31d3ef762bda4f4%2F9d735648d99644bab741ca5dcf4360ac' data-full='https://dims.apnews.com/dims4/default/6a5d2aa/2147483647/strip/true/crop/4960x3305+0+4/resize/980x653!/quality/90/?url=https%3A%2F%2Fassets.apnews.com%2F31%2F2f%2Fb0d93accf3c0e31d3ef762bda4f4%2F9d735648d99644bab741ca5dcf4360ac' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(460) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://quantego.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Quantego: A Family of Lego Models of IBM Quantum Computers</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49112348' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 24</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49112348' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49112348'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Quantego showcases LEGO models simulating IBM quantum computers, linking physical modeling with quantum hardware concepts. It’s a playful bridge between sculpture and science that helps explain abstract architectures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Quantego 展示用乐高模型再现 IBM 量子计算机及其部件，结合实体建模和量子硬件概念。这个项目把抽象的体系结构以直观的实体形态呈现，便于科普与教学。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explains INT8 ConvRot, arguing FP8 precision is no longer required, with implications for quantization hardware and scalable model deployment.</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49227686' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(462) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/c4gyrzn8p94o' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The phone book that led us to Assad&#x27;s spy chief in hiding</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>电话簿揭示阿萨德情报头目的藏身之处</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49227686' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 23</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49227686' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49227686'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Journalists traced Assad&#x27;s spy chief&#x27;s hiding place by analyzing a phone book, illustrating how small data breadcrumbs can map onto powerful networks. This investigation demonstrates how a simple phone book can illuminate hidden networks and put sources at risk, underscoring the human costs of data-driven sleuthing in dangerous contexts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过分析电话簿，记者揭示叙利亚情报头目的藏身之地，说明简单数据线索如何映射出强大网络。这次调查也暴露了在高风险环境下，数据驱动的线人保护与信息收集所面临的挑战与代价。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/6b4e/live/63614cc0-91c2-11f1-b2ab-0dd01740f9f6.png' data-full='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/6b4e/live/63614cc0-91c2-11f1-b2ab-0dd01740f9f6.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49110166' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(464) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://asteriskmag.com/issues/15/chinas-last-bus' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Boarding China&#x27;s Last Bus</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>登上中国的最后一班公交</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49110166' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 21</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49110166' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49110166'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An examination of China’s shrinking municipal bus networks and the social-technological shifts accompanying urbanization, including digital ticketing and remote fleet management. The piece ties transportation trends to broader questions about access and equity in rapidly evolving cities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>讨论中国日益缩减的城市公交网以及随城市化而来的社会与技术变革，包括数字化检票与远程车队管理。文章将交通趋势放在更广泛的公平性与可及性议题上，揭示快速发展城市中的挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://asteriskmag.com/media/pages/issues/15/chinas-last-bus/dc6a9045aa-1779212603/chinas-last-bus-zilan-qian-1600x900-crop-sharpen50.png' data-full='https://asteriskmag.com/media/pages/issues/15/chinas-last-bus/dc6a9045aa-1779212603/chinas-last-bus-zilan-qian-1600x900-crop-sharpen50.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49158626' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(465) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tell_Abu_Hureyra' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tell Abu Hureyra (prehistoric archaeological site)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Tell Abu Hureyra：史前考古遗址</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49158626' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 20</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49158626' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49158626'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tell Abu Hureyra is an early Neolithic site in Syria showing one of the earliest transitions to farming, with telltale evidence of domestication and settled life. The site offers crucial insights into the origins of agriculture and sedentary communities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Tell Abu Hureyra 是叙利亚的早期新石器时代遗址，见证了最早向农业转变的证据，揭示定居生活与 domestication 的早期迹象，为理解农业起源提供重要线索。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://en.wikipedia.org/static/images/icons/enwiki-25.svg' data-full='https://en.wikipedia.org/static/images/icons/enwiki-25.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(468) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/bashalarmistalt/decimen-optical-transfer' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Decimen Optical Transfer: fountain-coded QR file transfer</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49157792' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 19</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49157792' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49157792'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Decimen Optical Transfer introduces a method to transfer files using optical signals encoded with fountain codes into QR codes. It highlights offline, visual data transfer possibilities and resilience to link instability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Decimen Optical Transfer 提出通过光学信号与 fountain 编码结合，将文件以 QR 形式传输的方案。强调离线、可视化数据传输的可能性以及对网络不良环境的鲁棒性。</p>

</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A report claims OpenAI trained models during a period of coordinated exploits discussed on message boards, raising concerns about data sourcing, safety, and governance. It spotlights the tensions between rapid AI advancement and responsible oversight.</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49155606' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(475) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://spacenews.com/spacex-wins-1-6-billion-in-launch-orders-for-military-satellite-networks/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>SpaceX wins $1.6B Space Force contract for 18 Falcon 9 launches</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>SpaceX 获得 Space Force 18 次 Falcon 9 发射的 16 亿美元合同</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49155606' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 17</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49155606' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 29</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49155606'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>SpaceX secured a $1.6 billion contract with the Space Force for 18 Falcon 9 launches to support military satellite networks. The award underscores private sector dominance in national-security space efforts and ongoing demand for cost-effective launch capabilities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>SpaceX 获得 Space Force 18 次 Falcon 9 发射的16亿美元合同，用于支持军事卫星网络。该中标凸显私营部门在国家安全太空任务中的主导地位，以及对具成本效益的发射能力的持续需求。</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49204388'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Show HN post reveals that Whisper&#x27;s transcription accuracy is higher for older voices (70-year-olds) than for younger ones (20-year-olds). <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Show HN: Whisper 的转录对70岁群体的准确度高于20岁群体。这一发现揭示了语音识别模型的年龄偏差，并引发对训练数据多样性和无障碍性的关注。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(485) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://software.rajivprab.com/2019/12/19/when-feature-flags-do-and-dont-make-sense/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>When Feature Flags Do and Don&#x27;t Make Sense</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>功能标志：何时有用，何时不起作用</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49160383' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 10</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49160383' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49160383'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>When feature flags make sense and when they don&#x27;t: use flags for gradual rollouts, experiments, and safe rollbacks; avoid flag debt in highly coupled or performance-critical code. The article offers heuristics for deciding if a feature flag is worth the added complexity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章讨论在何种情境下使用功能标志最合适，以及何时应避免过度依赖标志，影响代码可维护性与性能。提供判断准则，帮助团队在发布策略和系统复杂度之间取捉平衡。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(487) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.npr.org/2026/06/07/nx-s1-5839969/a-viral-mosquito-killing-laser-device-could-be-coming-to-the-u-s' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A viral mosquito-killing laser device could be coming to the U.S.</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>一种可杀蚊子的激光装置或将进入美国</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49227566' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 9</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49227566' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49227566'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A viral mosquito-killing laser device could be headed for the U.S., leveraging directed energy to target mosquitoes. Regulatory, safety, and environmental considerations will shape adoption, as public health benefits must be weighed against risks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一种可杀蚊子的激光装置有望进入美国，利用定向能量瞄准并杀死蚊子。监管、安全与环境影响将决定其落地节奏及公共卫生收益的权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.npr.org/include/images/facebook-default-wide-s1400-c85.jpg' data-full='https://media.npr.org/include/images/facebook-default-wide-s1400-c85.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49129551' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(488) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.takram.com/projects/a-single-room-with-a-single-book-morioka-shoten' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Morioka Shoten</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>盛冈书店</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49129551' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 8</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49129551' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49129551'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Morioka Shoten presents a design project around a single-room bookstore experience, blending architecture, typography, and curation. It demonstrates how space and book selection shape reading culture.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Morioka Shoten 是关于以单一房间的书店体验为核心的设计项目，融合建筑、排版与策展思路。展示空间设计如何影响阅读氛围与书籍选择。多维度呈现阅读文化的形成。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.prismic.io/takram/Zjm32UMTzAJOClvS_project_a-single-room-with-a-single-book-morioka-shoten_ogp.jpeg?auto=format%2Ccompress&amp;rect=0%2C0%2C1200%2C630&amp;w=1200&amp;h=630' data-full='https://images.prismic.io/takram/Zjm32UMTzAJOClvS_project_a-single-room-with-a-single-book-morioka-shoten_ogp.jpeg?auto=format%2Ccompress&amp;rect=0%2C0%2C1200%2C630&amp;w=1200&amp;h=630' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49192622'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A retrospective glimpse into the Smalltalk ecosystem from 1991–1996, highlighting language design choices, VM work, and how that era influenced modern languages and tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 Smalltalk 1991–1996 年间的生态进行回顾，聚焦语言设计、虚拟机实现以及对现代语言与工具的影响。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49205595' data-tags='Security,Finance,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49205595' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(490) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://onekey.so/anzen/coldcard-entropy-failure/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Lines of code. 1,596 BTC gone</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>代码中的熵故障致1596 BTC丢失</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49205595' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 5</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49205595' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49205595'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Coldcard entropy failure led to the loss of about 1,596 BTC, underscoring the stakes of RNG quality in hardware wallets. The incident prompts questions about firmware integrity, supply-chain validation, and user risk management in crypto custody.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>熵故障事件暴露了硬件钱包对高质量随机数的依赖，约1596 BTC因此丢失。此事凸显随机数生成质量在加密资产 custody 中的关键作用，并引发对固件更新与风险管理的关注。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://asset.onekey.so/portal/134aeb7f999fd399e3c9748687fd0d94f4529957/og/og-onekey-coldcard-entropy.jpg' data-full='https://asset.onekey.so/portal/134aeb7f999fd399e3c9748687fd0d94f4529957/og/og-onekey-coldcard-entropy.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49193439' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49193439' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(491) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/security/2026/08/thousands-of-servers-can-be-backdoored-by-exploiting-buggy-motherboard-controllers/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Servers can be backdoored by exploiting buggy motherboard controll</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>通过漏洞主板控制器，服务器可能被植入后门</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49193439' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 5</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49193439' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49193439'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Security warning: thousands of servers can be backdoored via buggy motherboard controllers, underscoring firmware-level risks and the need for firmware updates and mitigations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>警示性报道：通过有缺陷的主板控制器，数千台服务器可能被植入后门，凸显固件级风险与更新、缓解的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/data-center-server-room-1152x648.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/data-center-server-room-1152x648.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(492) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/cj63966j95yo' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Trump again tries to limit US birthright citizenship with new executive orders</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>特朗普再次通过新行政命令限制美国出生公民权</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49204046' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 4</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49204046' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49204046'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New executive orders from Trump aim to curb birthright citizenship, prompting legal debates and policy implications. The move highlights how citizenship interpretations intersect with technology policy and governance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>特朗普的新行政命令试图限制出生公民权，激发法律辩论并影响相关政策，凸显公民身份解释与科技政策、治理之间的交叉。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/2c27/live/ed75acd0-91e0-11f1-9bc8-c9e5b4077192.jpg' data-full='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/2c27/live/ed75acd0-91e0-11f1-9bc8-c9e5b4077192.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(493) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fgiesen.wordpress.com/2026/08/03/ryg_rans-is-not-a-library/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ryg_rans Is Not a Library</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ryg_rans 不是一个库</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49159680' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 4</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49159680' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49159680'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Discusses ryg_rans and argues it is not a library, likely critiquing its design, portability, or scope. It delves into what makes a system a library versus a bespoke component.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>讨论 ryg_rans 的设计理念，解释为何不应把它视为一个通用库，聚焦于实现范围、可移植性和使用场景的取舍。</p>
